* 启动与基础设施
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: startup
:END:

Emacs 冷启动的第一阶段:GC / 路径常量 / 外部命令声明 / Frame 生命周期 / 键位辅助 / 包管理 / 基础行为。

本域代码必须最先执行——后续功能依赖这里定义的路径、进程、frame 与键位原语。

** GC 与启动优化
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: startup-gc
:END:

GC 启动期优化(启动完成后由 gcmh 重置)

+ 与 early-init.el 的同设置重复,但保留作为 main.el 自身的防御性优化——若有人绕过 early-init.el 直接 batch-load main.el(如 =configctl load= ),GC threshold 仍需在 main.el 入口推高。
+ 临时调高 GC 阈值以加速启动,启动完成后由 =gcmh-mode= 接管。

+ =user-emacs-directory= 由当前单 profile 直接指向本目录
+ =gc-cons-threshold= 启动期推到 =most-positive-fixnum= ,启动完成后由 =gcmh-mode= 重置为合理值(见 *基础行为设置* )
+ =gc-cons-percentage= 启动期推到 0.6(减少启动期 GC),启动完成后显式恢复到 0.1——GCMH 只动态调整 =gc-cons-threshold= ,不管 =gc-cons-percentage= ,故必须显式恢复,否则 GCMH 接管后 percentage 仍停留在 0.6。
+ 保留 Emacs 31 默认的 =file-name-handler-alist= 与 bidi 行为。daemon 冷启动不是目标，不能用未测量的优化破坏 TRAMP、压缩文件或 RTL 文本。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; main.el --- custom-config 主配置(由 emacs.org tangle 生成) -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-

;; SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 BrokenShine <xchai404@gmail.com>
;; SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT

(setq gc-cons-threshold most-positive-fixnum
      gc-cons-percentage 0.6)

(add-hook 'emacs-startup-hook
          (lambda ()
            (setq gc-cons-percentage 0.1)))

(message "[custom-config] 基础设施加载完成")
#+end_src

** 路径常量与外部命令声明
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: bootstrap
:END:

集中定义所有路径常量(org 目录、Guix profile)与外部命令名。

+ 只保存命令名，不缓存 Guix store 绝对路径；长驻 daemon 跨 generation 后仍由当前 =exec-path= 解析新命令。
+ =early-init.el= 把 =GUIX_PROFILE/bin= 和 =~/.local/bin= 加入 =exec-path= 。
+ 后续所有功能域都引用这里的 =custom:org-directory= 、 =custom:executable-*=

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref bootstrap/paths :tangle no
(defconst custom:emacs-dir user-emacs-directory
  "当前 Emacs profile 的根目录。")

(defconst custom:org-directory
  (expand-file-name "~/Documents/Org/")
  "Org 文件根目录。")

(defconst custom:org-roam-directory
  (expand-file-name "roam" custom:org-directory)
  "Org-roam 笔记目录。")

(defconst custom:org-inbox-file
  (expand-file-name "inbox.org" custom:org-directory)
  "Org 收件箱。")

(defconst custom:org-knowledge-directory
  (expand-file-name "experiences" custom:org-directory)
  "知识库经验卡片目录（人类域）。")

(defconst custom:agenote-directory
  (expand-file-name "agenote" custom:org-directory)
  "agenote 子域目录（agent 写入的卡片 + index.json）。")

(defconst custom:guix-profile
  (or (getenv "GUIX_PROFILE")
      (expand-file-name "~/.guix-profile"))
  "Guix profile 路径。")

(defconst custom:in-guix-environment-p
  (or (getenv "GUIX_ENVIRONMENT")
      (file-exists-p custom:guix-profile))
  "是否运行在 Guix 环境中。")
#+end_src

外部命令只保存命令名。Guix 保证包存在，实际进程启动时由当前 PATH 解析。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref bootstrap/executables :tangle no
(defconst custom:executable-aspell   "aspell")
(defconst custom:executable-direnv   "direnv")
(defconst custom:executable-fish     "fish")
(defconst custom:executable-git      "git")
(defconst custom:executable-godot    "godot")
(defconst custom:executable-hunspell "hunspell")
#+end_src

bootstrap 组装：路径常量 + 外部命令声明

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<bootstrap/paths>>

** 共享进程辅助
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: process-helper
:END:

统一的同步进程入口与 agenote CLI 适配层。

+ =custom/agenote-call= :agenote CLI 唯一入口,domain 必填,返回 plist
+ =custom/agenote-call-async= :长任务异步执行(reindex/lint/deduplicate/commit/viz)

agenote CLI 支持 =--domain human|agenote=,默认 agenote(与 MCP server 对齐)。

Emacs 端必须为每次调用显式选择 domain —— 不依赖默认值,避免人类域操作静默打到 agent 子域。

**不缓存 Guix store 绝对路径* *:
+ daemon 长驻跨 Guix profile 更新时,缓存的 =/gnu/store/.../bin/agenote= 会指向旧 generation。
+ =custom/agenote-call= 内部按需 =(executable-find "agenote")=,让每次调用都解析当前 PATH。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref helpers/agenote-process :tangle no
(defconst custom:agenote-valid-domains '(human agenote)
  "agenote CLI 支持的 domain 取值。与 ~agenote --help~ 对齐。")

(defun custom/agenote--resolve-executable ()
  "按需解析 agenote 命令路径。返回可执行路径字符串或 nil。
不缓存绝对 store 路径:daemon 跨 Guix generation 后能找到新 profile 的 agenote。
实际调用始终走本函数。"
  (executable-find "agenote"))

(defun custom/agenote--ensure-domain (domain)
  "校验 DOMAIN,返回小写 symbol。无效值 fail-fast。"
  (let ((d (if (stringp domain) (intern (downcase domain)) domain)))
    (unless (memq d custom:agenote-valid-domains)
      (error "custom/agenote-call: domain must be %s, got %S"
             (mapconcat #'symbol-name custom:agenote-valid-domains "|")
             domain))
    d))

(cl-defun custom/agenote-call (domain command &rest args)
  "同步调用 agenote CLI,DOMAIN 必填(human 或 agenote),COMMAND 子命令字符串,
ARGS 附加参数。

返回 plist:`(:status :stdout :stderr :command :domain)'。
- :status  进程退出码(-1 表示无法启动)
- :stdout  stdout 字符串(trimmed)
- :stderr  stderr 字符串(trimmed)
- :command  完整 argv 列表(便于诊断)
- :domain  实际使用的 domain symbol

用法示例:
  (custom/agenote-call 'human \"list\" \"--all\")
  (custom/agenote-call 'agenote \"search\" \"emacs\" \"--limit\" \"10\")
  (plist-get (custom/agenote-call 'human \"stats\") :stdout)"
  (let* ((dom (custom/agenote--ensure-domain domain))
         (exe (custom/agenote--resolve-executable))
         (argv (delq nil (append (list exe "--domain" (symbol-name dom) command) args))))
    (if (not exe)
        (list :status -1 :stdout "" :stderr "agenote executable not found"
              :command argv :domain dom)
      (let ((stdout-buffer (generate-new-buffer " *agenote-stdout*"))
            (stderr-file (make-temp-file "agenote-stderr"))
            status)
        (unwind-protect
            (progn
              ;; call-process 签名:(PROGRAM INFILE BUFFER DISPLAY &rest ARGS)
              ;; BUFFER 为 list 形式时:(STDOUT-DEST STDERR-DEST),STDERR-DEST
              ;; 必须是文件名(string),不是 buffer object。我们用临时文件
              ;; 捕获 stderr,再读回字符串。
              ;; PROGRAM 必须是 string;argv 是 (exe "--domain" "..." cmd args...),
              ;; 拆出 exe 作为 PROGRAM,其余作为 ARGS。
              (setq status
                    (apply #'call-process
                           (car argv) nil (list stdout-buffer stderr-file) nil
                           (cdr argv)))
              (list :status (or status -1)
                    :stdout (string-trim
                             (with-current-buffer stdout-buffer (buffer-string)))
                    :stderr (string-trim
                             (with-temp-buffer
                               (insert-file-contents stderr-file)
                               (buffer-string)))
                    :command argv
                    :domain dom))
          (when (buffer-live-p stdout-buffer) (kill-buffer stdout-buffer))
          (when (file-exists-p stderr-file) (delete-file stderr-file)))))))

(cl-defun custom/agenote-call-async (domain command &rest args)
  "异步调用 agenote CLI,参数同 `custom/agenote-call'。
长任务(reindex / lint / deduplicate / commit / viz)应使用本函数,避免阻塞
Emacs。结果写入 `*agenote-<command>*' buffer(special-mode,`g' 重跑,`q' 退出)。

返回启动的 process 对象;同步路径请用 `custom/agenote-call'。"
  (let* ((dom (custom/agenote--ensure-domain domain))
         (exe (custom/agenote--resolve-executable))
         (argv (delq nil (append (list exe "--domain" (symbol-name dom) command) args)))
         (buf-name (format "*agenote-%s*" command))
         (buffer (get-buffer-create buf-name)))
    (if (not exe)
        (progn
          (with-current-buffer buffer
            (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
              (erase-buffer)
              (insert "agenote executable not found\n")))
          (display-buffer buffer)
          nil)
      (with-current-buffer buffer
        (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (format "$ %s\n\n" (string-join argv " ")))
          (special-mode)
          ;; `g' re-runs the same command.
          (let ((mode-map custom/agenote-async-mode-map))
            (use-local-map (or mode-map (make-sparse-keymap))))
          (define-key (current-local-map) "g"
                      (lambda () (interactive)
                        (apply #'custom/agenote-call-async dom command args)))))
      (let ((proc (make-process
                   :name (format "agenote %s" command)
                   :buffer buffer
                   :command argv
                   :connection-type 'pipe
                   :sentinel (lambda (proc event)
                               (let ((buf (process-buffer proc)))
                                 (when (buffer-live-p buf)
                                   (with-current-buffer buf
                                     (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
                                       (goto-char (point-max))
                                       (insert
                                        (format "\n[process %s]\n"
                                                (string-trim event)))))))))))
        (display-buffer buffer)
        proc))))

;; minimal keymap for async result buffers (defined here so async functions can
;; reference it before the dashboard/knowledge sections are loaded)
(defvar custom/agenote-async-mode-map
  (let ((m (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (set-keymap-parent m special-mode-map)
    m)
  "Keymap for `*agenote-<command>*' async result buffers.")

(defun custom/agenote-call-string (domain command &rest args)
  "同步调 agenote,返回 stdout 字符串，供只消费文本的旧调用点使用。
DOMAIN/COMMAND/ARGS 同 `custom/agenote-call'。失败返回空串。
新代码应直接用 `custom/agenote-call';本 wrapper 仅为兼容旧调用点保留。"
  (plist-get (apply #'custom/agenote-call domain command args) :stdout))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<helpers/agenote-process>>
#+end_src

** Frame 生命周期与 daemon
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: frame
:END:

daemon/client 架构下的两阶段 frame 生命周期：created 只处理 display 初始化，server-ready 处理依赖最终启动 buffer 的行为。每个 initializer 通过 frame parameter 保证成功执行一次。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar custom--frame-created-initializers nil)
(defvar custom--server-ready-initializers nil)

(defun custom--run-frame-initializers (frame phase functions)
  "在 FRAME 运行 PHASE 对应的 FUNCTIONS，每个函数成功执行一次。"
  (when (frame-live-p frame)
    (let* ((parameter (pcase phase
                        ('created 'custom-frame-created-done)
                        ('server-ready 'custom-frame-server-ready-done)))
           (done (frame-parameter frame parameter)))
      (dolist (function functions)
        (unless (memq function done)
          (condition-case err
              (progn
                (funcall function frame)
                (push function done)
                (set-frame-parameter frame parameter done))
            (error
             (message "[frame:%s] %s 失败: %s"
                      phase function (error-message-string err)))))))))

(defun custom--dispatch-frame-created (frame)
  "运行 FRAME 的 created initializer。"
  (custom--run-frame-initializers
   frame 'created custom--frame-created-initializers))

(defun custom--dispatch-server-ready (&optional frame)
  "运行 FRAME 的 server-ready initializer。"
  (custom--run-frame-initializers
   (or frame (selected-frame)) 'server-ready custom--server-ready-initializers))

(defun custom/add-frame-created-hook (function)
  "把 FUNCTION 注册到 frame-created 阶段。"
  (unless (memq function custom--frame-created-initializers)
    (setq custom--frame-created-initializers
          (append custom--frame-created-initializers (list function))))
  (add-hook 'after-make-frame-functions #'custom--dispatch-frame-created)
  (unless (daemonp)
    (custom--dispatch-frame-created (selected-frame))))

(defun custom/add-server-ready-hook (function)
  "把 FUNCTION 注册到 server-ready 阶段。"
  (unless (memq function custom--server-ready-initializers)
    (setq custom--server-ready-initializers
          (append custom--server-ready-initializers (list function))))
  (if (daemonp)
      (add-hook 'server-after-make-frame-hook #'custom--dispatch-server-ready)
    (if after-init-time
        (custom--dispatch-server-ready (selected-frame))
      (add-hook 'emacs-startup-hook #'custom--dispatch-server-ready))))

(defun custom/daemon-runtime-p ()
  "返回当前是否为可交互的 daemon 会话（用于延迟预热等场景）。"
  (and (daemonp) (not noninteractive)))
#+end_src

** 键位声明
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: key-helper
:END:

=custom:binding-spec= 是本地键位、Which-key、帮助与 Dashboard 的唯一声明源。每条声明在绑定真实 keymap 的同时登记描述；第三方既有 keymap 的翻译继续留在 =data/which-key-zh.el= 。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar custom:binding-spec nil
  "本地声明式键位表。")

(defvar custom--pending-wk-descs nil
  "Which-key 加载前暂存的全局键位描述。")

(defun custom/binding--record (entry)
  "把 ENTRY 追加到 binding spec，并拒绝重复 key/mode。"
  (let ((key (plist-get entry :key))
        (mode (or (plist-get entry :mode) 'global)))
    (when (seq-find
           (lambda (existing)
             (and (equal key (plist-get existing :key))
                  (eq mode (or (plist-get existing :mode) 'global))))
           custom:binding-spec)
      (error "重复键位声明: %s (%s)" key mode))
    (setq custom:binding-spec
          (append custom:binding-spec (list entry)))))

(defun custom/binding--register-which-key (key description &optional mode)
  "为 KEY 注册 DESCRIPTION；MODE 非 nil 时注册 major-mode 描述。"
  (cond
   ((and mode (featurep 'which-key))
    (which-key-add-major-mode-key-based-replacements mode key description))
   ((featurep 'which-key)
    (which-key-add-key-based-replacements key description))
   ((not mode)
    (push (cons key description) custom--pending-wk-descs))))

(defun custom/bind (key target description group &rest properties)
  "声明并绑定全局 KEY 到 TARGET。
DESCRIPTION、GROUP 与 PROPERTIES 同时供 Which-key、帮助和 Dashboard 使用。"
  (let ((entry (append (list :key key
                             :command target
                             :description description
                             :group group)
                       properties)))
    (custom/binding--record entry)
    (unless (plist-get entry :prefix)
      (keymap-global-set key target))
    (custom/binding--register-which-key key description)
    entry))

(defun custom/bind-local (feature mode map key command description group)
  "声明 FEATURE 中 MODE/MAP 的局部 KEY。"
  (let ((entry (list :key key
                     :command command
                     :description description
                     :group group
                     :mode mode
                     :map map)))
    (custom/binding--record entry)
    (eval-after-load feature
      (lambda ()
        (keymap-set (symbol-value map) key command)
        (custom/binding--register-which-key key description mode)))
    entry))

(defun custom/declare-binding-prefix (key description)
  "声明 Dashboard 展示的前缀 KEY。"
  (custom/bind key nil description nil :prefix t :dashboard t))

(defun custom/register-pending-wk-descs ()
  "把暂存的全局键位描述注册到 Which-key。"
  (dolist (entry (nreverse custom--pending-wk-descs))
    (which-key-add-key-based-replacements (car entry) (cdr entry)))
  (setq custom--pending-wk-descs nil))

(defun custom/binding-dashboard-bindings ()
  "从 binding spec 生成 Dashboard 前缀摘要。"
  (mapcar
   (lambda (entry)
     (cons (plist-get entry :key) (plist-get entry :description)))
   (seq-filter (lambda (entry) (plist-get entry :dashboard))
               custom:binding-spec)))

(defun custom/binding-help-sections ()
  "从 binding spec 生成帮助分组。"
  (let (order table)
    (dolist (entry custom:binding-spec)
      (when-let* ((group (plist-get entry :group)))
        (unless (member group order)
          (setq order (append order (list group))))
        (push (cons (plist-get entry :key)
                    (plist-get entry :description))
              (alist-get group table nil nil #'equal))))
    (mapcar
     (lambda (group)
       (cons group (nreverse (alist-get group table nil nil #'equal))))
     order)))
#+end_src

** 包管理与加载策略
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: package-loading
:END:

声明本配置依赖 Guix 提供的包, =EMACSLOADPATH= 已预设,无需手动安装。

+ ~use-package-always-ensure nil~ :禁止 use-package 调用 package.el 安装
+ ~package-enable-at-startup nil~ :禁用 package.el 自动初始化(实际由 early-init.el 在更早时机设置,这里再保险设一次)
+ 所有包直接 =(require 'xxx)= 或 =(use-package xxx)= ,Guix 已在 =EMACSLOADPATH= 中放好

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'use-package)

(setq use-package-always-ensure nil
      package-enable-at-startup nil)

;; native-comp 的 eln 缓存重定向到 var/,避免污染配置根目录(Emacs 29+)。
(when (fboundp 'startup-redirect-eln-cache)
  (startup-redirect-eln-cache
   (expand-file-name "var/eln-cache/" user-emacs-directory)))
#+end_src

** 基础行为设置
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: core-behavior
:END:

GC 策略、文件备份、历史记录、自动保存、custom-file 位置等基础设置。

+ ~gcmh-mode~ :启动后接管 GC,空闲时自动 GC,编辑时不打断
+ ~no-littering~ :把各种包生成的文件统一放到 =var/= 和 =etc/=
+ ~savehist~ / =recentf= :minibuffer 历史与最近文件持久化
+ ~desktop~ :会话恢复(daemon 默认关闭,避免恢复成本转移到启动)
+ ~custom-file~ :指向 =etc/custom.el= ,避免 customize 污染 init.el
+ 失去焦点后在空闲期保存正常访问的本地文件 buffer；不在焦点事件里同步写盘，也不保存内部/伪造临时 buffer

GCMH + 智能垃圾回收管理 (启动后接管 GC)
+ =gcmh-idle-delay 'auto'= 让 GCMH 自适应 idle 时间 (短空闲快 GC、长空闲等更空闲再 GC), 避免编辑过程中被打断。
+ =gcmh-high-cons-threshold 32 MiB= 是 daemon 长运行环境的合理上限:
  太小 (默认 16 MiB) → GC 频繁; 太大 (64+ MiB) → 单次 GC 卡顿长。
  32 MiB 在 8+ GiB 内存机器上不构成压力,与 gcmh-mode 维持的 low-cons-threshold (默认 1 MiB) 的差距让 GCMH 能区分"短暂峰值"和"持续压力"。
+ 不再在 use-package 外手动 =setq gc-cons-threshold=:
  GCMH 接管后该变量由 GCMH 动态调整,手动 setq 会与 GCMH 打架。

启动画面与基础体验
+ daemon 长驻下 client 关闭 *不退出 Emacs*; 真正 kill-emacs 时无需二次确认
+ 所有未保存内容在 focus-loss 时已落盘, 见下方 =after-focus-change-function= 。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package gcmh
  :demand t
  :custom
  (gcmh-idle-delay 'auto)
  (gcmh-auto-idle-delay-factor 10)
  (gcmh-high-cons-threshold (* 32 1024 1024))
  :config
  (gcmh-mode 1))

(setq read-process-output-max (* 4 1024 1024)
      process-adaptive-read-buffering nil
      fast-but-imprecise-scrolling t)

(setq inhibit-startup-screen t
      initial-scratch-message nil
      ring-bell-function 'ignore
      confirm-kill-emacs nil
      confirm-kill-processes nil)

(use-package no-littering
  :demand t
  :config
  (setq backup-directory-alist
        `((".*" . ,(no-littering-expand-var-file-name "backup/"))))
  (setq auto-save-file-name-transforms
        `((".*" ,(no-littering-expand-var-file-name "auto-save/") t)))
  (setq custom-file (no-littering-expand-etc-file-name "custom.el"))
  (when (file-exists-p custom-file)
    (load custom-file t)))

(use-package savehist
  :demand t
  :config
  (savehist-mode 1))

(use-package recentf
  :demand t
  :custom
  (recentf-max-saved-items 300)
  :config
  (recentf-mode 1))

(defconst custom:focus-save-idle-delay 1
  "失焦后保存用户文件 buffer 前等待的空闲秒数。")

(defvar custom--focus-save-timer nil
  "失焦保存的 pending idle timer。")

(defun custom/user-file-buffer-p ()
  "当前 buffer 是否为正常访问的、可保存的本地用户文件 buffer。"
  (and buffer-file-name
       buffer-file-truename
       (buffer-modified-p)
       (not buffer-read-only)
       (not (buffer-base-buffer))
       (not (file-remote-p buffer-file-name))))

(defun custom/save-user-file-buffers ()
  "保存已修改的本地用户文件 buffer，不触碰内部或远程 buffer。"
  (setq custom--focus-save-timer nil)
  (save-some-buffers t #'custom/user-file-buffer-p))

(defun custom/any-frame-focused-p ()
  "任一存活 frame 已聚焦或焦点状态未知时返回非 nil。"
  (seq-some (lambda (frame)
              (frame-focus-state frame))
            (frame-list)))

(defun custom/schedule-focus-save ()
  "根据当前焦点状态安排或取消空闲保存。"
  (when (timerp custom--focus-save-timer)
    (cancel-timer custom--focus-save-timer)
    (setq custom--focus-save-timer nil))
  (unless (custom/any-frame-focused-p)
    (setq custom--focus-save-timer
          (run-with-idle-timer custom:focus-save-idle-delay nil
                               #'custom/save-user-file-buffers))))

(add-function :after after-focus-change-function #'custom/schedule-focus-save)

(unless (or (daemonp) noninteractive)
  (server-mode 1))
#+end_src

* 界面与外观
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: appearance
:END:

所有 display-dependent 的视觉配置:主题、字体、窗口分割、面板布局、mode-line、tab-line、dashboard、帮助系统、右键菜单与 which-key 中文化。

display-dependent 设置统一通过 =custom/apply-frame-appearance= 在新 frame 创建时触发(见 *主题与字体* )。

** Which-key 中文描述数据
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: which-key-data
:END:

全局键、major mode 局部键与正则替换规则的数据源。行为注册位于 [[#which-key-ui][Which-key 菜单注册]]。

*** 外置翻译数据

翻译表是独立的数据资产：[[file:data/which-key-zh.el][Which-key 中文描述]]、[[file:data/context-menu-zh.el][右键菜单中文描述]] 与 [[file:data/help-zh.el][快捷键帮助中文描述]]。
修改数据文件后重启 Emacs 即可生效；其中只能写注释和预定变量的字面量 =setq=，不得定义函数、feature 或副作用。
=scripts/configctl check= 会校验文件名、变量名、括号和数据边界。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/i18n-data :tangle no
(defconst custom:i18n-data-directory
  (expand-file-name "data/" custom:emacs-dir)
  "本地化纯数据文件目录。")

(defconst custom:i18n-data-files
  '("which-key-zh.el" "context-menu-zh.el" "help-zh.el")
  "按加载顺序列出的本地化数据文件。")

(defvar custom:which-key-description-spec nil)
(defvar custom:which-key-major-mode-description-spec nil)
(defvar custom:which-key-regexp-replacements nil)
(defvar custom:context-menu-label-translations nil)
(defvar custom:help-introduction nil)

(defun custom/load-i18n-data ()
  "加载并验证本地化纯数据文件。"
  (dolist (name custom:i18n-data-files)
    (let ((file (expand-file-name name custom:i18n-data-directory)))
      (unless (file-readable-p file)
        (error "本地化数据文件不可读: %s" file))
      (load file nil 'nomessage)))
  (dolist (symbol '(custom:which-key-description-spec
                    custom:which-key-major-mode-description-spec
                    custom:which-key-regexp-replacements
                    custom:context-menu-label-translations
                    custom:help-introduction))
    (unless (listp (symbol-value symbol))
      (error "本地化数据格式无效: %s" symbol))))

(custom/load-i18n-data)
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<appearance/i18n-data>>
#+end_src

** 右键菜单本地化
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: context-menu-i18n
:END:

集中维护菜单标签翻译,并在最终 keymap 上递归替换显示文本;不改写各包的菜单生成逻辑。

*** 翻译数据

翻译表位于 [[file:data/context-menu-zh.el][data/context-menu-zh.el]]。

*** Keymap 翻译引擎

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/context-menu-engine :tangle no
(defun custom/context-menu--translate-string (string)
  "翻译 STRING；未登记时返回原字符串。"
  (let* ((plain (substring-no-properties string))
         (translation (cdr (assoc plain custom:context-menu-label-translations))))
    (if (not translation)
        string
      (if (> (length string) 0)
          (apply #'propertize translation (text-properties-at 0 string))
        translation))))

(defun custom/context-menu--translate-object (object)
  "递归翻译 OBJECT 中的字符串。"
  (cond
   ((stringp object)
    (custom/context-menu--translate-string object))
   ((consp object)
    (mapcar #'custom/context-menu--translate-object object))
   (t object)))

(defun custom/context-menu--translate-menu-item (item)
  "翻译菜单项 ITEM 的标题和子菜单。"
  (when (and (consp item) (eq (car item) 'menu-item))
    (let ((label-cell (cdr item))
          (binding (nth 2 item))
          (properties (nthcdr 3 item)))
      (when label-cell
        (setcar label-cell
                (custom/context-menu--translate-object (car label-cell))))
      (when (keymapp binding)
        (custom/context-menu--translate-keymap binding))
      (while (consp properties)
        (when (and (eq (car properties) :help)
                   (consp (cdr properties)))
          (setcar (cdr properties)
                  (custom/context-menu--translate-object (cadr properties))))
        (setq properties (cddr properties))))))

(defun custom/context-menu--translate-keymap (keymap)
  "递归翻译 KEYMAP 内的菜单标题。"
  (when (keymapp keymap)
    (when (and (consp keymap)
               (eq (car-safe keymap) 'keymap)
               (stringp (cadr keymap)))
      (setcar (cdr keymap)
              (custom/context-menu--translate-string (cadr keymap))))
    (map-keymap
     (lambda (_event binding)
       (cond
        ((and (consp binding) (eq (car binding) 'menu-item))
         (custom/context-menu--translate-menu-item binding))
        ((keymapp binding)
         (custom/context-menu--translate-keymap binding))))
     keymap))
  keymap)

(defun custom/context-menu-translate (menu)
  "翻译 `context-menu-map' 返回的 MENU。"
  (custom/context-menu--translate-keymap menu))

(when (fboundp 'context-menu-map)
  (unless (advice-member-p #'custom/context-menu-translate #'context-menu-map)
    (advice-add 'context-menu-map :filter-return #'custom/context-menu-translate)))

(with-eval-after-load 'magit-section
  (unless (advice-member-p #'custom/context-menu--translate-string
                           #'magit-menu-format-desc)
    (advice-add 'magit-menu-format-desc :filter-return
                #'custom/context-menu--translate-string)))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<appearance/context-menu-engine>>
#+end_src

** 快捷键帮助
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: help
:END:

维护完整快捷键参考与 Dashboard 复用的精简入口。

*** 帮助数据

键位、Which-key 描述、帮助分组和 Dashboard 摘要统一由 [[#keybindings][键位声明]] 中的 =custom/bind= 写入 =custom:binding-spec= 生成(函数 =custom/binding-help-sections= 与 =custom/binding-dashboard-bindings=)。本节只剩帮助页引言文字(=custom:help-introduction=)和外置到 [[file:data/help-zh.el][data/help-zh.el]] 的非键位说明;键位相关的描述不再外置。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/help-data :tangle no
(defface custom-help-section-title
  '((t :weight bold :height 1.2))
  "帮助缓冲区分类标题样式。前景色由 color-scheme hook 统一设置。"
  :group 'help)

#+end_src

*** 帮助渲染与入口

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/help-render :tangle no
(defun custom/help--extract-dashboard-bindings ()
  "返回 dashboard 需要展示的关键前缀绑定。"
  (custom/binding-dashboard-bindings))

(defun custom/help--insert-section (title bindings)
  "向当前 buffer 插入一个分类段落。
TITLE 为分类标题,BINDINGS 为 ((key . desc) ...) 列表。"
  (insert (propertize (format "═══ %s ═══" title)
                      'face 'custom-help-section-title))
  (insert "\n\n")
  (dolist (binding bindings)
    (insert (format "  %-24s %s\n"
                    (propertize (car binding) 'face 'font-lock-keyword-face)
                    (cdr binding))))
  (insert "\n"))

(defun custom/show-help ()
  "显示完整的快捷键帮助。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((buf (get-buffer-create "*Shortcuts Help*")))
    (with-current-buffer buf
      (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
        (erase-buffer)
        (special-mode)
        (insert "\n")
        (insert (propertize "快捷键参考\n\n"
                            'face '(:height 1.5 :weight bold)))
        (dolist (paragraph custom:help-introduction)
          (insert paragraph "\n"))
        (insert "\n")
        (dolist (section (custom/binding-help-sections))
          (custom/help--insert-section (car section) (cdr section)))
        (insert (propertize "提示:" 'face '(:weight bold)))
        (insert " 按 ")
        (insert (propertize "q" 'face 'font-lock-keyword-face))
        (insert " 关闭此帮助窗口\n"))
      (goto-char (point-min)))
    (pop-to-buffer buf)))

#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<appearance/help-data>>
<<appearance/help-render>>
#+end_src

** Modeline
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: modeline
:END:

Starship 风格响应式 mode-line:
+ 四档布局保留，但 redisplay 只经过一个顶层 renderer
+ tier、路径和状态在一次调用内各计算一次。
+ 图标、路径和 buffer 名采用缓存；路径中的单独 =.= 目录保留为 =.= ，避免隐藏目录缩写产生空字符串。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'battery)
(require 'project)

(declare-function nerd-icons-icon-for-file "nerd-icons")
(declare-function nerd-icons-icon-for-mode "nerd-icons")
(declare-function nerd-icons-faicon "nerd-icons")
(declare-function nerd-icons-mdicon "nerd-icons")
(defvar mode-line-format-right-align)

(defcustom custom:modeline-show-time t
  "是否在 mode-line 右段显示时间。"
  :type 'boolean)

(defcustom custom:modeline-show-vcs t
  "是否在 mode-line 显示 VCS 分支。"
  :type 'boolean)

(defcustom custom:modeline-path-max-width 40
  "路径段最大显示宽度。"
  :type 'integer)

(defvar custom:modeline--nerd-icons-available
  (require 'nerd-icons nil t))

(defvar custom:modeline--icon-cache (make-hash-table :test #'equal)
  "Mode-line 静态图标缓存。")

(defconst custom:modeline-icon-cache-limit 256
  "Mode-line 图标缓存的最大条目数。")

(defvar-local custom:modeline--flymake-counts '(0 0))
(defvar-local custom:modeline--eglot-info nil)
(defvar-local custom:modeline--project-cache :unknown)
(defvar-local custom:modeline--vc-state-cache :unknown)
(defvar-local custom:modeline--path-cache nil)
(defvar-local custom:modeline--name-cache nil)

(defun custom/modeline-tier (&optional window)
  "按 WINDOW 宽度返回 wide、medium、narrow 或 compact。"
  (let ((width (window-width window)))
    (cond
     ((>= width 120) 'wide)
     ((>= width 100) 'medium)
     ((>= width 80) 'narrow)
     (t 'compact))))

(defun custom/modeline--icon (name &optional file)
  "返回 Nerd Font 图标 NAME；FILE 非 nil 时按文件类型取图标。

NAME 是 nerd-icons 图标名(如 \"nf-md-source_branch\")。自动按前缀分发:
  - \"nf-md-*\" → nerd-icons-mdicon
  - \"nf-fa-*\"  → nerd-icons-faicon
  - 其他         → 先试 mdicon 再试 faicon
TTY 下 Nerd Font 字形以普通文字渲染（用户终端已安装 Nerd Font），
故不按 display 类型守卫，只看 nerd-icons 包是否可用。出错时返回空串。"
  (if custom:modeline--nerd-icons-available
      (let* ((key (if (and file (stringp file))
                      (list name (file-name-nondirectory file))
                    name))
             (cached (gethash key custom:modeline--icon-cache :missing)))
        (if (not (eq cached :missing))
            cached
          (let ((icon
                 (or (ignore-errors
                       (cond
                        ((and file (stringp file))
                         (nerd-icons-icon-for-file file))
                        ((string-prefix-p "nf-md-" name)
                         (nerd-icons-mdicon name))
                        ((string-prefix-p "nf-fa-" name)
                         (nerd-icons-faicon name))
                        (t
                         (if (string-prefix-p "nf-" name)
                             (nerd-icons-mdicon name)
                           (nerd-icons-faicon name)))))
                     "")))
            (when (>= (hash-table-count custom:modeline--icon-cache)
                      custom:modeline-icon-cache-limit)
              (clrhash custom:modeline--icon-cache))
            (puthash key icon custom:modeline--icon-cache)
            icon)))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--with-icon (icon text)
  "组合 ICON 与 TEXT。"
  (cond
   ((string-empty-p (or icon "")) (or text ""))
   ((string-empty-p (or text "")) icon)
   (t (concat icon " " text))))

(defun custom/modeline--segment (text face)
  "用 FACE 呈现非空 TEXT。"
  (if (string-empty-p (or text ""))
      ""
    (propertize text 'face face)))

(defun custom/modeline--join (&rest segments)
  "以 Starship 风格分隔符连接非空 SEGMENTS。"
  (string-join (seq-filter
                (lambda (segment)
                  (and (stringp segment) (not (string-empty-p segment))))
                segments)
               " · "))

(defun custom/modeline--path-initials (path)
  "把 PATH 的目录部分缩成首字母。"
  (let* ((remote (or (file-remote-p path) ""))
         (local (or (file-remote-p path 'localname) path))
         (parts (split-string
                 (string-remove-prefix
                  "~/" (abbreviate-file-name (directory-file-name local)))
                 "/" t))
         (short
          (mapcar
           (lambda (part)
             (if (string-prefix-p "." part)
                 (let ((rest (string-remove-prefix "." part)))
                   (if (string-empty-p rest)
                       "."
                     (concat "." (substring rest 0 1))))
               (substring part 0 1)))
           parts)))
    (concat remote (string-join short "/"))))

(defun custom/modeline--project-info ()
  "返回当前 buffer 的 (ROOT NAME) project 缓存。"
  (when (eq custom:modeline--project-cache :unknown)
    (setq custom:modeline--project-cache
          (condition-case nil
              (when-let* ((project (project-current nil))
                          (root (project-root project)))
                (list root
                      (file-name-nondirectory (directory-file-name root))))
            (error nil))))
  custom:modeline--project-cache)

(defun custom/modeline--reset-project-cache (&rest _)
  "失效当前 buffer 的 project 缓存。"
  (setq custom:modeline--project-cache :unknown
        custom:modeline--path-cache nil))

(defun custom/modeline--refresh-vc-state (&rest _)
  "通过 VC 公开接口刷新当前 buffer 的状态缓存。"
  (setq custom:modeline--vc-state-cache
        (when buffer-file-name
          (condition-case nil
              (when-let* ((backend (vc-backend buffer-file-name)))
                (vc-state buffer-file-name backend))
            (error nil)))))

(defun custom/modeline--refresh-flymake-counts (&rest _)
  "通过 Flymake 公开接口刷新错误与警告计数。"
  (setq custom:modeline--flymake-counts
        (if (and (bound-and-true-p flymake-mode)
                 (fboundp 'flymake-diagnostics))
            (let ((errors 0)
                  (warnings 0))
              (dolist (diagnostic (flymake-diagnostics))
                (let* ((type (flymake-diagnostic-type diagnostic))
                       (severity
                        (alist-get 'severity
                                   (alist-get type flymake-diagnostic-types))))
                  (cond
                   ((and severity (>= severity 2)) (cl-incf errors))
                   ((and severity (>= severity 1)) (cl-incf warnings)))))
              (list errors warnings))
          '(0 0))))

(defun custom/modeline--refresh-eglot-info (&rest _)
  "通过 Eglot 公开接口刷新连接状态缓存。"
  (setq custom:modeline--eglot-info
        (when (fboundp 'eglot-current-server)
          (when (ignore-errors (eglot-current-server))
            '("eglot" connected)))))

(defun custom/modeline--path-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的路径段。"
  (if (or (eq tier 'compact) (not buffer-file-name))
      ""
    (let* ((project (custom/modeline--project-info))
           (key (list buffer-file-name project custom:modeline-path-max-width)))
      (if (equal key (car-safe custom:modeline--path-cache))
          (cdr custom:modeline--path-cache)
        (let* ((root (car-safe project))
               (directory (file-name-directory buffer-file-name))
               (path (if (and root (file-in-directory-p buffer-file-name root))
                         (file-relative-name directory root)
                       directory))
               (display (custom/modeline--path-initials path))
               (segment
                (if (string-empty-p (or display ""))
                    ""
                  (custom/modeline--segment
                   (custom/modeline--with-icon
                    (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-folder_open")
                    (truncate-string-to-width
                     display custom:modeline-path-max-width 0 nil t))
                   'shadow))))
          (setq custom:modeline--path-cache (cons key segment))
          segment)))))

(defun custom/modeline--name-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的 buffer 名。compact 档由 major-left 段独占显示 major,
此处返回空串避免 compact 档 major 名重复出现。"
  (if (eq tier 'compact)
      ""
    (let ((key (list tier buffer-file-name (buffer-name))))
      (if (equal key (car-safe custom:modeline--name-cache))
          (cdr custom:modeline--name-cache)
        (let* ((name (if buffer-file-name
                         (file-name-nondirectory buffer-file-name)
                       (buffer-name)))
               (width (pcase tier ('wide 32) ('medium 24) (_ 18)))
               (segment
                (custom/modeline--segment
                 (custom/modeline--with-icon
                  (custom/modeline--icon "nf-fa-file_o" buffer-file-name)
                  (truncate-string-to-width name width 0 nil t))
                 'font-lock-function-name-face)))
          (setq custom:modeline--name-cache (cons key segment))
          segment)))))

(defun custom/modeline--branch-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的 VCS 分支。"
  (if (or (eq tier 'compact) (not custom:modeline-show-vcs))
      ""
    (let ((branch
           (when (and (stringp vc-mode)
                      (string-match
                       "\\(?:Git\\|Hg\\|SVN\\)[-:]\\([^() ]+\\)" vc-mode))
             (string-trim (match-string 1 vc-mode)))))
      (if branch
          (custom/modeline--segment
           (custom/modeline--with-icon
            (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-source_branch") branch)
           'font-lock-type-face)
        ""))))

(defun custom/modeline--vc-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的修改与 VC 状态(含状态 icon)。
复刻原版 git-status:10 种状态各自带语义化 nerd-icons icon + face。"
  (if (or (eq tier 'compact) (not buffer-file-name))
      ""
    (when (eq custom:modeline--vc-state-cache :unknown)
      (custom/modeline--refresh-vc-state))
    (let* ((state (if (buffer-modified-p)
                      'buffer-modified
                    custom:modeline--vc-state-cache))
           (spec (pcase state
                   ('up-to-date       (list "clean"     "nf-md-check_circle"        'success))
                   ('edited           (list "edited"    "nf-md-pencil"              'warning))
                   ('buffer-modified  (list "unsaved"   "nf-md-content_save_alert"  'warning))
                   ('added            (list "added"     "nf-md-plus_circle"         'success))
                   ('removed          (list "removed"   "nf-md-minus_circle"        'error))
                   ('missing          (list "missing"   "nf-md-alert_circle"        'error))
                   ('conflict         (list "conflict"  "nf-md-alert_octagon"       'error))
                   ('needs-merge      (list "merge"     "nf-md-source_merge"        'warning))
                   ('needs-update     (list "update"    "nf-md-cloud_download"      'warning))
                   ('ignored          (list "ignored"   "nf-md-eye_off"             'shadow))
                   ('unregistered     (list "untracked" "nf-md-help_circle"         'warning))
                   (_ nil))))
      (if spec
          (pcase-let ((`(,text ,icon-name ,face) spec))
            (custom/modeline--segment
             (custom/modeline--with-icon
              (custom/modeline--icon icon-name) text)
             face))
        ""))))

(defun custom/modeline--coding-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的编码(wide/medium 显示,与旧版 tier 密度一致)。"
  (if (memq tier '(wide medium))
      (custom/modeline--segment
       (custom/modeline--with-icon
        (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-lock")
        (upcase
         (symbol-name
          (or (coding-system-base buffer-file-coding-system) 'undecided))))
       'font-lock-string-face)
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--position-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的位置百分比与 buffer 大小(wide 显示大小)。"
  (if (memq tier '(wide medium))
      (let* ((range (max 1 (- (point-max) (point-min))))
             (percent
              (round (* 100.0
                        (/ (float (- (point) (point-min))) range))))
             (base (custom/modeline--with-icon
                    (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-flag_checkered")
                    (format "%d%%%%" percent)))
             (text (if (eq tier 'wide)
                       (format "%s %s" base
                               (custom/modeline--with-icon
                                (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-format_list_bulleted")
                                (file-size-human-readable (buffer-size))))
                     base)))
        (custom/modeline--segment text 'font-lock-doc-face))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--location-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的行列坐标 (%l,%c)。compact 档不显示。
wide/medium/narrow 都显示(原版行为)。"
  (if (eq tier 'compact)
      ""
    (custom/modeline--segment
     (custom/modeline--with-icon
      (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-map_marker")
      (format "(%s)" (format-mode-line "%l,%c")))
     'shadow)))

(defun custom/modeline--diagnostics-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的 Flymake 诊断计数(wide/medium 显示)。"
  (if (and (memq tier '(wide medium))
           (bound-and-true-p flymake-mode))
      (pcase-let ((`(,errors ,warnings) custom:modeline--flymake-counts))
        (let* ((total (+ errors warnings))
               (icon-name (cond
                           ((> errors 0)   "nf-md-times_circle")
                           ((> warnings 0) "nf-md-alert")
                           (t              "nf-md-check_circle")))
               (text (pcase tier
                       ('wide    (format "E:%d W:%d" errors warnings))
                       ('medium  (format "%d:%d" errors warnings))
                       (_        (number-to-string total))))
               (face (cond
                      ((> errors 0)   'error)
                      ((> warnings 0) 'warning)
                      (t              'success))))
          (custom/modeline--segment
           (custom/modeline--with-icon (custom/modeline--icon icon-name) text)
           face)))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--eglot-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的 Eglot 状态(三态 + wide 显示 server 名,wide/medium 显示)。"
  (if (and (memq tier '(wide medium)) custom:modeline--eglot-info)
      (pcase-let ((`(,name ,status) custom:modeline--eglot-info))
        (let* ((point (pcase status
                        ('connected "●")
                        ('connecting "◐")
                        (_ "○")))
               (face (pcase status
                       ('connected 'success)
                       ('connecting 'warning)
                       (_ 'shadow)))
               (text (if (eq tier 'wide)
                         (format "%s %s" name point)
                       point)))
          (custom/modeline--segment
           (custom/modeline--with-icon
            (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-server_network") text)
           face)))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--global-segment (tier)
  "返回 TIER 对应的全局状态字符串。compact 档不显示。
过滤 `battery-mode-line-string' 和 `display-time-string',
避免与自定义 battery/time 段重复。"
  (if (eq tier 'compact)
      ""
    (let* ((items (if (listp global-mode-string)
                      global-mode-string
                    (list global-mode-string)))
           (filtered (seq-remove
                      (lambda (item)
                        (memq item '(battery-mode-line-string
                                     display-time-string)))
                      items))
           (text (string-trim (format-mode-line filtered))))
      (if (string-empty-p text) "" text))))

(defun custom/modeline--major-left-segment (tier)
  "compact 档左侧 major mode(其他档在右侧显示)。"
  (if (eq tier 'compact)
      (custom/modeline--segment
       (custom/modeline--with-icon
        (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-code_tags")
        (symbol-name major-mode))
       'font-lock-keyword-face)
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--major-right-segment (tier)
  "非 compact 档右侧 major mode(带 icon)。"
  (if (eq tier 'compact)
      ""
    (custom/modeline--segment
     (custom/modeline--with-icon
      (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-code_tags")
      (symbol-name major-mode))
     'font-lock-keyword-face)))

(defun custom/modeline--battery-segment (tier)
  "返回 wide 档电池状态(带 percent face 着色)。
需要 `display-battery-mode' 启用才会填充 `battery-mode-line-string'。"
  (if (eq tier 'wide)
      (let* ((raw (and (boundp 'battery-mode-line-string)
                       (stringp battery-mode-line-string)
                       (string-trim battery-mode-line-string)))
             (percent (and raw
                           (string-match "\\([0-9]+\\)%%" raw)
                           (string-to-number (match-string 1 raw))))
             (face (cond
                    ((not percent) 'font-lock-doc-face)
                    ((>= percent 50) 'success)
                    ((>= percent 20) 'warning)
                    (t 'error))))
        (if (and raw (not (string-empty-p raw)))
            (custom/modeline--segment
             (custom/modeline--with-icon
              (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-battery")
              (replace-regexp-in-string "%" "%%" raw nil t))
             face)
          ""))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline--time-segment (tier)
  "返回非 compact 档时间(wide 显示秒)。"
  (if (and custom:modeline-show-time (not (eq tier 'compact)))
      (let* ((wide (eq tier 'wide))
             (time (format-time-string (if wide "%H:%M:%S" "%H:%M"))))
        (custom/modeline--segment
         (custom/modeline--with-icon
          (custom/modeline--icon "nf-md-clock")
          time)
         'font-lock-string-face))
    ""))

(defun custom/modeline-render-left ()
  "计算并返回左段(文件相关)字符串。

拆分为 left/right 两个 renderer 而非单一 render 返回含
`mode-line-format-right-align' 的 list:右对齐符号嵌套在 :eval 返回的
list 里时,Emacs 测量后续内容像素宽失效(nerd-icons 图标像素宽≠列宽),
会把右段推出窗口可见区。right-align 必须位于 mode-line-format 顶层
list 才能正确测量(旧版结构,带图标右对齐也正确)。"
  (let ((tier (custom/modeline-tier)))
    (custom/modeline--join
     (custom/modeline--path-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--name-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--branch-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--vc-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--coding-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--position-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--location-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--major-left-segment tier))))

(defun custom/modeline-render-right ()
  "计算并返回右段(系统/状态相关)字符串。见 `custom/modeline-render-left'。"
  (let ((tier (custom/modeline-tier)))
    (custom/modeline--join
     (custom/modeline--diagnostics-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--eglot-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--global-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--major-right-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--battery-segment tier)
     (custom/modeline--time-segment tier))))

(with-eval-after-load 'flymake
  (add-hook 'flymake-after-diagnostics-hook
            #'custom/modeline--refresh-flymake-counts)
  (add-hook 'flymake-mode-hook
            #'custom/modeline--refresh-flymake-counts))

(with-eval-after-load 'eglot
  (add-hook 'eglot-managed-mode-hook #'custom/modeline--refresh-eglot-info)
  (add-hook 'eglot-server-initialized-hook
            #'custom/modeline--refresh-eglot-info))

(add-hook 'find-file-hook #'custom/modeline--refresh-vc-state)
(add-hook 'after-save-hook #'custom/modeline--refresh-vc-state)
(add-hook 'after-revert-hook #'custom/modeline--refresh-vc-state)
(add-hook 'after-save-hook #'custom/modeline--reset-project-cache)
(add-hook 'after-change-major-mode-hook #'custom/modeline--reset-project-cache)

;; 顶层 list 结构:right-align 必须在顶层,嵌套在 :eval 返回 list 里会导致
;; 右段像素测量错误而被推出窗口(见 render-left 注释)。
(setq-default mode-line-format
              '(" "
                (:eval (custom/modeline-render-left))
                mode-line-format-right-align
                " "
                (:eval (custom/modeline-render-right))))
(setq-default mode-line-inactive-format
              '(" "
                (:eval (custom/modeline-render-left))
                mode-line-format-right-align
                " "
                (:eval (custom/modeline-render-right))))

;; 数据源 minor mode 启用:
;; - display-battery-mode 维护 `battery-mode-line-string'(modeline 电池段数据源)
;; - 时间由 `format-time-string' 在 time-segment 内直接计算,不启用
;;   display-time-mode(避免其写入 global-mode-string 导致时间重复显示)。
;; - size-indication-mode 保持 nil:position 段 wide 档已显示 buffer 大小。
(display-battery-mode 1)
#+end_src

** Tab-line
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: tab-line
:END:

基于内置 =tab-line= 的 per-frame 文件标签,隔离多个 emacsclient frame 的缓冲区序列。

+ 禁用 frame 级 =tab-bar=,改用 window-local =tab-line= 作为文件标签栏——每个 window 一条标签栏,避免与 [[#frame][Frame 生命周期]] 的 per-frame 隔离冲突。
+ 只有关联文件的 buffer 才出现在标签栏(=custom/tab-visible-p= 白名单),临时 buffer 与 minibuffer 自动排除。

*** 常量与窗口定位

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/tab-line-core :tangle no
(require 'tab-line)
(tab-bar-mode -1)

(defconst custom:tabs-name-max-width 36
  "单个标签名最大宽度。")

(defvar-local custom/tab-line--name-cache nil
  "当前 buffer 的 tab-line 标签名缓存。")

(defun custom/tab-visible-p (buffer)
  "判断 BUFFER 是否应该显示在标签栏中。
白名单机制:只有关联文件的 buffer 才显示。"
  (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
       (buffer-file-name buffer)))

(defun custom--tabs-target-window (&optional window)
  "返回标签命令应优先操作的 WINDOW。"
  (or (and (window-live-p window) window)
      (selected-window)))
#+end_src

*** Per-frame 缓冲区跟踪

每个 frame 维护一个有序的文件 buffer 列表,存储在 frame parameter =custom--frame-tab-buffers= 中。这是多 emacsclient frame 隔离的核心:不同 frame 的标签序列完全独立。

#+attr_org: :width 60
| 状态变更      | 触发 hook                              | 行为                                     |
|---------------+----------------------------------------+------------------------------------------|
| 缓冲区可见    | =window-buffer-change-functions=         | 唯一注册路径:追加到 frame 列表尾部(去重) |
| 缓冲区被 kill | =kill-buffer-hook=                       | 从所有 frame 的列表移除                  |
| frame 被删除  | =delete-frame-functions=                 | 丢弃该 frame 的标签状态                  |
| 项目切换      | =project-after-switch-project-functions= | 清空当前 frame 列表(见 [[#project-navigation][项目与导航]])       |

注册顺序即标签排列顺序(append-only),保证用户切换的稳定心智模型。=window-buffer-change-functions= 是唯一的注册入口,不再依赖 =find-file-hook= / =server-switch-hook= 等重复触发源。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/tab-line-tracking :tangle no
(defun custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list (&optional frame)
  "返回 FRAME 的标签缓冲区列表。"
  (frame-parameter (or frame (selected-frame)) 'custom--frame-tab-buffers))

(defun custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list (buffers &optional frame)
  "设置 FRAME 的标签缓冲区列表为 BUFFERS。"
  (set-frame-parameter (or frame (selected-frame)) 'custom--frame-tab-buffers buffers))

(defun custom--tabs-register-buffer (buffer &optional frame)
  "将 BUFFER 注册到 FRAME 的标签列表尾部(去重)。"
  (let* ((target-frame (or frame (selected-frame)))
         (buffers (or (custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list target-frame) '())))
    (unless (memq buffer buffers)
      (custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list
       (append buffers (list buffer))
       target-frame))))

(defun custom--tabs-unregister-buffer (buffer &optional frame)
  "从 FRAME 的标签列表中移除 BUFFER。"
  (let* ((target-frame (or frame (selected-frame)))
         (buffers (or (custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list target-frame) '())))
    (when (memq buffer buffers)
      (custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list
       (delq buffer buffers)
       target-frame))))

(defun custom--tabs-on-window-buffer-change (frame-or-window)
  "缓冲区变更时,把新缓冲区注册到对应 frame。
FRAME-OR-WINDOW 可以是 frame(全局 hook)或 window(buffer-local hook)。
这是标签列表的唯一注册入口,不依赖 find-file-hook / server-switch-hook。"
  (cond
   ((frame-live-p frame-or-window)
    (walk-windows
     (lambda (window)
       (when (eq (window-frame window) frame-or-window)
         (let ((buffer (window-buffer window)))
           (when (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
                      (custom/tab-visible-p buffer)
                      (not (minibufferp buffer)))
             (custom--tabs-register-buffer buffer frame-or-window)))))
     'no-mini frame-or-window))
   ((window-live-p frame-or-window)
    (let ((buffer (window-buffer frame-or-window))
          (frame (window-frame frame-or-window)))
      (when (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
                 (custom/tab-visible-p buffer)
                 (not (minibufferp buffer)))
        (custom--tabs-register-buffer buffer frame))))))

(defun custom--tabs-on-kill-buffer ()
  "缓冲区被关闭时,从所有 frame 的标签列表中移除。"
  (let ((buffer (current-buffer)))
    (dolist (frame (frame-list))
      (when (frame-live-p frame)
        (let ((buffers (custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list frame)))
          (when (memq buffer buffers)
            (custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list (delq buffer buffers) frame)))))))

(defun custom/tabs-clear-for-frame (&optional frame)
  "清空 FRAME 的标签缓冲区列表(项目切换时调用)。
只清 frame parameter,不重建;后续 window-buffer-change 自然填充。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((target-frame (or frame (selected-frame))))
    (custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list nil target-frame)
    (walk-windows
     (lambda (window)
       (when (eq (window-frame window) target-frame)
         (set-window-parameter window 'tab-line-cache nil)))
     'no-mini target-frame)
    (when (called-interactively-p 'interactive)
      (message "已清空当前 frame 的标签列表"))))

(defun custom--tabs-on-frame-deletion (frame)
  "FRAME 删除时清理标签状态。"
  (custom--tabs-set-frame-buffer-list nil frame))

(defun custom--tabs-frame-buffers (&optional window)
  "返回 WINDOW 所在 frame 的存活 + 可见缓冲区列表。"
  (let* ((target-window (custom--tabs-target-window window))
         (target-frame (window-frame target-window))
         (frame-buffers (custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list target-frame)))
    (seq-filter (lambda (buf)
                  (and (buffer-live-p buf)
                       (custom/tab-visible-p buf)))
                (or frame-buffers '()))))
#+end_src

*** 标签渲染

渲染阶段不构造任何主题相关的 RGB 值,只选择内置语义 face(current/inactive),具体配色交给 =tab-line-tab-face-functions= 钩子和 [[tab-line-face-policy][face 继承策略]]。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/tab-line-render :tangle no
(defun custom/tab-line-tabs ()
  "返回当前窗口标签栏要展示的 buffer 列表。"
  (or (custom--tabs-frame-buffers (selected-window))
      (list (current-buffer))))

(defun custom/tab-line-tab-name (buffer &optional _buffers)
  "生成 BUFFER 的标签名(图标 + 名称 + 修改标记)。"
  (with-current-buffer buffer
    (let ((key (list (buffer-name) buffer-file-name major-mode
                     (buffer-modified-p))))
      (if (equal key (car-safe custom/tab-line--name-cache))
          (cdr custom/tab-line--name-cache)
        (let* ((name (truncate-string-to-width
                      (buffer-name)
                      custom:tabs-name-max-width 0 nil t))
               (icon (when custom:modeline--nerd-icons-available
                       (require 'nerd-icons)
                       (nerd-icons-icon-for-buffer)))
               (prefix (if (and (stringp icon)
                                (not (string-empty-p icon)))
                           (concat icon " ")
                         ""))
               (suffix (if (buffer-modified-p) " ●" ""))
               (label (concat prefix name suffix)))
          (setq custom/tab-line--name-cache (cons key label))
          label)))))

(defun custom--tab-line-help-echo (selected-p)
  "返回标签的中文提示文本。"
  (if selected-p
      "当前标签:鼠标中键关闭,右键打开菜单"
    "左键切换标签,鼠标中键关闭,右键打开菜单"))

(defun custom/tab-line-tab-name-format (tab tabs)
  "以接近 IDE 的样式格式化 TAB。
face 选择交给内置 `tab-line-tab-face-functions',不手动构造 RGB plist;
current/inactive 用内置 face,自动跟随主题。"
  (let* ((buffer-p (bufferp tab))
         (selected-p (if buffer-p
                         (eq tab (window-buffer))
                       (cdr (assq 'selected tab))))
         (name (if buffer-p
                   (funcall tab-line-tab-name-function tab tabs)
                 (cdr (assq 'name tab))))
         (face (if selected-p 'tab-line-tab-current 'tab-line-tab-inactive))
         (label (concat " " (string-replace "%" "%%" name)))
         (help-echo (custom--tab-line-help-echo selected-p)))
    (dolist (fn tab-line-tab-face-functions)
      (setq face (funcall fn tab tabs face buffer-p selected-p)))
    (propertize
     (concat label
             (when (and tab-line-close-button-show
                        (or (eq tab-line-close-button-show t)
                            (eq tab-line-close-button-show
                                (if selected-p 'selected 'non-selected))))
               tab-line-close-button))
     'face face
     'keymap tab-line-tab-map
     'mouse-face (unless selected-p 'tab-line-highlight)
     'help-echo help-echo
     'follow-link 'ignore
     'tab tab)))
#+end_src

*** 按钮与 Face
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: tab-line-face-policy
:END:

标签栏所有 face 走主题语义色继承,不构造任何显式 RGB 值。主题切换时由继承关系自动更新。TTY 下 [[#mixed-gui-tty-constraints][=custom/apply-terminal-faces=]] 把 =tab-line-tab-current= 的 =:background= 设为 =unspecified=（透明）、前景用 =brightwhite= + 下划线实现"当前标签"语义,无需专门维护 TTY plist。

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 状态         | 继承自              | 追加属性           | 目的                    |
|--------------+---------------------+--------------------+-------------------------|
| 当前标签     | =mode-line=           | semibold + 下划线  | 强对比底色,清晰标识当前 |
| 非当前标签   | =shadow=              | 无                 | 低对比,弱化背景标签     |
| 鼠标悬停高亮 | =mode-line-highlight= | 无                 | 复用主题原生 hover 表现 |
| 关闭按钮高亮 | =error=               | 去背景/边框/下划线 | 关闭动作的警示语义      |
| 标签栏整体   | 无                  | height 0.95        | 比正文略矮,视觉分隔     |

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/tab-line-faces :tangle no
(defun custom/apply-tab-line-button-preset ()
  "应用统一的标签栏按钮文本样式。"
  (setq tab-line-close-button
        (propertize " x "
                    'rear-nonsticky nil
                    'keymap tab-line-tab-close-map
                    'mouse-face 'tab-line-close-highlight
                    'help-echo "关闭标签")
        tab-line-left-button
        (propertize " < "
                    'rear-nonsticky nil
                    'keymap tab-line-left-map
                    'mouse-face 'tab-line-highlight
                    'help-echo "向左滚动标签栏")
        tab-line-right-button
        (propertize " > "
                    'rear-nonsticky nil
                    'keymap tab-line-right-map
                    'mouse-face 'tab-line-highlight
                    'help-echo "向右滚动标签栏")))

(set-face-attribute 'tab-line nil
                    :inherit nil
                    :height 0.95
                    :box nil
                    :overline nil
                    :underline nil)
(set-face-attribute 'tab-line-tab-current nil
                    :inherit 'mode-line
                    :weight 'semibold
                    :underline t)
(set-face-attribute 'tab-line-tab-inactive nil
                    :inherit 'shadow
                    :box nil)
(set-face-attribute 'tab-line-highlight nil
                    :inherit 'mode-line-highlight)
(set-face-attribute 'tab-line-close-highlight nil
                    :inherit 'error
                    :background 'unspecified
                    :box nil
                    :underline nil)
#+end_src

*** 标签操作

关闭语义对键盘和鼠标统一为 =bury + unregister=,不 kill buffer:标签从列表移除,但 buffer 内容保留,可被 =recentf= 或其他 window 重新引用。

#+attr_org: :width 60
| 入口     | 操作              | kill buffer? | fallback                        |
|----------+-------------------+--------------+---------------------------------|
| 键盘关闭 | bury + unregister | 否           | 切到下一个可见 buffer,无则 bury |
| 鼠标关闭 | 同上(共享路径)    | 否           | 同上                            |

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/tab-line-actions :tangle no
(defun custom--tabs-switch-to-buffer (buffer &optional window)
  "在 WINDOW 中切换到 BUFFER,并刷新标签显示。"
  (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
    (let ((target-window (custom--tabs-target-window window))
          (switch-to-buffer-obey-display-actions nil))
      (with-selected-window target-window
        (switch-to-buffer buffer)
        (tab-line-force-update t)))))

(defun custom/tabs-next (&optional arg)
  "切换到当前标签列表中的下一个标签。ARG 为步数。"
  (interactive "p")
  (let* ((target-window (custom--tabs-target-window))
         (buffers (custom--tabs-frame-buffers target-window))
         (current (window-buffer target-window))
         (position (seq-position buffers current))
         (step (or arg 1)))
    (when (and position buffers)
      (custom--tabs-switch-to-buffer
       (nth (mod (+ position step) (length buffers)) buffers)
       target-window))))

(defun custom/tabs-previous (&optional arg)
  "切换到当前标签列表中的上一个标签。ARG 为步数。"
  (interactive "p")
  (custom/tabs-next (- (or arg 1))))

(defun custom/tabs-select-index (index)
  "切换到当前标签列表中的第 INDEX 个标签。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((target-window (custom--tabs-target-window))
         (buffers (custom--tabs-frame-buffers target-window))
         (buffer (nth (1- index) buffers)))
    (if buffer
        (custom--tabs-switch-to-buffer buffer target-window)
      (user-error "当前标签数量不足 %d 个" index))))

(defun custom/tabs-close-buffer (&optional window)
  "关闭当前标签(bury + unregister,不 kill buffer)。
键盘与鼠标关闭语义一致;原鼠标分支缺 fallback 的不对称已修正。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((target-window (custom--tabs-target-window window))
         (buffer (window-buffer target-window))
         (target-frame (window-frame target-window)))
    (custom--tabs-unregister-buffer buffer target-frame)
    (if-let* ((next (seq-find (lambda (b)
                                (and (buffer-live-p b)
                                     (custom/tab-visible-p b)
                                     (not (eq b buffer))))
                              (custom--tabs-get-frame-buffer-list target-frame))))
        (custom--tabs-switch-to-buffer next target-window)
      (with-selected-window target-window
        (bury-buffer buffer)
        (let ((fallback (seq-find (lambda (b)
                                    (and (buffer-live-p b)
                                         (not (eq b buffer))))
                                  (buffer-list target-frame))))
          (when fallback
            (switch-to-buffer fallback)))
        (tab-line-force-update t)))))

(defun custom/tab-line-close-tab (tab)
  "鼠标关闭按钮回调:与键盘 close 走同一路径(语义对称)。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((buffer (if (bufferp tab) tab (current-buffer))))
    (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
      (save-window-excursion
        (select-window (custom--tabs-target-window))
        (switch-to-buffer buffer)
        (custom/tabs-close-buffer)))))
#+end_src

** Git 与窗口路由
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: git-display
:END:

提供 Git 文件/仓库操作及 Magit、终端等复用的 display-buffer 路由。

*** Git 命令

Git 操作辅助函数（供 C-c g 前缀和右键菜单复用）。
+ Magit 状态页路由到底部 ~side-window~ （40% 高度）
+ COMMIT_EDITMSG 经 ~server-window~ 路由到上方主编辑窗口（保持「上编辑 + 下 magit」布局一致）
+ *文件级操作* ：blame / log / diff / stage / discard / timemachine
+ *仓库级操作* ：push / pull / stash push / stash pop
+ 所有操作复用启动域声明的 =custom:executable-git= 。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/git-commands :tangle no
(defun custom/git-repo-root (&optional file)
  "返回 FILE 所在 Git 仓库根目录。"
  (when-let* ((target (or file (buffer-file-name) default-directory)))
    (locate-dominating-file target ".git")))

(defun custom/git-buffer-file ()
  "返回当前 buffer 对应的文件，不存在时抛出用户错误。"
  (or (buffer-file-name)
      (user-error "当前缓冲区没有关联文件")))

(defun custom/git--ensure-repo (&optional file)
  "确保 FILE 或当前 buffer 位于 Git 仓库中，并返回仓库根目录。"
  (or (custom/git-repo-root file)
      (user-error "当前不在 Git 仓库中")))

(defun custom/git--executable ()
  "返回 Git 命令名，由当前 `exec-path' 在调用时解析。"
  custom:executable-git)

(defun custom/git--run (&rest args)
  "在当前仓库中执行 Git ARGS。"
  (let ((default-directory (custom/git--ensure-repo)))
    (with-temp-buffer
      (let ((status (apply #'process-file (custom/git--executable) nil t nil args))
            (output (string-trim (buffer-string))))
        (unless (eq status 0)
          (error "Git 命令失败: %s"
                 (if (string-empty-p output) (string-join args " ") output)))
        output))))

(defun custom/git-status-dwim ()
  "打开当前仓库的 Magit 状态页。"
  (interactive)
  (magit-status-setup-buffer (custom/git--ensure-repo)))

(defun custom/git-blame-current-file ()
  "对当前文件执行 blame。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git-buffer-file)
  (call-interactively #'magit-blame-addition))

(defun custom/git-log-current-file ()
  "查看当前文件历史。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git-buffer-file)
  (call-interactively #'magit-log-buffer-file))

(defun custom/git-diff-current-file ()
  "查看当前文件 diff。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git-buffer-file)
  (call-interactively #'magit-diff-buffer-file))

(defun custom/git-stage-current-file ()
  "Stage 当前文件。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((file (custom/git-buffer-file)))
    (custom/git--ensure-repo file)
    (custom/git--run "add" "--" file)
    (message "已 stage: %s" (file-name-nondirectory file))))

(defun custom/git-discard-current-file ()
  "丢弃当前文件所有未提交修改。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((file (custom/git-buffer-file)))
    (custom/git--ensure-repo file)
    (when (y-or-n-p (format "丢弃 %s 的所有未提交修改？"
                            (file-name-nondirectory file)))
      (custom/git--run "restore" "--source=HEAD" "--staged" "--worktree" "--" file)
      (revert-buffer t t t)
      (message "已丢弃: %s" (file-name-nondirectory file)))))

(defun custom/git-timemachine-toggle ()
  "切换当前文件的 git-timemachine。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git-buffer-file)
  (call-interactively #'git-timemachine-toggle))

(defun custom/git-push-current-repo ()
  "Push 当前仓库。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git--ensure-repo)
  (call-interactively #'magit-push-current-to-upstream))

(defun custom/git-pull-current-repo ()
  "Pull 当前仓库。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git--ensure-repo)
  (call-interactively #'magit-pull-from-upstream))

(defun custom/git-stash-push ()
  "Stash 当前仓库中的修改。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git--ensure-repo)
  (custom/git--run "stash" "push" "-m"
                   (format-time-string "stash-%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"))
  (message "已创建 stash"))

(defun custom/git-stash-pop ()
  "恢复最近一次 stash。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/git--ensure-repo)
  (when (y-or-n-p "恢复最近一次 stash？")
    (custom/git--run "stash" "pop")
    (message "已恢复最近一次 stash")))

(defun custom/git-commit-setup-buffer-maybe ()
  "当前 buffer 是 Git 提交消息文件时加载并启用 `git-commit-mode'。"
  (when (and buffer-file-name
             (member (file-name-nondirectory buffer-file-name)
                     '("COMMIT_EDITMSG" "MERGE_MSG" "TAG_EDITMSG"
                       "NOTES_EDITMSG" "PULLREQ_EDITMSG")))
    (require 'git-commit)
    (git-commit-setup-check-buffer)))
#+end_src

*** Display-buffer 路由

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/git-display :tangle no
(cl-defun custom/register-side-window (regex side size &key slot width)
  "把 REGEX 匹配的 buffer 路由到 SIDE 边的 side-window，占用 SIZE 比例。

REGEX : buffer-name 匹配字符串正则
SIDE  : bottom / right / left / top（符号）
SIZE  : 0~1 浮点比例
SLOT  : 默认 0（同 side 多窗口的堆叠顺序）
WIDTH : 非 nil 强制按 window-width；nil（默认）时按 SIDE 自动推导
        （left/right → window-width，top/bottom → window-height）。

规则追加到 `display-buffer-alist' 末尾。顺序敏感：更具体的规则应先注册。"
  (let* ((slot (or slot 0))
         (use-width (or width (memq side '(left right))))
         (window-dim (if use-width 'window-width 'window-height)))
    (add-to-list 'display-buffer-alist
                 `(,regex
                   (display-buffer-in-side-window)
                   (side . ,side)
                   (slot . ,slot)
                   (,window-dim . ,size))
                 t)))

(defun custom/display-buffer-in-main-window (buffer &optional _alist)
  "把 BUFFER 显示在「主编辑窗口」并选中。
主编辑窗口 = 当前 frame 内最靠上（y 坐标最小）的非 side-window 窗口。
保证无论光标当前在哪个窗口（如底部 magit），COMMIT_EDITMSG
都稳定落到上方编辑窗口，与「上编辑 + 下 magit」布局一致。

若无候选（如 frame 只剩 side-window），退化为 `display-buffer-pop-up-window'。

同时用作 `display-buffer-alist' 的 action 函数（参数 ALIST 被忽略）和
`server-window' 的值（server.el 调用时只传 buffer 一个参数）。"
  (let* ((frame (selected-frame))
         (candidates
          (cl-remove-if
           (lambda (w) (window-parameter w 'window-side))
           (window-list frame 'no-minibuf (frame-first-window frame))))
         (target (car (sort candidates
                            (lambda (a b)
                              (let ((ya (nth 1 (window-edges a nil t t)))
                                    (yb (nth 1 (window-edges b nil t t))))
                                (or (< ya yb)
                                    (and (= ya yb)
                                         (< (nth 0 (window-edges a nil t t))
                                            (nth 0 (window-edges b nil t t)))))))))))
    (if (window-live-p target)
        (progn
          (set-window-buffer target buffer)
          (select-window target)
          target)
      (display-buffer buffer '(display-buffer-pop-up-window)))))

(defun custom/display-or-focus (buffer action &optional select)
  "有则聚焦，无则按 ACTION 创建。
BUFFER 已在某窗口显示时，聚焦该窗口（SELECT 非 nil）；否则用 ACTION
（display-buffer 动作列表）创建新窗口。临时绑定 display-buffer-alist 为 nil，
避免递归匹配 display-buffer-alist 中的规则。"
  (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
    (if-let* ((win (get-buffer-window buffer)))
        (when select (select-window win))
      (let ((display-buffer-alist nil))
        (display-buffer buffer action)))))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<appearance/git-commands>>
<<appearance/git-display>>
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(custom/bind "C-c g s" #'custom/git-status-dwim "版本控制状态" "Git")
(custom/bind "C-c g b" #'custom/git-blame-current-file "当前文件追责" "Git")
(custom/bind "C-c g l" #'custom/git-log-current-file "当前文件日志" "Git")
(custom/bind "C-c g d" #'custom/git-diff-current-file "当前文件差异" "Git")
(custom/bind "C-c g t" #'custom/git-timemachine-toggle "文件时光机" "Git")
(custom/bind "C-c g S" #'custom/git-stage-current-file "暂存当前文件" "Git")

(custom/bind "C-c g @" #'custom/git-stash-pop "恢复暂存" "Git")
#+end_src

** 主题与字体
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: theme-fonts
:END:

背景透明度、字体高度、行号、括号匹配、鼠标支持、ef-themes、spacious-padding、ultra-scroll、pulsar。

+ *主题* : =ef-themes= 通过本域 [[#color-scheme][颜色方案]] 跟随 Darkman 自动切换深浅色。GUI 与真彩 TTY 共用主题前景 palette；TTY 仅清除背景,详见 [[#mixed-gui-tty-constraints][GUI/TTY 混合 daemon 约束]]
+ *字体大小* :110(11pt),由 =custom:default-font-height= 控制
+ *行号* :相对行号 + 自适应宽度
+ 所有 ~display-dependent~ 设置都通过 =custom/apply-frame-appearance= 在新 frame 创建时触发

*** GUI/TTY 混合 daemon 约束
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: mixed-gui-tty-constraints
:END:

daemon 启动期 =(display-graphic-p)= 恒为 nil,所有依赖 display 类型的 frame 参数仍必须 per-frame。Emacs theme 是全局状态；daemon 启动时预载 Ef theme，真彩 TTY 与 GUI 共用当前 Ef 明暗主题的前景 palette。TTY client 创建后只清除所有命名 face 的背景与反色属性，再用粗体、下划线等非颜色属性强化选区、搜索、补全等交互状态。

#+attr_org: :width 75
| 设置/包          | daemon 启动期陷阱             | TTY 行为                | 处理方式                            |
|------------------+-------------------------------+-------------------------+-------------------------------------|
| 光标类型         | #ERROR                        | 后创 TTY frame 污染 GUI | 用 =set-frame-parameter 'cursor-type= |
| spacious-padding | =(display-graphic-p)= 永远 nil  | no-op                   | 无条件启用                          |
| ultra-scroll     | 同上                          | 无效果                  | 无条件启用(GUI client 需要)         |
| diff-hl(TTY)     | 无 fringe,fringe 指示器不可见 | 改用 margin             | =fboundp= 守卫 + TTY 分支按需启用     |
| fringe 宽度      | 行号函数若清零 fringe         | —                       | 禁止在行号函数清零 fringe(详见下表) |
| ef-themes        | =(display-graphic-p)= 永远 nil | daemon/真彩 TTY 共用 palette | daemon 预载；standalone 真彩 TTY 也加载主题 |
| TTY 配色         | 无 alpha 通道                 | 主题前景 + 全 face 透明 | 清背景与反色，不覆盖主题前景 |
| Nerd Font 图标   | =(display-graphic-p)= 永远 nil | 字形以文字渲染          | 守卫改为 =custom:modeline--nerd-icons-available=,不查 display |
#+TBLFM: $2=setq-default= 是全局副作用

TTY 背景透明由 =custom/apply-terminal-faces= 实现:daemon 中 TTY 会继承全局 Ef theme，延迟加载的包也会继续定义带背景的 face，故不能维护有限清单。该函数恢复 TTY frame 的透明背景参数，对全部命名 face 统一设置 =:background unspecified= 与 =:inverse-video nil=，确保 fringe 与行号 gutter 不绘制主题底色；随后仅为 =hl-line= 恢复 Ef palette 的 =bg-dim= 背景，选区、搜索、补全等状态仍用粗体和下划线区分。Foot 的 =alpha-mode=all= 让这块独立 RGB 背景同样应用终端 =alpha=0.88=，因此当前行是半透明色块而非实色或横线。主题启用和 Lisp 文件加载后自动重施，使主题修改的 frame 参数与后出现的 face 都遵守透明策略。=early-init.el= 与 =window-system-default-frame-alist= 负责在 main.el 之前注入初始终端参数，避免启动闪屏。该策略只在真实 TTY 会话(非 batch、≥8 色)下生效,batch/GUI 为 no-op。

行号设置函数 =custom/setup-file-line-numbers= 故意不调 =(set-window-fringes ... 0)= ——=diff-hl= 和 =flymake= 的 fringe 指示器(=flymake-fringe-indicator-position= 设为 =left-fringe=)依赖 fringe 宽度,清零会让它们全部消失。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defcustom custom:frame-background-opacity 88
  "GUI frame 背景透明度(0~100,100 = 不透明)。"
  :type 'integer
  :group 'faces)

(defcustom custom:default-font-height 110
  "默认字体大小(1/10 pt)。"
  :type 'integer
  :group 'faces)

(defconst custom:terminal-frame-parameters
  '((background-color . "unspecified-bg")
    (foreground-color . "unspecified-fg")
    (menu-bar-lines . 0)
    (tool-bar-lines . 0)
    (vertical-scroll-bars . nil)
    (horizontal-scroll-bars . nil))
  "TTY frame 在 face 初始化前应用的无色显示参数。")

(defun custom/install-terminal-frame-defaults ()
  "把透明 TTY 参数合并进 `window-system-default-frame-alist'。"
  (if-let* ((entry (assq t window-system-default-frame-alist)))
      (dolist (parameter custom:terminal-frame-parameters)
        (setf (alist-get (car parameter) (cdr entry)) (cdr parameter)))
    (push (cons t (copy-tree custom:terminal-frame-parameters))
          window-system-default-frame-alist)))

(custom/install-terminal-frame-defaults)

(dolist (param '((menu-bar-lines . 0)
                 (tool-bar-lines . 0)
                 (vertical-scroll-bars)
                 (horizontal-scroll-bars)))
  (unless (assq (car param) default-frame-alist)
    (push param default-frame-alist)))

(setq display-line-numbers-type 'relative)
(global-display-line-numbers-mode 1)

(defcustom custom:large-file-size-threshold (* 2 1024 1024)
  "关闭高成本显示功能的文件大小阈值（字节）。"
  :type 'integer
  :group 'literal)

(defun custom/setup-file-line-numbers ()
  "为当前文件设置行号；大型文件直接关闭，避免扫描完整 buffer。"
  (if (> (buffer-size) custom:large-file-size-threshold)
      (display-line-numbers-mode -1)
    (when (and (boundp 'display-line-numbers-width)
               buffer-file-name
               display-line-numbers)
      (let* ((max-line (line-number-at-pos (point-max) 'absolute))
             (digits (max 3 (length (number-to-string max-line)))))
        (setq display-line-numbers-width (1+ digits))))))

(add-hook 'find-file-hook #'custom/setup-file-line-numbers)

(dolist (mode '(ghostel-mode term-mode eshell-mode shell-mode
                             dashboard-mode special-mode
                             agent-shell-mode))
  (add-hook (intern (concat (symbol-name mode) "-hook"))
            (lambda () (display-line-numbers-mode -1))))

(xterm-mouse-mode 1)
(context-menu-mode 1)
(global-hl-line-mode 1)

(defconst custom:terminal-face-emphasis
  '((highlight :underline t)
    (region :underline t :extend nil)
    (secondary-selection :underline t)
    (match :underline t)
    (show-paren-match :weight bold :underline t)
    (show-paren-mismatch :weight bold :underline t)
    (lazy-highlight :underline t)
    (isearch :weight bold :underline t)
    (isearch-fail :weight bold :underline t)
    (line-number-current-line :weight bold)
    (mode-line :box nil :overline t)
    (mode-line-inactive :box nil)
    (tab-line :height 0.95 :box nil)
    (tab-line-tab-current :underline t :weight semi-bold :box nil)
    (tab-line-tab-inactive :box nil)
    (header-line :box nil :underline t)
    (completions-highlight :weight bold :underline t)
    (vertico-current :weight bold :underline t)
    (corfu-current :weight bold :underline t))
  "TTY 透明背景下补充的非颜色交互语义。")

(defun custom/terminal-theme-background ()
  "返回 TTY 当前行使用的 Ef theme 柔和背景色。
背景取当前 palette 的 `bg-dim'；若主题 API 尚不可用则返回 nil，保持全透明。"
  (when (fboundp 'ef-themes-get-color-value)
    (condition-case nil
        (ef-themes-get-color-value 'bg-dim)
      (error nil))))

(defun custom/apply-terminal-faces (&optional frame)
  "让 TTY FRAME 的所有命名 face 使用终端透明背景。
清除背景与反色后，为 `hl-line' 恢复 Ef theme 的柔和背景，并用
`custom:terminal-face-emphasis' 补充其余非颜色交互语义；所有前景色继续由
当前 Ef theme 提供。Foot 需使用 alpha-mode=all 才能让 hl-line 背景半透明。
仅在真实 TTY（非 batch、至少 8 色）下生效；GUI 为 no-op。"
  (condition-case err
      (when (and (not noninteractive)
                 (not (display-graphic-p frame))
                 (>= (display-color-cells frame) 8))
        (let ((tty (or frame (selected-frame)))
              (hl-line-background (custom/terminal-theme-background)))
          (with-selected-frame tty
            (dolist (parameter custom:terminal-frame-parameters)
              ;; Ef theme owns foreground-color after startup; only the initial
              ;; frame defaults use unspecified-fg to avoid a pre-theme flash.
              (unless (eq (car parameter) 'foreground-color)
                (set-frame-parameter tty (car parameter) (cdr parameter))))
            (dolist (face (face-list))
              (set-face-attribute face tty
                                  :background 'unspecified
                                  :inverse-video nil))
            (dolist (entry custom:terminal-face-emphasis)
              (when (facep (car entry))
                (apply #'set-face-attribute
                       (car entry) tty (cdr entry))))
            (when (and hl-line-background (facep 'hl-line))
              (set-face-attribute 'hl-line tty
                                  :background hl-line-background
                                  :underline nil
                                  :extend t)))))
    (error
     (message "custom/apply-terminal-faces: %s" (error-message-string err)))))

(defun custom/refresh-terminal-faces (&rest _)
  "重新对所有存活 TTY frame 应用透明背景策略。"
  (unless noninteractive
    (dolist (frame (frame-list))
      (when (and (frame-live-p frame)
                 (not (display-graphic-p frame)))
        (custom/apply-terminal-faces frame)))))

(defun custom/refresh-terminal-faces-after-load (_file)
  "包加载并可能定义新 face 后刷新所有 TTY frame。"
  (custom/refresh-terminal-faces))

(add-hook 'enable-theme-functions #'custom/refresh-terminal-faces)
(add-hook 'after-load-functions #'custom/refresh-terminal-faces-after-load)

(defun custom/apply-frame-appearance (&optional frame)
  "为 FRAME 应用所有 display-dependent 外观设置。
+ GUI: 字体高度、透明度、window-divider、tab-line face 重算
+ TTY: 背景透明（清遮挡终端背景的 face 背景）、diff-hl margin 模式。
  真彩 TTY 的前景色继续使用当前 Ef theme palette。"
  (condition-case err
      (with-selected-frame (or frame (selected-frame))
        (let ((graphic-p (display-graphic-p)))
          (set-frame-parameter nil 'cursor-type (if graphic-p 'bar 'box))
          (if graphic-p
              (progn
                (set-face-attribute 'default nil :height custom:default-font-height)
                (set-frame-parameter nil 'alpha-background custom:frame-background-opacity)
                (when (fboundp 'custom/apply-tab-line-button-preset)
                  (custom/apply-tab-line-button-preset))
                (when (fboundp 'ef-themes-take-over-modus-themes-mode)
                  (ef-themes-take-over-modus-themes-mode 1)))
            (progn
              (window-divider-mode -1)
              (custom/apply-terminal-faces)
              (when (fboundp 'diff-hl-margin-mode)
                (diff-hl-margin-mode 1))))
          (force-mode-line-update t)))
    (error
     (message "custom/apply-frame-appearance: %s" (error-message-string err)))))

(custom/add-frame-created-hook #'custom/apply-frame-appearance)

(use-package ef-themes
  :defer t
  :bind
  (("<f6>" . ef-themes-rotate)
   ("C-<f6>" . ef-themes-select)
   ("M-<f6>" . ef-themes-load-random))
  :custom
  (ef-themes-mixed-fonts t)
  (ef-themes-italic-constructs t)
  :config
  (when (fboundp 'custom/apply-tab-line-button-preset)
    (add-hook 'ef-themes-after-load-theme-hook
              #'custom/apply-tab-line-button-preset)))

(use-package spacious-padding
  :demand t
  :custom
  (spacious-padding-widths
   '(:internal-border-width 8
                            :header-line-width 4
                            :mode-line-width 1
                            :tab-width 4
                            :right-divider-width 1
                            :scroll-bar-width 8))
  :config
  ;; spacious-padding 作为自定义主题启用；`enable-theme-functions' 会自动
  ;; 恢复所有 TTY face 的透明背景策略。
  (spacious-padding-mode 1))

(use-package ultra-scroll
  :demand t
  :init
  (setq scroll-conservatively 101
        scroll-margin 0
        pixel-scroll-precision-interpolate-page t)
  :config
  (ultra-scroll-mode 1))

(use-package pulsar
  :demand t
  :custom
  (pulsar-pulse t)
  (pulsar-delay 0.045)
  (pulsar-iterations 8)
  (pulsar-face 'pulsar-cyan)
  :config
  (setq pulsar-pulse-functions
        '(recenter-top-bottom
          reposition-window
          other-window
          windmove-left windmove-right windmove-up windmove-down
          consult-line consult-ripgrep consult-buffer
          xref-find-definitions xref-find-references
          xref-go-back xref-go-forward
          custom/code-jump-back custom/code-jump-forward
          flymake-goto-next-error flymake-goto-prev-error))
  (pulsar-global-mode 1))

(size-indication-mode 1)
(display-battery-mode 1)
#+end_src

(custom/register-side-window "^\\*Flymake diagnostics\\|flymake-diagnostics-buffer-mode\\b" 'bottom 0.3)
(custom/register-side-window "^\\*compilation\\|compilation-mode\\b" 'bottom 0.33)
