** 右键菜单与 Which-key 中文化
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: which-key-ui
:END:

右键菜单翻译 + which-key 中文描述注册。

+ *context-menu* : 对 =context-menu-map= 做 advice,翻译右键菜单英文项(数据见 =data/context-menu-zh.el=)
+ *which-key 数据* : 集中维护 C-x / C-c / M-s / M-g 等前缀子树的中文描述,数据见 =data/which-key-zh.el=
  - 全局前缀 + 叶子命令: =custom:which-key-description-spec=
  - major mode 局部前缀: =custom:which-key-major-mode-description-spec=
  - 通用 key/description 替换: =custom:which-key-regexp-replacements=
  - =(KEY . DESC)= 表示叶子命令; =(KEY DESC CHILD...)= 表示前缀组,自动展开为完整键序列
+ which-key 本体在 *键位与补全* 域用 use-package 加载;本块仅注册描述,=with-eval-after-load 'which-key= 触发

#+begin_src emacs-lisp

(defun custom/which-key--join-key (prefix key)
  "将 PREFIX 与 KEY 合并为 which-key 可用的键序列字符串。"
  (if (and prefix (> (length prefix) 0))
      (concat prefix " " key)
    key))

(defun custom/which-key--flatten-spec (prefix spec)
  "将 PREFIX 下的单个 SPEC 展开为 ((key . desc) ...) 列表。"
  (pcase spec
    (`(,key ,desc . ,children)
     (let ((full-key (custom/which-key--join-key prefix key)))
       (append (list (cons full-key desc))
               (custom/which-key--flatten-specs full-key children))))
    (`(,key . ,desc)
     (list (cons (custom/which-key--join-key prefix key) desc)))
    (_
     (error "无法识别的 which-key 描述项: %S" spec))))

(defun custom/which-key--flatten-specs (prefix specs)
  "将 PREFIX 下的 SPECS 展开为平铺的 ((key . desc) ...) 列表。"
  (apply #'append
         (mapcar (lambda (spec)
                   (custom/which-key--flatten-spec prefix spec))
                 specs)))

(defconst custom:which-key-replacements
  (custom/which-key--flatten-specs nil custom:which-key-description-spec)
  "Which-key 全局中文描述映射表。")

(defvar custom:which-key-descriptions-applied nil
  "避免重复写入 `which-key-replacement-alist'。")

(defun custom/which-key-apply-descriptions ()
  "将中文描述注册到 `which-key'。"
  (when (and (featurep 'which-key)
             (not custom:which-key-descriptions-applied))
    (dolist (entry custom:which-key-regexp-replacements)
      (setq which-key-replacement-alist
            (append which-key-replacement-alist (list entry))))
    (dolist (entry custom:which-key-replacements)
      (which-key-add-key-based-replacements (car entry) (cdr entry)))
    (dolist (mode-entry custom:which-key-major-mode-description-spec)
      (let ((mode (car mode-entry))
            (specs (cdr mode-entry)))
        (dolist (entry (custom/which-key--flatten-specs nil specs))
          (which-key-add-major-mode-key-based-replacements
            mode (car entry) (cdr entry)))))
    (setq custom:which-key-descriptions-applied t)))

(with-eval-after-load 'which-key
  ;; 先注册 custom/bind 收集的延迟描述
  (custom/register-pending-wk-descs)
  ;; 再注册集中维护的描述
  (custom/which-key-apply-descriptions))
#+end_src

** 颜色方案(ef-themes)
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: color-scheme
:END:

ef-themes 通过 D-Bus 跟随 Darkman 自动切换深浅色。daemon 启动时即预载当前主题并注册监听器，让 GUI 与 TTY client 共用一致的前景 palette；真彩 standalone TTY 也加载当前主题。TTY frame 随后由 [[#mixed-gui-tty-constraints][=custom/apply-terminal-faces=]] 仅清除背景与反色属性。

+ =custom/color-scheme-init= 注册 D-Bus 监听器,深浅色变化时自动 =ef-themes-select= 对应主题
+ 通过 [[#frame][Frame 生命周期]] 在新 frame 创建时应用当前色彩状态
+ 导出 =custom/register-buffer-refresh!= / =custom/register-theme-refresh!= 供 Dashboard 使用

*** 状态与主题应用

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/color-scheme-core :tangle no

(require 'dbus)

(defcustom custom/color-scheme-light-theme 'ef-cyprus
  "浅色模式下加载的主题。"
  :type 'symbol
  :group 'appearance)

(defcustom custom/color-scheme-dark-theme 'ef-owl
  "深色模式下加载的主题。"
  :type 'symbol
  :group 'appearance)

(defconst custom/color-scheme-state-file
  (expand-file-name "var/color-scheme-state.el" user-emacs-directory)
  "颜色方案状态文件路径。")

(defvar custom/color-scheme-current-mode nil
  "当前颜色方案模式（'light 或 'dark）。")

(defvar custom/color-scheme--initialized nil
  "颜色方案模块是否已完成初始化。")

(defvar custom/color-scheme--applying-theme nil
  "是否正在执行主题切换，避免 frame hook 重入。")

(defun custom/color-scheme-read-state ()
  "从状态文件读取当前模式。返回 'light 或 'dark，文件不存在或读取失败时返回 nil。"
  (condition-case err
      (when (file-exists-p custom/color-scheme-state-file)
        (let ((content (with-temp-buffer
                         (insert-file-contents custom/color-scheme-state-file)
                         (buffer-string))))
          (cond
           ((string-match-p "dark" content) 'dark)
           ((string-match-p "light" content) 'light)
           (t nil))))
    (error
     (message "[color-scheme] 无法读取状态文件: %s" (error-message-string err))
     nil)))

(defun custom/color-scheme-save-state (mode)
  "将当前模式保存到状态文件。MODE 为 'light 或 'dark。"
  (condition-case err
      (let ((dir (file-name-directory custom/color-scheme-state-file)))
        (unless (file-exists-p dir)
          (make-directory dir t))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (insert ";; color-scheme-state.el -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-\n")
          (insert ";; 自动生成，勿手动修改\n")
          (insert (format "(setq custom/color-scheme-current-mode '%s)\n" mode))
          (write-region (point-min) (point-max) custom/color-scheme-state-file)))
    (error
     (message "[color-scheme] 无法保存状态文件: %s" (error-message-string err)))))

;; D-Bus 接口常量

(defconst custom/color-scheme-dbus-service "nl.whynothugo.darkman"
  "Darkman D-Bus 服务名称。")

(defconst custom/color-scheme-dbus-path "/nl/whynothugo/darkman"
  "Darkman D-Bus 对象路径。")

(defconst custom/color-scheme-dbus-interface "nl.whynothugo.darkman"
  "Darkman D-Bus 接口。")

;; 核心函数

(defun custom/color-scheme-get-from-dbus ()
  "通过 D-Bus 获取当前 Darkman 模式。返回 'light、'dark 或 nil。"
  (condition-case err
      (when (featurep 'dbus)
        (let ((mode (dbus-get-property
                     :session
                     custom/color-scheme-dbus-service
                     custom/color-scheme-dbus-path
                     custom/color-scheme-dbus-interface
                     "Mode")))
          (cond
           ((string= mode "light") 'light)
           ((string= mode "dark") 'dark)
           (t nil))))
    (error
     (message "[color-scheme] D-Bus 获取失败: %s" (error-message-string err))
     nil)))

(defun custom/color-scheme-apply-theme (mode)
  "根据 MODE 加载对应的主题并保存状态。
MODE 为 'light 或 'dark。
独立实例在 GUI 或至少 256 色的 TTY frame 中加载；daemon 无 frame 时也预载主题。"
  (setq custom/color-scheme-current-mode mode)
  (custom/color-scheme-save-state mode)
  (unless custom/color-scheme--applying-theme
    (let ((custom/color-scheme--applying-theme t)
          (target-theme (pcase mode
                          ('dark custom/color-scheme-dark-theme)
                          ('light custom/color-scheme-light-theme)
                          (_ nil))))
      (when target-theme
        (when (or (daemonp)
                  (custom/display-supports-themes-p))
          (unless (eq (car custom-enabled-themes) target-theme)
            (ef-themes-load-theme target-theme))
          (message "[color-scheme] 已同步主题: %s" target-theme))))))

#+end_src

*** D-Bus 监听

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/color-scheme-dbus :tangle no
;; D-Bus 信号监听

(defvar custom/color-scheme-dbus-handler nil
  "D-Bus 信号监听器的注册 ID。")

(defun custom/color-scheme-register-listener ()
  "注册 D-Bus 信号监听器，响应 Darkman 模式变化。"
  (condition-case err
      (when (and (featurep 'dbus)
                 (not custom/color-scheme-dbus-handler))
        (setq custom/color-scheme-dbus-handler
              (dbus-register-signal
               :session
               custom/color-scheme-dbus-service
               custom/color-scheme-dbus-path
               custom/color-scheme-dbus-interface
               "ModeChanged"
               (lambda (mode)
                 (let ((new-mode (cond
                                  ((string= mode "light") 'light)
                                  ((string= mode "dark") 'dark))))
                   (when (and new-mode
                              (not (eq new-mode custom/color-scheme-current-mode)))
                     (message "[color-scheme] Darkman 模式变化: %s" mode)
                     (custom/color-scheme-apply-theme new-mode))))))
        (message "[color-scheme] 已注册 D-Bus 监听器"))
    (error
     (message "[color-scheme] 无法注册 D-Bus 监听器: %s" (error-message-string err))
     nil)))

#+end_src

*** Frame 初始化

主题初始化时机按运行模式分流:

#+attr_org: :width 50
| 运行模式   | 触发时机            | 原因                            |
|------------+---------------------+---------------------------------|
| daemon     | daemon 启动阶段 | 启动时间不敏感，提前支付主题加载与 D-Bus 注册成本 |
| 独立 Emacs | 立即初始化       | 启动时 selected-frame 已存在                      |

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/color-scheme-init :tangle no
(defun custom/display-supports-themes-p (&optional frame)
  "FRAME（默认 selected-frame）是否可呈现 Ef theme 的完整前景 palette。"
  (with-selected-frame (or frame (selected-frame))
    (or (display-graphic-p)
        (and (not noninteractive)
             (>= (display-color-cells) 256)))))

(defun custom/color-scheme-init ()
  "初始化颜色方案适配：
1. 先从状态文件读取模式，立即应用主题
2. 异步验证 D-Bus，不一致则更新
3. 注册 D-Bus 监听器"
  (let ((initial-mode (or (custom/color-scheme-read-state)
                          (custom/color-scheme-get-from-dbus)
                          'dark)))
    (custom/color-scheme-apply-theme initial-mode)
    (setq custom/color-scheme--initialized t)
    (custom/color-scheme-register-listener)
    (run-with-idle-timer 1 nil
                         (lambda ()
                           (let ((dbus-mode (custom/color-scheme-get-from-dbus)))
                             (when (and dbus-mode
                                        (not (eq dbus-mode custom/color-scheme-current-mode)))
                               (message "[color-scheme] 状态文件与 D-Bus 不一致，更新为 %s" dbus-mode)
                               (custom/color-scheme-apply-theme dbus-mode)))))))

(defun custom/color-scheme-apply-for-frame (&optional frame)
  "在 FRAME 可用时补齐与主题相关的 frame 初始化。
主题是全局状态，不能在每个新 frame 上重复 `load-theme`，否则多 client
几乎同时创建 frame 时容易在 PGTK/GTK 初始化路径中崩溃。"
  (with-selected-frame (or frame (selected-frame))
    (when (custom/display-supports-themes-p)
      (when (fboundp 'ef-themes-take-over-modus-themes-mode)
        (ef-themes-take-over-modus-themes-mode 1))
      (if (not custom/color-scheme--initialized)
          (custom/color-scheme-init)
        (let ((target-theme (pcase custom/color-scheme-current-mode
                              ('dark custom/color-scheme-dark-theme)
                              ('light custom/color-scheme-light-theme)
                              (_ nil))))
          (when (and target-theme
                     (not (eq (car custom-enabled-themes) target-theme)))
            (ef-themes-load-theme target-theme)
            (message "[color-scheme] 新 frame 同步主题: %s" target-theme)))))))

(require 'ef-themes)
(custom/color-scheme-init)
(custom/add-frame-created-hook #'custom/color-scheme-apply-for-frame)
#+end_src

*** Face 与缓冲区刷新

本配置只拥有 Ef 主题;颜色通过 face 继承或 Ef palette API 获取,不在刷新函数里硬编码 RGB,也不依赖 Modus palette。主题切换后的级联刷新通过 =custom/theme-refresh-functions= 列表(各功能模块用 =custom/register-theme-refresh!= 幂等注册)统一驱动,见 [[#dashboard][Dashboard]] 的边框 bar face 更新。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref appearance/color-scheme-refresh :tangle no
(defun custom/color-scheme-apply-custom-face-colors ()
  "主题切换后重新应用自定义 face 颜色。
dashboard 卡片已改用边框风格(左竖线 + 标题分隔线,见 `dashboard-card-bar'),
不再涂整行背景,故主题切换无需同步卡片背景色。"
  (when (facep 'custom-help-section-title)
    (set-face-attribute 'custom-help-section-title nil
                        :inherit 'font-lock-keyword-face)))

(defvar custom/theme-refresh-functions nil
  "主题切换后需要调用的刷新函数列表。")

(defun custom/register-theme-refresh! (fn)
  "注册 FN 为主题切换后调用的刷新函数（幂等）。"
  (unless (memq fn custom/theme-refresh-functions)
    (setq custom/theme-refresh-functions
          (append custom/theme-refresh-functions (list fn)))))

(defun custom/run-theme-refresh ()
  "执行所有已注册的主题切换刷新函数。"
  (dolist (fn custom/theme-refresh-functions)
    (condition-case err
        (funcall fn)
      (error
       (message "[color-scheme] theme-refresh %S failed: %S"
                fn (error-message-string err))))))

(defvar custom/theme-buffer-refreshers nil
  "Alist of (MAJOR-MODE . REFRESH-FN) pairs。")

(defun custom/register-buffer-refresh! (mode refresh-fn)
  "为 MAJOR-MODE 注册主题切换后的 buffer 重渲染函数 REFRESH-FN。幂等。"
  (let ((entry (assq mode custom/theme-buffer-refreshers)))
    (unless (and entry (eq (cdr entry) refresh-fn))
      (if entry
          (setcdr entry refresh-fn)
        (push (cons mode refresh-fn) custom/theme-buffer-refreshers)))))

(defun custom/color-scheme-refresh-registered-buffers ()
  "重新渲染所有可见窗口中已注册 mode 的缓冲区。"
  (dolist (frame (frame-list))
    (when (frame-live-p frame)
      (dolist (window (window-list frame 'no-minibuf))
        (when (window-live-p window)
          (let ((buffer (window-buffer window)))
            (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
              (with-current-buffer buffer
                (let ((refresher
                       (cdr (assq major-mode custom/theme-buffer-refreshers))))
                  (when (and refresher (functionp refresher))
                    (condition-case err
                        (with-selected-window window
                          (funcall refresher))
                      (error
                       (message "[color-scheme] buffer-refresh %s failed: %S"
                                major-mode
                                (error-message-string err))))))))))))))

(custom/register-theme-refresh! #'custom/color-scheme-apply-custom-face-colors)
(custom/register-theme-refresh! #'custom/color-scheme-refresh-registered-buffers)
(custom/register-theme-refresh! #'custom/refresh-terminal-faces)
(add-hook 'ef-themes-after-load-theme-hook #'custom/run-theme-refresh)
(custom/run-theme-refresh)
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<appearance/color-scheme-core>>
<<appearance/color-scheme-dbus>>
<<appearance/color-scheme-init>>
<<appearance/color-scheme-refresh>>
#+end_src

* 编辑与文本
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: editing
:END:

通用编辑习惯、DWIM 智能编辑函数、ghostel 终端集成。

** 通用编辑习惯
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: editing-defaults
:END:

缩进 / tab、括号匹配、EditorConfig、选区扩展、Git 差异高亮、Smartparens、文档模式自动换行。

+ ~indent-tabs-mode nil~ + ~tab-width 2~ :默认用空格缩进
+ ~show-paren-mode~ :高亮匹配括号
+ ~save-place-mode~ / ~repeat-mode~ :内置体验增强
+ ~~editorconfig~ :遵循项目 =.editorconfig=
+ ~smartparens~ :替代 ~electric-pair-mode~ ,支持 wrap/unwrap/slurp/barf
+ ~diff-hl~ :fringe 显示 Git 差异(终端用 margin)
+ 文档模式(org/markdown/text)自动 =visual-line-mode=
+ =global-so-long-mode= 自动降级超长行 buffer；大于阈值的文件关闭行号并跳过全 buffer 行数扫描

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(setq-default indent-tabs-mode nil
              tab-width 2
              fill-column 100
              tab-always-indent nil
              truncate-lines t
              word-wrap nil)

(global-so-long-mode 1)

(show-paren-mode 1)
(save-place-mode 1)
(repeat-mode 1)
(when (boundp 'show-paren-context-when-offscreen)
  (setq show-paren-context-when-offscreen 'child-frame))

(use-package editorconfig
  :demand t
  :custom
  (editorconfig-trim-whitespaces-mode
   (if (fboundp 'delete-trailing-whitespace-mode)
       'delete-trailing-whitespace-mode
     'ws-butler-mode))
  :config
  (editorconfig-mode 1))

(use-package expand-region
  :defer t
  :commands (er/expand-region er/contract-region))

;; 行尾空格清理:Emacs 31+ 用内置 delete-trailing-whitespace-mode,29-30 fallback ws-butler。
(if (fboundp 'delete-trailing-whitespace-mode)
    (add-hook 'text-mode-hook #'delete-trailing-whitespace-mode)
  (use-package ws-butler
    :defer t
    :hook ((text-mode . ws-butler-mode)
           (prog-mode . ws-butler-mode))))

;; diff-hl 的 margin 模式只在 TTY 启用(GUI 走 fringe):原先 daemon 启动期
;; (unless (display-graphic-p) ...) 判断永远为 nil,会强启 GUI client 的 margin
;; 模式(fringe 指示器被 margin 取代,且 GUI 下 margin 有显示问题)。margin 由
;; [[#mixed-gui-tty-constraints][GUI/TTY 约束]] 的 frame hook TTY 分支按需启用。
(use-package diff-hl
  :defer t
  :hook ((prog-mode . diff-hl-mode)
         (text-mode . diff-hl-mode))
  :config
  (global-diff-hl-mode 1))

(use-package smartparens
  :hook ((prog-mode . smartparens-mode)
         (markdown-mode . smartparens-mode)
         (org-mode . smartparens-mode))
  :config
  (require 'smartparens-config))

(use-package crux
  :defer t
  :commands (crux-open-with
             crux-rename-file-and-buffer
             crux-delete-file-and-buffer
             crux-copy-file-preserve-attributes
             crux-duplicate-current-line-or-region))

;; git-messenger 用 C-c g m(非 b):C-c g b 已被 custom/git-blame-current-file
;; 占用,而 :bind 是 git-messenger 唯一的加载触发器,键被覆盖会让整个包不可用。
(use-package git-messenger
  :defer t
  :bind ("C-c g m" . git-messenger:popup-message)
  :custom
  (git-messenger:show-detail t)
  (git-messenger:use-magit-popup t))

(dolist (hook '(org-mode-hook markdown-mode-hook gfm-mode-hook text-mode-hook))
  (add-hook hook #'visual-line-mode))
#+end_src

** DWIM 智能编辑函数
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: editing-dwim
:END:

智能复制 / 剪切 / 注释 / 重命名等 DWIM 函数,以及前向声明。

+ =custom/copy-dwim= :有选区复制选区,否则复制当前行(类 VS Code)
+ =delete-selection-mode= :有选区时输入/粘贴自动覆盖选区(替代旧 =custom/paste-dwim= ,内置且覆盖所有插入类命令,不止 yank)
+ =custom/cut-dwim= :有选区剪切选区,否则剪切整行
+ =custom/comment-dwim-stable= :稳定执行注释(region 优先)
+ =custom/rename-dwim= :eglot 管理时调 eglot-rename,否则 rename-visited-file
+ 全选用内置 =mark-whole-buffer= (C-a),关闭窗口+buffer 用内置 =kill-buffer-and-window= (C-x k)

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun custom/copy-dwim ()
  "智能复制:有选区时复制选区,否则复制当前行。"
  (interactive)
  (cond
   ((use-region-p)
    (kill-ring-save (region-beginning) (region-end))
    (deactivate-mark)
    (message "已复制选区"))
   (t
    (kill-ring-save (line-beginning-position) (line-beginning-position 2))
    (message "已复制当前行"))))

;; 有选区时输入/粘贴自动覆盖选区(类 VS Code):delete-selection-mode 覆盖所有
;; 插入类命令(不止 yank),替代旧 custom/paste-dwim,C-S-v 直接绑 yank。
(delete-selection-mode 1)

(defun custom/cut-dwim ()
  "智能剪切:有选区时剪切选区,否则剪切当前行。"
  (interactive)
  (cond
   ((use-region-p)
    (kill-region (region-beginning) (region-end)))
   (t
    (kill-whole-line))))

(defun custom/comment-dwim-stable ()
  "稳定执行注释/取消注释。优先处理 region,否则处理当前行。"
  (interactive)
  (if (use-region-p)
      (progn
        (comment-or-uncomment-region (region-beginning) (region-end))
        (deactivate-mark))
    (comment-line 1)))

(defun custom/rename-dwim ()
  "重命名符号或当前文件。"
  (interactive)
  (cond
   ((and (fboundp 'eglot-managed-p)
         (eglot-managed-p)
         (fboundp 'eglot-rename))
    (call-interactively #'eglot-rename))
   ((and buffer-file-name
         (fboundp 'rename-visited-file))
    (call-interactively #'rename-visited-file))
   (t
    (user-error "当前缓冲区不支持重命名"))))

(defun custom/reset-text-scale ()
  "重置当前缓冲区缩放级别。"
  (interactive)
  (text-scale-set 0))

;; 关闭 buffer + 窗口直接用内置 kill-buffer-and-window(C-x k 绑它):
;; 原自定义版 (condition-case nil ... (error nil)) 会静默吞掉所有错误(如未
;; 保存确认被拒绝时用户得不到任何反馈),内置版行为一致且不吞错误。

(use-package indent-bars
  :defer t
  :hook ((c-ts-mode c++-ts-mode go-ts-mode java-ts-mode json-ts-mode
                    python-ts-mode rust-ts-mode typescript-ts-mode yaml-ts-mode) .
                    indent-bars-mode)
  :custom
  (indent-bars-color nil)
  (indent-bars-color-by-depth '(:homogeneity 0.5))
  (indent-bars-pattern ".")
  (indent-bars-width-frac 0.15)
  (indent-bars-pad-pos 1)
  (indent-bars-zigzag nil)
  (indent-bars-no-stretch-char nil))

(use-package goto-chg
  :defer t
  :commands (goto-last-change goto-last-change-reverse))

(use-package multiple-cursors
  :defer t
  :commands (mc/edit-lines
             mc/mark-next-like-this mc/mark-previous-like-this
             mc/mark-all-like-this mc/mark-all-like-this-in-defun))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(custom/bind "C-c e l" #'mc/edit-lines "每行一个多光标" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e n" #'mc/mark-next-like-this "标记下一个匹配" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e p" #'mc/mark-previous-like-this "标记上一个匹配" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e a" #'mc/mark-all-like-this "标记全部匹配" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e d" #'mc/mark-all-like-this-in-defun "函数内全部匹配" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e ." #'goto-last-change "上一处修改" "编辑变换")
(custom/bind "C-c e ," #'goto-last-change-reverse "下一处修改" "编辑变换")
#+end_src

*** 编辑辅助包

+ =indent-bars= :基于 tree-sitter 的缩进竖线。只在 ts-mode 启用——非 ts-mode 缺乏可靠的缩进 AST,indent-bars 会画错。竖线不自己算颜色(=indent-bars-color nil=,follow 主题),按深度递减饱和度;pattern 用单字符 dot 列,关闭锯齿避免 ts 节点抖动。
+ =goto-chg= :undo-tree 风格的「按修改点跳转」,比 undo 逐次翻历史更精准。
+ =multiple-cursors= :在选中 region 内每个匹配字符串处插入 cursor,适合批量重命名/格式化。

** 终端集成 ghostel
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: terminal
:END:

ghostel 终端模拟器(libghostty-vt 后端): =custom/open-terminal= 在底部 side-window 打开,自动 cd 到项目根目录。

+ 底部 side-window(高度 1/3),自动切换到项目根目录;不在项目则用当前目录;带前缀参数则用当前文件所在目录
+ 有已显示的终端窗口则聚焦;有终端 buffer 但未显示则显示到底部;无则新建
+ =C-h= 在 ghostel 内重绑为发送原始 Ctrl+H(全局被 backward-char 占用), =M-方向键= 保持窗口切换
+ 关键 hack:终端里的 =git commit= / =git rebase -i= 需要 EDITOR 返回;ghostel 的 pre-spawn-hook 跑在进程创建 *之前* , =get-buffer-process= 返回 nil,故不能用 =with-editor-export-git-editor= (会抛 "Cannot export environment variables in this buffer"),必须直接调 =with-editor--setup= (内部 API),手动绑定 =with-editor--envvar=

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(eval-when-compile
  (require 'with-editor nil t))

;; agent-shell 后端标记的前向声明,避免 byte-compile free variable 警告
(defvar custom--agent-ghostel-p nil)

<<compat/ghostel-with-editor>>

(defun custom/ghostel-send-ctrl-h ()
  "向 ghostel 发送 C-h(全局被 backward-char 占用,需显式重绑)。"
  (interactive)
  (ghostel-send-key "h" "ctrl"))

;; ghostel 惰性加载,hook/keymap 加载前为 void,故用 with-eval-after-load 守卫。
(with-eval-after-load 'ghostel
  (add-hook 'ghostel-pre-spawn-hook #'custom/ghostel-export-git-editor)
  (when (boundp 'ghostel-mode-map)
    (define-key ghostel-mode-map (kbd "C-h") #'custom/ghostel-send-ctrl-h)))

(defconst custom:terminal-side-action
  '((display-buffer-in-side-window
     (side . bottom) (slot . 0) (window-height . 0.33)))
  "终端面板的 display-buffer action:底部 side-window,高度 1/3。")

(defun custom--terminal-existing-buffer ()
  "返回当前已存在的普通 ghostel buffer(非 agent 后端),无则 nil。"
  (cl-find-if
   (lambda (buf)
     (and (buffer-live-p buf)
          (with-current-buffer buf (derived-mode-p 'ghostel-mode))
          (not (buffer-local-value 'custom--agent-ghostel-p buf))))
   (buffer-list)))

(defun custom/open-terminal (&optional arg)
  "打开 ghostel 终端(底部 side-window,高度 1/3),自动切换到项目根目录。
如果不在项目中,则使用当前目录。
带前缀参数 ARG 时,使用当前文件所在目录。

有已显示的终端窗口则聚焦;有终端 buffer 但未显示则按
`custom:terminal-side-action' 显示到底部;无则新建。"
  (interactive "P")
  (let* ((project-root (when-let* ((proj (project-current nil)))
                         (project-root proj)))
         (shell (or shell-file-name
                    (getenv "SHELL")
                    "/bin/sh"))
         (default-directory
          (cond
           (arg default-directory)                  ; 前缀参数:当前文件目录
           (project-root project-root)              ; 在项目中:项目根目录
           (t default-directory))))                 ; 不在项目中:当前目录
    ;; let 绑定 shell-file-name(原 setq 是永久全局副作用,污染后续所有子进程)。
    ;; ghostel 在此 let 内 spawn,继承动态绑定的值;let 退出后恢复原值。
    (let ((shell-file-name shell)
          (buf (or (custom--terminal-existing-buffer)
                   ;; 无现存普通 ghostel:用 save-window-excursion 隔离窗口副作用
                   (let ((before (buffer-list)))
                     (save-window-excursion (ghostel))
                     (cl-find-if
                      (lambda (b)
                        (with-current-buffer b (derived-mode-p 'ghostel-mode)))
                      (cl-set-difference (buffer-list) before))))))
      (when (buffer-live-p buf)
        (custom/display-or-focus buf custom:terminal-side-action t)))))

;; Agent Shell:AI agent 的 Emacs 端 UI(与 terminal/ghostel 独立)。
(use-package agent-shell
  :defer t
  :commands (agent-shell agent-shell-toggle))

(custom/bind "C-c a a" #'agent-shell "Agent Shell" "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a A" #'agent-shell-toggle "切换 Agent Shell" "生活应用")

#+end_src

* 编程与开发
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: programming
:END:

Eglot (LSP 客户端)、Tree-sitter、语言注册表、格式化、拼写检查、envrc、各语言 major mode。

** LSP / Tree-sitter / 诊断 / 格式化
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: programming-tools
:END:

Eglot (LSP 客户端)、Tree-sitter、语言注册表、Apheleia 保存时格式化、Flymake 诊断、envrc direnv 集成。

+ ~eglot~ :Emacs 29+ 内置 LSP 客户端,配合 =eglot-x= 扩展协议(声明/实现/类型定义跳转)
+ LSP 服务器注册延迟到首次进入 prog-mode(避免启动期 spawn 进程)
+ ~apheleia~ :保存时自动格式化(语言对应关系集中维护)
+ ~flymake~ :代码诊断(Emacs 内置)。Eglot 默认通过 flymake 接收 LSP diagnostics
+ ~envrc~ :direnv buffer-local 环境变量注入

*** 语言能力表

所有语言配置围绕单一真相源 =custom:language-capabilities= 组织,新增语言只改这一份。下表是每条目字段的含义和它的派生消费者:

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 字段       | 含义                                          | 派生消费者                                |
|------------+-----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------------------|
| =:modes=     | 生效的所有 major mode(传统 mode 在前,ts 在后) | eglot auto-modes、Apheleia mode-alist     |
| =:ts-mode=   | Tree-sitter 对应 mode(nil = 无 ts 版本)       | =major-mode-remap-alist=                    |
| =:server=    | LSP server 启动命令(nil = 无 LSP)             | =eglot-server-programs=、eglot auto-modes |
| =:formatter= | Apheleia formatter 符号(nil = 不格式化)       | Apheleia mode-alist                       |

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'json)

(defconst custom:language-capabilities
  '((:modes (c-mode c-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode c-ts-mode
            :server ("ccls")
            :formatter clang-format)
    (:modes (c++-mode c++-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode c++-ts-mode
            :server ("ccls")
            :formatter clang-format)
    (:modes (gdscript-mode)
            :server nil                       ; gdscript LSP 由 godot 自带
            :formatter nil)
    (:modes (go-mode go-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode go-ts-mode
            :server ("gopls")
            :formatter gofmt)
    (:modes (java-mode java-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode java-ts-mode
            :server nil
            :formatter nil)
    (:modes (json-mode json-ts-mode js-json-mode)
            :ts-mode json-ts-mode
            :server nil
            :formatter js-beautify)
    (:modes (markdown-mode gfm-mode)
            :server nil
            :formatter pandoc-markdown)
    (:modes (nix-mode)
            :server nil
            :formatter nixfmt)
    (:modes (python-mode python-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode python-ts-mode
            :server ("pylsp")
            :formatter black)
    (:modes (rust-mode rust-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode rust-ts-mode
            :server ("rust-analyzer")
            :formatter rustfmt)
    (:modes (typescript-mode typescript-ts-mode)
            :ts-mode typescript-ts-mode
            :server nil
            :formatter js-beautify)
    (:modes (sh-mode bash-mode)
            :server nil
            :formatter shfmt)
    (:modes (fish-mode)
            :server nil
            :formatter fish-indent)
    (:modes (yaml-mode)
            :server nil
            :formatter yq-yaml)
    (:modes (zig-mode)
            :server ("zls")
            :formatter zig-fmt))
  "统一的语言能力表。每条条目:
  :modes     生效的所有 major mode 列表(传统 mode 在前,ts-mode 在后)
  :ts-mode   Tree-sitter 对应 mode(nil 表示无 ts 版本)
  :server    LSP server 启动命令列表(nil 表示无 LSP)
  :formatter Apheleia formatter 符号(nil 表示不格式化)")

(defun custom/language--collect (field &optional non-nil)
  "收集所有语言能力表中 FIELD 的值。NON-NIL 时只收集非 nil 条目。"
  (let (result)
    (dolist (entry custom:language-capabilities)
      (let ((value (plist-get entry field)))
        (when (or (not non-nil) value)
          (push value result))))
    (nreverse result)))

(defconst custom:language-treesit-remaps
  (delq nil
        (mapcar (lambda (entry)
                  (let ((orig-mode (car (plist-get entry :modes)))
                        (ts-mode (plist-get entry :ts-mode)))
                    (when (and orig-mode ts-mode)
                      (cons orig-mode ts-mode))))
                custom:language-capabilities))
  "Tree-sitter major mode remap 列表(从 `custom:language-capabilities' 派生)。")

(defconst custom:language-eglot-auto-modes
  (apply #'append
         (mapcar (lambda (entry)
                   (if (plist-get entry :server)
                       (plist-get entry :modes)
                     nil))
                 custom:language-capabilities))
  "进入这些 major mode 时自动触发 `eglot-ensure'。
不调 `eglot--lookup-mode' 私有 API 做可用性预检测;进入 mode 时无条件
`eglot-ensure',server 缺失的错误由 Eglot 自身暴露。")

(defconst custom:language-eglot-server-programs
  (let ((seen (make-hash-table :test 'equal))
        (result nil))
    (dolist (entry custom:language-capabilities)
      (let ((server (plist-get entry :server)))
        (when server
          (let ((key (mapconcat #'identity server " ")))
            (if-let* ((pair (gethash key seen)))
                (setcar pair (append (car pair) (plist-get entry :modes)))
              (push (cons (plist-get entry :modes) server) result)
              (puthash key (car result) seen))))))
    (nreverse result))
  "附加到 `eglot-server-programs' 的语言服务器配置(从能力表派生,按 server 命令去重)。")

(defconst custom:language-apheleia-formatters
  '((pandoc-markdown "pandoc" "-f" "gfm+yaml_metadata_block" "-t" "gfm" "--standalone" "--wrap=none"))
  "Apheleia 自定义 formatter 命令定义(mode-alist 只引用符号)。")

(defconst custom:language-apheleia-mode-alist
  (let (result)
    (dolist (entry custom:language-capabilities)
      (let ((formatter (plist-get entry :formatter)))
        (when formatter
          (dolist (mode (plist-get entry :modes))
            (push (cons mode formatter) result)))))
    (nreverse result))
  "Apheleia major mode 对应关系(从 `custom:language-capabilities' 派生)。")
#+end_src

*** Tree-sitter grammar 加载

Grammar 的供给链完全由 Guix 处理,配置侧不需要参与:

+ =home-emacs-service= 的 wrapper(=emacs-wrapper=)启动 emacs 前用 =evaluate-search-paths= 把 profile 的 =lib/tree-sitter= 注入 =TREE_SITTER_GRAMMAR_PATH= 环境变量。
+ profile 的 =site-start.el= 加载 Guix 自带的 =guix-emacs.el=,后者在 =(with-eval-after-load 'treesit ...)= 里把该环境变量拆分加入 =treesit-extra-load-path=。Grammar 解析由此自动完成,无需本配置插手。
+ =(setq treesit-enabled-modes t)= 和 =(setq treesit-auto-install-grammar nil)= 已是 Emacs 29+/31 的默认值,不重复设置;grammar 运行时下载由 manifest 禁,可复现性由 Guix 侧而非这里保证。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(dolist (mapping custom:language-treesit-remaps)
  (when (fboundp (cdr mapping))
    (add-to-list 'major-mode-remap-alist mapping)))
#+end_src

*** Eglot 激活序列

Eglot 是 =:defer t=,延迟加载的激活链:

1. 首次进入任意 =prog-mode= → =custom--eglot-setup-once= 触发 =(require 'eglot)=。
2. Eglot 加载后(无论由 prog-mode-hook 触发 require,还是 eglot 命令 autoload),=with-eval-after-load 'eglot= 注册:
   - 语言服务器程序表(=custom:language-eglot-server-programs= 附加到 =eglot-server-programs=)
   - 为声明了 =:server= 的语言 mode 挂 =eglot-ensure=
3. 进入声明了 =:server= 的 mode 时直接 =eglot-ensure=;server 缺失的错误由 Eglot 自身暴露,不预检测可用性。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package eglot
  :defer t
  :custom
  (eglot-autoshutdown t)
  (eglot-workspace-configuration
   `((:pylsp . (:plugins
                (:black (:enabled t)
                        :pylsp_mypy (:enabled t :live_mode nil)
                        :rope (:enabled t)
                        :pycodestyle (:enabled ,json-false)
                        :mccabe (:enabled ,json-false)
                        :pyflakes (:enabled ,json-false))))))
  :config
  (add-hook 'eglot-managed-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when (fboundp 'eglot-inlay-hints-mode)
                (eglot-inlay-hints-mode 1)))))

(defun custom--eglot-setup-once ()
  "首次进入 prog-mode 时触发 eglot 加载,由 after-load 钩子完成实际配置。"
  (remove-hook 'prog-mode-hook #'custom--eglot-setup-once)
  (require 'eglot))

(add-hook 'prog-mode-hook #'custom--eglot-setup-once)

(with-eval-after-load 'eglot
  (dolist (prog custom:language-eglot-server-programs)
    (add-to-list 'eglot-server-programs prog))
  (dolist (mode custom:language-eglot-auto-modes)
    (add-hook (intern (format "%s-hook" mode)) #'eglot-ensure)))

(with-eval-after-load 'eglot
  (require 'eglot-x)
  (eglot-x-setup))
#+end_src

*** Apheleia 与 Flymake

Apheleia 用 =:hook (prog-mode . apheleia-mode)= 而非 =:defer t= 触发加载:后者没有可靠的首次加载入口,=apheleia-global-mode= 不会启用,保存时格式化在用户首次手动调 Apheleia 命令前都失效。进任何 prog buffer 就启用 global mode,保存时格式化立即生效。

Flymake 不需要 global 化——Eglot 在它管理的 buffer 内自动启用 =flymake-mode-hook=;非 LSP buffer 由各 major mode 的 =syntax-table= 提供诊断。全局跳转键 =M-g n/p= 与 =C-c l e= 在 [[#keybindings][前缀键体系]] 域统一绑定,不在此处 =:bind=,避免 flymake 未加载时键位悬空。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defcustom custom/format-on-save-enabled t
  "是否启用保存时自动格式化。"
  :type 'boolean
  :group 'literal)

(use-package apheleia
  :hook (prog-mode . apheleia-mode)
  :config
  (when custom/format-on-save-enabled
    (apheleia-global-mode 1))
  (dolist (formatter custom:language-apheleia-formatters)
    (setf (alist-get (car formatter) apheleia-formatters)
          (cdr formatter)))
  (dolist (mapping custom:language-apheleia-mode-alist)
    (setf (alist-get (car mapping) apheleia-mode-alist)
          (cdr mapping))))

(use-package flymake
  :ensure nil
  :custom
  (flymake-fringe-indicator-position 'left-fringe)
  (flymake-no-changes-timeout 0.5))

(defun custom/flymake-list-errors-dwim ()
  "打开/关闭 Flymake 诊断列表面板。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((buf (get-buffer "*Flymake diagnostics*")))
    (if (and buf (get-buffer-window buf))
        (quit-window nil (get-buffer-window buf))
      (flymake-show-buffer-diagnostics))))

(use-package envrc
  :demand t
  :if (executable-find custom:executable-direnv)
  :config
  (envrc-global-mode 1))

(use-package rainbow-delimiters
  :hook (prog-mode . rainbow-delimiters-mode))
#+end_src

** 代码操作辅助函数
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: code-commands
:END:

供快捷键和右键菜单复用的代码操作函数:复制行、移动行、跳转、格式化、块注释。

+ =custom/tabs-next= / =custom/tabs-previous= / =custom/tabs-refresh-context= 由 [[#tab-line][Tab-line]] 提供
+ 本块定义的函数被 [[#global-keys][全局直达键]]、[[#programming-keys][代码与导航键]] 复用

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun custom/code--current-line-text ()
  "返回当前行文本,包含行尾换行。"
  (buffer-substring (line-beginning-position)
                    (line-beginning-position 2)))

(defun custom/code-duplicate-line ()
  "复制当前行并在下方粘贴。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((column (current-column))
        (line (custom/code--current-line-text))
        (insert-at (line-beginning-position 2)))
    (goto-char insert-at)
    (insert line)
    (goto-char insert-at)
    (move-to-column column)))

(defun custom/code-move-line-up ()
  "将当前行上移一行。"
  (interactive)
  (transpose-lines 1)
  (forward-line -2))

(defun custom/code-move-line-down ()
  "将当前行下移一行。"
  (interactive)
  (forward-line 1)
  (transpose-lines 1)
  (forward-line -1))

(defun custom/code-goto-definition ()
  "跳转到定义。"
  (interactive)
  (call-interactively #'xref-find-definitions))

(defun custom/code-goto-references ()
  "查找所有引用。"
  (interactive)
  (call-interactively #'xref-find-references))

(defun custom/code-jump-back ()
  "跳回之前的位置。"
  (interactive)
  (if (fboundp 'xref-go-back) (xref-go-back) (pop-mark)))

(defun custom/code-jump-forward ()
  "跳到下一个位置。"
  (interactive)
  (if (fboundp 'xref-go-forward) (xref-go-forward)
    (message "前进功能不可用")))

(defun custom/code-format-dwim ()
  "智能格式化:有选区格式化选区,否则格式化整个缓冲区。"
  (interactive)
  (if (use-region-p)
      (indent-region (region-beginning) (region-end))
    (cond
     ((and (fboundp 'eglot-managed-p) (eglot-managed-p))
      (eglot-format-buffer))
     (t
      (indent-region (point-min) (point-max))))))

(defun custom/code-comment-block ()
  "使用块注释包围选区。"
  (interactive)
  (if (use-region-p)
      (let ((comment-style 'multi-line))
        (comment-region (region-beginning) (region-end)))
    (message "请先选择要块注释的区域")))
#+end_src

** 各语言 major mode
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: language-modes
:END:

GDScript、Fish、JSON / kdl / Kotlin / Markdown / Nix / Rust / TypeScript / Web / Zig、Scheme (Guile) + Arei、Yasnippet。

+ 各 mode 以 =:defer t= + =:mode= 触发,lazy-load
+ Arei(Guile 开发)只在真实 Scheme buffer 启用,跳过 org src 临时 buffer
+ *Arei 兼容性*:arei 0.9.7 的 Guix =.elc= 与 Emacs 31 =buffer-local-set-state= 宏不兼容。配置从 =.el= 源加载绕过(在 [[#compatibility][实验性与待确认]] 的 =emacs31/arei-compat= 片段定义,此处展开);=arei-mode= 调用包裹 =condition-case=,防止拖垮 scheme-mode 染色。Arei 的 =:init= 在 use-package 展开时立即执行(先于 autoload),确保首次加载就走源。
+ *Yasnippet 兼容性*:旧 =.elc= 缺少 Emacs 31 =define-globalized-minor-mode= 生成的 buffer-local 状态变量，会在新 client 的 major-mode hook 中报错。daemon 启动时从 =yasnippet.el= 源加载并立即启用，避免 timer 首用失败。

                   "C-c p" #'markdown-preview "预览 Markdown" "模式局部")

(defun custom/pdf-open (file)
  "选择并打开 PDF FILE。"
  (interactive (list (read-file-name "PDF: " nil nil t nil
                                     (lambda (candidate)

  (find-file file))

* 项目与导航
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: projects
:END:

项目管理(project.el + consult-fd + consult-ripgrep)、文件与缓冲区导航。

** 项目管理 project.el
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: project-navigation
:END:

配置内置 =project.el= 项目管理:自动识别项目根、文件搜索、grep、ibuffer 按项目分组。

+ 使用 project.el 内置 API(=project-switch-project= / =project-known-project-roots= / =project-current=)
+ =project-switch-commands= = Dired / consult-fd / consult-ripgrep
+ Emacs 31 的命令项格式为 =(COMMAND LABEL &optional KEY)= ，显式键避免依赖 =project-prefix-map= 的间接查找。
+ =consult-fd= : 项目内快速文件搜索( =custom/find-file-in-project= ),fd 缺失时降级为 find
+ ibuffer 用内置 project filter 分组( =project-current= + =project-root= )
+ 项目前缀 =C-x p= 在本域末尾绑定(功能内聚)
+ 项目切换清空当前 frame 的 tab-line( =project-after-switch-project-functions= ,见 [[#tab-line][Tab-line]])

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun custom/project-root-or-default ()
  "返回当前 project 的 root,不在项目中时返回 `default-directory'。"
  (or (when-let* ((proj (project-current nil)))
        (project-root proj))
      default-directory))

(defun custom/switch-project ()
  "切换到已知项目,并以 Dired 打开项目根目录。

使用 `project-switch-project'(Emacs 29+ project.el);切换后
`project-after-switch-project-functions' 触发(见 tab-line 域)。"
  (interactive)
  (require 'project)
  (call-interactively #'project-switch-project)
  (when-let* ((proj (project-current nil))
              (root (project-root proj)))
    (dired root)))

(defun custom/open-project-folder ()
  "打开任意文件夹:切换 `default-directory' 并显示 Dired。

含 .git 的目录会通过 `project-remember-project' 注册到已知项目。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((dir (read-directory-name "项目目录: ")))
    (when (file-directory-p dir)
      (setq dir (file-name-as-directory (expand-file-name dir))
            default-directory dir)
      (when (file-directory-p (expand-file-name ".git" dir))
        (project-remember-project (project-current nil)))
      (dired dir))))

(defun custom/open-directory ()
  "打开当前文件所在目录(或当前 default-directory)的 Dired。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((dir (if buffer-file-name
                 (file-name-directory buffer-file-name)
               default-directory)))
    (when dir
      (dired dir))))

;; switch-commands 控制 project-switch-project 后的默认动作;更细粒度的 C-x p
;; 入口在 [[#keybindings][前缀键体系]] 注册,这里只设默认。
(setq project-switch-commands
      '((project-dired "打开目录" ?d)
        (custom/find-file-in-project "寻找文件" ?f)
        (custom/project-search "查找关键字" ?r)))

;; ibuffer 用内置 predicate filter 按 project.el 已知根目录分组。
(with-eval-after-load 'ibuffer
  (require 'ibuf-ext)
  (defun custom/ibuffer-set-project-groups ()
    "按 `project-known-project-roots' 设置当前 Ibuffer 的项目分组。"
    (setq ibuffer-filter-groups
          (mapcar
           (lambda (root)
             (list (file-name-nondirectory (directory-file-name root))
                   (cons 'predicate
                         `(when-let* ((project
                                       (project-current nil default-directory)))
                            (equal (project-root project) ,root)))))
           (project-known-project-roots)))
    (ibuffer-update nil t))
  (add-hook 'ibuffer-hook
            #'custom/ibuffer-set-project-groups))

(defun custom/find-file-in-project ()
  "用 consult-fd 查找当前项目内文件。

在 project.el 项目内时限定到项目根目录;否则从当前目录搜索。
依赖外部命令 fd；缺失时 consult-fd 自动降级为 consult-find(find 命令)。"
  (interactive)
  (require 'consult)
  (let ((default-directory (custom/project-root-or-default)))
    (call-interactively #'consult-fd)))

(defun custom/project-search ()
  "用 consult-ripgrep 在当前项目内搜索。"
  (interactive)
  (require 'consult)
  (let ((default-directory (custom/project-root-or-default)))
    (call-interactively #'consult-ripgrep)))

;; 项目切换时清空当前 frame 的 tab-line 标签列表(等价原 projectile-after-switch-project-hook)。
(with-eval-after-load 'project
  (add-hook 'project-after-switch-project-functions
            (lambda (&rest _) (custom/tabs-clear-for-frame))))
#+end_src

项目前缀 =C-x p= 与 =project.el= 同源,在下方 src-block 直接绑定。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(custom/bind "C-x p p" #'project-switch-project "切换项目" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p f" #'project-find-file "查找项目文件" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p s" #'custom/project-search "项目全文搜索" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p o" #'custom/open-project-folder "打开项目目录" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p O" #'custom/open-directory "目录浏览" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p k" #'project-kill-buffers "关闭项目缓冲区" "项目")
(custom/bind "C-x p D" #'project-dired "项目根 Dired" "项目")

* Org 与知识库
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: org-knowledge
:END:

Org 基础设置、capture 模板、org-roam、Babel、org-modern UI、Olivetti 写作模式;知识库系统(agenote CLI 集成)由 =org/knowledge= 与 =org/agenote= 两个 noweb 模块内联提供。

** Org 基础 / Capture / Roam / UI
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: org-core
:END:

Org 目录确保、Org 基础设置、capture 模板、org-modern / org-appear UI、org-roam 笔记网络、Olivetti / Deft。

+ =org-directory= : =~/Documents/Org/=
+ =org-startup-folded 'overview= : 打开时仅显示一级标题
+ =org-capture-templates= : 经验卡片三种 ENTRY_TYPE(note / mistake / ascended)
+ =org-roam= :笔记网络( =roam/= 子目录)
+ =org-modern= + =org-appear= : UI 现代化
+ 知识库 CRUD 由 =org/knowledge= 模块提供(本文档内联,见后文)

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun custom--ensure-org-directories ()
  "确保所有 Org 相关目录存在。"
  (dolist (dir (list custom:org-directory
                     (expand-file-name "agenda" custom:org-directory)
                     custom:org-roam-directory
                     custom:org-knowledge-directory
                     (expand-file-name "babel" custom:org-directory)
                     (expand-file-name "tangle" custom:org-directory)))
    (unless (file-exists-p dir)
      (make-directory dir t))))

(use-package org
  :defer t
  :custom
  (org-directory custom:org-directory)
  (org-agenda-files (list (expand-file-name "agenda" custom:org-directory)))
  (org-hide-emphasis-markers t)
  (org-startup-indented t)
  (org-hide-leading-stars t)
  (org-ellipsis "...")
  (org-auto-align-tags nil)
  (org-tags-column 0)
  (org-catch-invisible-edits 'show-and-error)
  (org-special-ctrl-a/e t)
  (org-insert-heading-respect-content t)
  (org-pretty-entities t)
  (org-use-sub-superscripts '{})
  (org-cycle-separator-lines 2)
  (org-startup-folded 'overview)
  (org-blank-before-new-entry '((heading . t) (plain-list-item . auto)))
  (org-fontify-whole-heading-line t)
  (org-fontify-done-headline t)
  (org-fontify-quote-and-verse-blocks t)
  (org-src-fontify-natively t)
  (org-src-tab-acts-natively t)
  (org-src-preserve-indentation t)
  (org-src-window-setup 'current-window)
  (org-edit-src-content-indentation 0)
  (org-image-actual-width '(300))
  :config
  ;; daemon/client 下按 frame 判断图片显示能力
  (defun custom--org-toggle-inline-images ()
    (setq-local org-startup-with-inline-images (display-graphic-p)))
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook #'custom--org-toggle-inline-images)

  (defun custom/org-setup-visuals ()
    "减少 Org 文档视图中的编辑器噪音。"
    (display-line-numbers-mode -1))
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook #'custom/org-setup-visuals)

  ;; C-j 在 Org 内分发:表格内走原命令,表格外走 next-line
  (defun custom/org-c-j-dispatch ()
    "在 Org buffer 中分发 C-j:表格内走 `org-return-and-maybe-indent',
其余位置走 `next-line'。"
    (interactive)
    (call-interactively
     (if (org-at-table-p)
         #'org-return-and-maybe-indent
       #'next-line)))
  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "C-j") #'custom/org-c-j-dispatch)
  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "TAB") #'org-cycle)
  (define-key org-mode-map (kbd "<backtab>") #'org-shifttab))
#+end_src

*** Org Capture 模板
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: capture-templates
:END:

=org= 用 =:defer t= 加载,capture 模板从 org 的 =:config= 移出到独立的 =with-eval-after-load 'org-capture=:这些辅助函数只依赖 bootstrap 常量(无 org 依赖),但 =org-capture-templates= 需 org-capture 加载后设置才有意义。模板设置延迟到首次 capture 时。=k= 前缀是经验/笔记子菜单,=ki/kt/kd/ke/kr/kn/km/ka= 八条模板覆盖四类数据生命周期:

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 键 | 名称            | 目标         | 生命周期语义                             |
|----+-----------------+--------------+------------------------------------------|
| =ki= | Inbox 草稿      | =inbox.org=    | 一句话草稿、待分流事项                   |
| =kt= | 任务            | =agenda/=      | 普通任务(进 agenda)                      |
| =kd= | 带日期任务      | =agenda/=      | 任务 + SCHEDULED 日期                    |
| =ke= | 日程事件        | =agenda/=      | 日程事件(无 TODO 关键字)                 |
| =kr= | Roam 长期笔记   | =roam/=        | 长期笔记与双向关联知识                   |
| =kn= | 经验-笔记       | =experiences/= | note 条目(对齐 agenote-base entry-types) |
| =km= | 经验-踩坑       | =experiences/= | mistake 条目                             |
| =ka= | 经验-试错后最优 | =experiences/= | ascended 条目                            |

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar custom/org-capture--last-category nil
  "本次 capture 的经验卡 category。")
(defvar custom/org-capture--roam-title nil
  "本次 Roam capture 的标题。")
(defun custom/org-capture--category ()
  "返回本次 capture 选定的 category。"
  (or custom/org-capture--last-category "general"))
(defun custom/org-capture--experience-file ()
  "生成经验卡片目标文件路径(人类域,从 `agenote fields --category' 拉分类)。"
  (custom--ensure-org-directories)
  (let* ((choices (or (condition-case nil
                          (split-string
                           (custom/agenote-call-string 'human "fields" "--category")
                           "\n" t "[ \t]+")
                        (error nil))
                      '("emacs" "guix" "general" "gamedev" "devops"
                        "research" "tooling")))
         (cat (completing-read "经验卡类别: " choices nil t))
         (dir (expand-file-name (concat "experiences/" cat)
                                custom:org-directory)))
    (setq custom/org-capture--last-category cat)
    (unless (file-directory-p dir)
      (make-directory dir t))
    (expand-file-name (format "%s.org" (format-time-string "%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"))
                      dir)))
(defun custom/org-capture--agenda-file ()
  "返回本次 capture 的目标 agenda 文件。
当前 `org-agenda-files' 只有一项;未来扩展为多文件时改为 completing-read。"
  (custom--ensure-org-directories)
  (car (org-agenda-files)))

(defun custom/org-capture--roam-file ()
  "返回本次长期笔记的 Roam 文件路径。"
  (custom--ensure-org-directories)
  (setq custom/org-capture--roam-title
        (read-string "长期笔记标题: "))
  (expand-file-name
   (format "%s.org" (format-time-string "%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"))
   custom:org-roam-directory))

(defun custom/org-capture--roam-title-value ()
  "返回当前 Roam capture 标题。"
  (or custom/org-capture--roam-title "未命名笔记"))
(with-eval-after-load 'org-capture
  (setq org-capture-templates
        `(("ki" "Inbox 草稿" entry
           (file ,custom:org-inbox-file)
           "* TODO %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%i\n")
          ("kt" "任务 (agenda)" entry
           (file custom/org-capture--agenda-file)
           "* TODO %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%i\n")
          ("kd" "带日期任务 (agenda)" entry
           (file custom/org-capture--agenda-file)
           "* TODO %?\nSCHEDULED: %^t\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%i\n")
          ("ke" "日程事件 (agenda)" entry
           (file custom/org-capture--agenda-file)
           "* %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%i\n")
          ("kr" "Roam 长期笔记" plain
           (file custom/org-capture--roam-file)
           "#+title: %(custom/org-capture--roam-title-value)\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:CREATED:  %U\n:END:\n\n%?")
          ("kn" "经验-笔记 (note)" plain
           (file custom/org-capture--experience-file)
           "* %^{标题}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(format-time-string \"%Y%m%d-%H%M%S\")\n:CREATED:  %(format-time-string \"[%Y-%m-%d %a]\")\n:CATEGORY: %(custom/org-capture--category)\n:TECH:     %^{技术栈}\n:TYPE:     workflow\n:STATUS:   done\n:ENTRY_TYPE: note\n:END:\n#+filetags: :%^{标签}:\n\n** 事项内容\n%?\n** 为什么值得长期保留\n\n** 适用场景与例外\n\n** 后续行动\n")
          ("km" "经验-踩坑 (mistake)" plain
           (file custom/org-capture--experience-file)
           "* %^{标题}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(format-time-string \"%Y%m%d-%H%M%S\")\n:CREATED:  %(format-time-string \"[%Y-%m-%d %a]\")\n:CATEGORY: %(custom/org-capture--category)\n:TECH:     %^{技术栈}\n:TYPE:     debug\n:STATUS:   done\n:ENTRY_TYPE: mistake\n:END:\n#+filetags: :%^{标签}:\n\n** 原始问题\n%?\n** 纠错反馈\n\n** 这次到底错在哪里\n\n** 最终正确处理\n")
          ("ka" "经验-试错后最优 (ascended)" plain
           (file custom/org-capture--experience-file)
           "* %^{标题}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(format-time-string \"%Y%m%d-%H%M%S\")\n:CREATED:  %(format-time-string \"[%Y-%m-%d %a]\")\n:CATEGORY: %(custom/org-capture--category)\n:TECH:     %^{技术栈}\n:TYPE:     debug\n:STATUS:   done\n:ENTRY_TYPE: ascended\n:END:\n#+filetags: :%^{标签}:\n\n** 前几轮失败的根因\n%?\n** 检索过的知识源\n\n** 核对过的真实文件或输出\n\n** 最终采用的最强方案\n")))
  (add-hook 'org-capture-after-finalize-hook
            (lambda ()
              (setq custom/org-capture--last-category nil
                    custom/org-capture--roam-title nil))))
#+end_src

*** Org UI 与检索

+ =org-modern= / =org-appear= :Org 视觉现代化与光标处标记显隐。
+ =org-indent-mode= 启用( =org-startup-indented t= ),块缩进样式由 =org-modern-indent= 补回:原生 =org-modern= 在 org-indent 启用时会关闭代码块 fringe 边框,=org-modern-indent= 用 bracket + wrap-prefix 在 org-indent 下近似 =org-modern= 的块样式。
+ =org-modern-table= 启用细线表格，=org-modern-block-fringe= 无条件设为 4:daemon 启动期 =(display-graphic-p)= 永远为 nil,有条件的判断会让 GUI client 的 block fringe 被关掉;TTY 下 fringe 参数被忽略,设 4 无副作用。
+ Org buffer 隐藏行号，减少文档阅读时的编辑器噪音；代码块、表格和元数据继续使用主题既有的等宽 face。
+ =org-roam= autosync 在首次 roam 命令(=:commands= 触发加载)后启动。
+ =deft= 只作历史 =notes/= 内容的只读检索入口(=deft-recursive nil=,不递归其他 Org 子目录)。新笔记统一走 [[#capture-templates][Capture 模板]](=ki= inbox / =kr= roam),不要用 Deft 创建。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package org-appear
  :hook (org-mode . org-appear-mode)
  :custom
  (org-appear-autoemphasis t)
  (org-appear-autolinks t)
  (org-appear-autosubmarkers t)
  (org-appear-autoentities t))

(use-package org-bullets-mode
  :defer t
  :config
  :hook org-mode)

(use-package org-modern
  :hook (org-mode . org-modern-mode)
  :custom
  (org-modern-label-border 0.12)
  (org-modern-star 'replace)
  (org-modern-replace-stars '("" "" "󰜋" "󰜌" "" "" ""))
  (org-modern-list
   '((?- . "‣")
     (?* . "•")
     (?+ . "‣")))
  (org-modern-hide-stars 'leading)
  (org-modern-table t)
  (org-modern-table-vertical 2)
  (org-modern-table-horizontal 0.12)
  (org-modern-keyword t)
  (org-modern-todo t)
  (org-modern-tag t)
  (org-modern-block-name t)
  (org-modern-block-fringe 4))

(use-package org-modern-indent
  :defer t
  :config
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook #'org-modern-indent-mode 90))

(use-package org-roam
  :defer t
  :commands (org-roam-node-find org-roam-node-insert org-roam-buffer-toggle)
  :custom
  (org-roam-directory custom:org-roam-directory)
  (org-roam-database-connector 'sqlite)
  (org-roam-completion-everywhere nil)
  :config
  (org-roam-db-autosync-mode))

(use-package olivetti
  :defer t
  :commands (olivetti-mode)
  :custom
  (olivetti-body-width 100))

(use-package deft
  :defer t
  :commands (deft)

  (deft-directory (expand-file-name "notes" custom:org-directory))
  :config
  (setq deft-recursive nil))

** Knowledge 系统
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: knowledge
:END:

agenote CLI 集成的知识库 CRUD。

+ =custom/knowledge-*= :知识库经验卡片 CRUD 与双视图
+ =custom/agenote-*= :agenote 子域浏览与健康度面板
+ Knowledge 与 agenote 直接复用启动域路径常量和 [[#process-helper][共享进程辅助]],不维护重复注入点或私有进程封装

*** 职责归属
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: knowledge-ownership
:END:

知识库的文件系统操作全部下沉给 agenote CLI,Emacs 只负责交互调用和缓冲区刷新。这种分层避免 Emacs 与 CLI 的索引/扫描逻辑漂移:

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 职责                        | 归属                              |
|-----------------------------+-----------------------------------|
| 索引列表                    | =agenote list --json=               |
| inbox 归档 / slug / reindex | =agenote inbox-archive= 子命令      |
| 交互调用 + buffer 刷新      | Emacs(=custom/knowledge-*=)         |
| 递归扫描 experiences/       | 故意不存在(Dashboard 走 CLI 索引) |

*** 路径与目录生命周期

目录创建采用按需幂等 =ensure=,而非固定延迟 timer。原因:固定 3 秒的 =run-with-idle-timer= 会与用户的 capture / inbox 打开 / before-save 操作竞态(timer 触发时文件可能正被写入)。=custom--ensure-knowledge-directories= 挂在 =org-capture-before-finalize-hook= 和 =org-mode-hook= 上,确保目录在真正需要时才存在,=experiences/<category>/= 子目录在 Capture 时按需创建。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/knowledge-core :tangle no
(defun custom--ensure-knowledge-directories ()
  "确保知识库相关目录和索引文件存在(幂等)。
experiences/ 下的类别子目录在 Capture 时按需创建，不在此处预建。
inbox.org 不存在时用最小骨架初始化,已存在则保持原样。"
  (let ((dirs (list (expand-file-name "experiences" custom:org-directory))))
    (dolist (dir dirs)
      (unless (file-exists-p dir)
        (make-directory dir t))))
  (dolist (file (list custom:org-inbox-file))
    (unless (file-exists-p file)
      (let ((base (file-name-base file)))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (insert (format "#+title: %s\n#+date: [%s]\n\n* Inbox\n"
                          base (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %a")))
          (write-region (point-min) (point-max) file))))))

(with-eval-after-load 'org-capture
  (add-hook 'org-capture-before-finalize-hook
            #'custom--ensure-knowledge-directories))
(with-eval-after-load 'org
  (add-hook 'org-mode-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when (and buffer-file-name
                         (string-prefix-p custom:org-directory buffer-file-name))
                (custom--ensure-knowledge-directories)))))

#+end_src

*** Capture 与卡片命名

经验卡片的标题 slug 化、PROPERTIES 规范化、reindex 索引同步由两部分共同完成:
+ =org-capture-templates= 的 =kn/km/ka= 模板用 =%(format-time-string ...)= 在 Emacs 侧生成 ID/CREATED 时间戳与结构化 PROPERTIES(见 [[#capture-templates][Capture 模板]])。
+ =agenote inbox-archive= 子命令负责 inbox 条目的 slug、归档与 reindex。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/knowledge-capture :tangle no
(defun custom/knowledge-capture ()
  "捕获一张经验卡片，走 org-capture 模板。
`org-capture nil \"k\"' 进入 k 前缀子菜单（note/mistake/ascended，
对齐 agenote-base entry-types 语义），由 `org-capture-templates' 的
kn/km/ka 三条模板负责生成结构化 PROPERTIES + body 章节，落到
experiences/<category>/<timestamp>.org。模板定义见 org-core 域。

不预检查 `org-capture-templates':`org-capture' 是 autoload,首次调用
会自动加载 org-capture 并装配模板;预检查反而会在 fresh daemon
(org 未加载)时拒绝运行,要求用户先手动调一次。"
  (interactive)
  (org-capture nil "k"))
#+end_src

*** 检索与 Inbox

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/knowledge-search :tangle no
(defun custom/knowledge-search (query)
  "在知识库中全文检索经验。
使用 consult-ripgrep 递归搜索 experiences/。"
  (interactive "s检索关键词: ")
  (let ((dir (expand-file-name "experiences" custom:org-directory)))
    (cond
     ((fboundp 'consult-ripgrep)
      (consult-ripgrep (list dir) query))
     ((fboundp 'rgrep)
      (rgrep query "*.org" (list dir)))
     (t
      (message "未找到可用的搜索工具")))))

(defun custom/knowledge-search-by-tag (tag)
  "在知识库中按标签检索经验。
优先使用 agenote tags 命令，回退到 ripgrep 搜索 :TAG: 模式。"
  (interactive "s标签: ")
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let ((buf (get-buffer-create "*agenote-tags*")))
        (with-current-buffer buf
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (custom/agenote-call-string 'human "tags" tag)))
        (display-buffer buf))
    (custom/knowledge-search (format ":%s:" tag))))

(defun custom/knowledge-archive-inbox-entry (category)
  "将 inbox.org 中光标所在的条目归档到 experiences/<CATEGORY>/ 目录。
slug 算法、PROPERTIES 模板与 reindex 触发全部由 agenote CLI 的
`inbox-archive' 子命令负责,Emacs 只负责收集 heading + body 并调 CLI。"
  (interactive
   (list (completing-read "归档分类: "
                          (or (condition-case nil
                                  (split-string
                                   (custom/agenote-call-string 'human "fields" "--category")
                                   "\n" t "[ \t]+")
                                (error nil))
                              '("emacs" "guix" "general" "gamedev" "devops" "research" "tooling"))
                          nil t)))
  (let* ((heading (or (org-get-heading t t t t) ""))
         (full-text (progn (org-copy-subtree) (car kill-ring)))
         (body (if (and full-text (string-match "^\\*+ [^\n]+\n:PROPERTIES:\n\\(?:[^\n]*\n\\)*?:END:\n"
                                                full-text))
                   (substring full-text (match-end 0))
                 (or full-text "")))
         (stdin (json-encode `[(("heading" . ,heading) ("body" . ,body))]))
         (result (custom/agenote-call 'human "inbox-archive"
                                      "--category" category "--stdin" "--prune")))
    (if (eq (plist-get result :status) 0)
        (progn
          (setq kill-ring (cdr kill-ring))
          (when (buffer-live-p (get-buffer custom:org-inbox-file))
            (with-current-buffer (get-buffer custom:org-inbox-file)
              (revert-buffer t t t)))
          (message "已归档: %s" (string-trim (plist-get result :stdout))))
      (message "归档失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr)))))

#+end_src

inbox 归档的 body 提取逻辑: =org-copy-subtree= 把当前 subtree(含 heading 与 =PROPERTIES= 块)复制到 kill-ring,这里只取正文——从第一行 heading 之后到第一个 =:END:= 之间的 =PROPERTIES= 块被正则剥离,剩余作为 body 通过 =--stdin= 传给 CLI。CLI 完成归档后会 reindex + prune inbox,Emacs 端只需清理 kill-ring 并 =revert-buffer= 刷新 inbox buffer。

*** 生命周期与 CLI

卡片生命周期命令(=touch= / =archive= / =restore= / =review=)作用于人类域 =experiences/= 卡片,ID 从当前 buffer 的 =:ID:= 属性取;在非经验卡片 buffer 调用会提示并 abort(=custom/knowledge--ensure-experience-buffer= 守卫,排除 agenote 子域与无 ID 文件)。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/knowledge-cli :tangle no
(defun custom/knowledge-stats ()
  "显示人类知识库统计信息（调 `agenote --domain human stats`）。"
  (interactive)
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let ((buf (get-buffer-create "*agenote-stats*")))
        (with-current-buffer buf
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (custom/agenote-call-string 'human "stats"))
          (goto-char (point-min)))
        (display-buffer buf))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-open-file (file)
  "打开知识库 FILE。"
  (interactive "f知识库文件: ")
  (let ((target-file (expand-file-name file)))
    (find-file target-file)))

(defun custom/knowledge-open-inbox ()
  "打开知识库收件箱。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/knowledge-open-file custom:org-inbox-file))

(defun custom/knowledge--current-id ()
  "从当前 buffer 的 PROPERTIES 取 :ID: 值。
返回 ID 字符串或 nil（当前 buffer 非经验卡片 / 无 :ID:）。"
  (when (and (eq major-mode 'org-mode)
             (buffer-file-name))
    (org-with-wide-buffer
     (goto-char (point-min))
     (when (re-search-forward "^:ID:\\s-+\\([0-9]\\{8\\}-[0-9]\\{6\\}\\)" nil t)
       (match-string 1)))))

(defun custom/knowledge--ensure-experience-buffer ()
  "确保当前 buffer 是人类域经验卡片，返回其 :ID:。
否则 message 报错并返回 nil。人类域 = buffer 文件位于
`custom:org-knowledge-directory' 之下（不含 agenote 子域）。"
  (let ((id (custom/knowledge--current-id))
        (file (buffer-file-name))
        (exp-dir (expand-file-name custom:org-knowledge-directory))
        (agenote-dir (expand-file-name "agenote" custom:org-directory)))
    (cond
     ((null file)
      (message "当前 buffer 无关联文件") nil)
     ((string-prefix-p agenote-dir (expand-file-name file))
      (message "当前卡片在 agenote 子域；人类域操作请打开 experiences/ 下的卡片") nil)
     ((not (string-prefix-p exp-dir (expand-file-name file)))
      (message "当前文件不在 experiences/ 下") nil)
     ((null id)
      (message "当前卡片无 :ID: 属性") nil)
     (t id))))

(defun custom/knowledge-touch (&optional used-only)
  "更新当前经验卡片的 LAST_USED + LAST_VERIFIED（人类域,调 `agenote touch <ID>'）。
带前缀参数 USED-ONLY 时只更新 LAST_USED。"
  (interactive "P")
  (let ((id (custom/knowledge--ensure-experience-buffer)))
    (when id
      (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
          (let ((result (apply #'custom/agenote-call
                               'human "touch" id
                               (when used-only '("--used-only")))))
            (if (zerop (plist-get result :status))
                (message "已 touch: %s%s" id (if used-only " (仅 USED)" ""))
              (message "touch 失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
        (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))))

(defun custom/knowledge-archive (reason)
  "归档当前经验卡片（人类域,调 `agenote archive <ID> --reason REASON'）。
卡片被移到 archived/，可通过 `custom/knowledge-restore' 恢复。"
  (interactive "s归档原因: ")
  (let ((id (custom/knowledge--ensure-experience-buffer)))
    (when id
      (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
          (let* ((result (custom/agenote-call 'human "archive" id "--reason" reason)))
            (if (zerop (plist-get result :status))
                (message "已归档: %s (%s)" id reason)
              (message "归档失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
        (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))))

(defun custom/knowledge-restore (id)
  "恢复已归档的经验卡片（人类域,调 `agenote restore <ID>'）。
ID 通过 completing-read 从归档列表选取（若可获取），否则手动输入。"
  (interactive
   (list (read-string "恢复的卡片 ID: "
                      (when-let* ((cur (custom/knowledge--current-id)))
                        cur))))
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let ((result (custom/agenote-call 'human "restore" id)))
        (if (zerop (plist-get result :status))
            (message "已恢复: %s" id)
          (message "恢复失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-review ()
  "审查当前经验卡片（人类域,调 `agenote review <ID>'），输出显示在专用 buffer。"
  (interactive)
  (let ((id (custom/knowledge--ensure-experience-buffer)))
    (when id
      (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
          (let ((buf (get-buffer-create "*agenote-review*"))
                (result (custom/agenote-call 'human "review" id)))
            (with-current-buffer buf
              (erase-buffer)
              (insert (plist-get result :stdout))
              (goto-char (point-min)))
            (display-buffer buf))
        (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))))

(defun custom/knowledge-memory (arg)
  "人类域记忆系统概览或操作。
无前缀：调 `agenote --domain human memory' 显示概览到 buffer。
前缀 ARG 非 nil：让用户输入 --flag 参数(自由文本,透传给 CLI)。"
  (interactive "P")
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let* ((extra-args (if arg
                             (let ((input (read-string
                                           "memory 参数 (如 --stale, --type feedback, 留空显示概览): ")))
                               (split-string-and-unquote input))
                           nil))
             (result (apply #'custom/agenote-call 'human "memory" extra-args))
             (buf (get-buffer-create "*agenote-memory*")))
        (with-current-buffer buf
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (plist-get result :stdout))
          (goto-char (point-min)))
        (display-buffer buf))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-connect (id-a id-b &optional desc)
  "双向链接两张卡片（人类域,调 `agenote connect ID_A ID_B --desc DESC'）。
ID-A 默认取当前 buffer 的 :ID:，ID-B 与 DESC 在 minibuffer 收集。"
  (interactive
   (let* ((a (or (custom/knowledge--current-id)
                 (read-string "主卡片 ID: ")))
          (b (read-string "链接到卡片 ID: "))
          (d (read-string "描述 (可选): ")))
     (list a b d)))
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let* ((extra-args (delq nil (list id-a id-b
                                         (unless (string-empty-p desc) "--desc")
                                         (unless (string-empty-p desc) desc))))
             (result (apply #'custom/agenote-call 'human "connect" extra-args)))
        (if (zerop (plist-get result :status))
            (message "已链接: %s ↔ %s" id-a id-b)
          (message "链接失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-merge (primary-id secondary-ids reason)
  "合并卡片：把 SECONDARY-IDS 并入 PRIMARY-ID（人类域,调 `agenote merge'）。
SECONDARY-IDS 为空格分隔的多个 ID，REASON 记录合并原因。"
  (interactive
   (let* ((p (or (custom/knowledge--current-id)
                 (read-string "主卡片 ID（保留）: ")))
          (s (read-string "次卡片 ID（合并后删除，多个用空格）: "))
          (r (read-string "合并原因: ")))
     (list p (split-string s) r)))
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let* ((extra-args (append (list primary-id) secondary-ids
                                 (unless (string-empty-p reason)
                                   (list "--reason" reason))))
             (result (apply #'custom/agenote-call 'human "merge" extra-args)))
        (if (zerop (plist-get result :status))
            (message "已合并 %d 张到 %s" (length secondary-ids) primary-id)
          (message "合并失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-deduplicate ()
  "检测重复卡片（人类域,异步调 `agenote deduplicate'），结果显示在 buffer。"
  (interactive)
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (custom/agenote-call-async 'human "deduplicate")
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-lint (arg)
  "格式校验所有经验卡片（人类域,异步调 `agenote lint'）。
无前缀：仅检查并显示报告。
前缀 ARG 非 nil：调 `agenote lint --fix' 自动修复。"
  (interactive "P")
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let ((args (if arg '("--fix") '("--check"))))
        (apply #'custom/agenote-call-async 'human "lint" args)
        (when arg (message "正在自动修复格式问题...")))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge-commit (summary)
  "提交知识库变更到 git（人类域,异步调 `agenote commit -m SUMMARY'）。
index.json 由 .gitignore 排除，仅提交卡片与 MEMORY 变更。"
  (interactive "s提交总结: ")
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (progn
        (custom/agenote-call-async 'human "commit" "-m" summary)
        (message "正在提交: %s" summary))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))

(defun custom/knowledge--setup-agenda ()
  "将经验目录加入 agenda 自定义命令。"
  (add-to-list
   'org-agenda-custom-commands
   '("K" "知识库回顾"
     ((tags "difficulties|lessons"
            ((org-agenda-files (list (expand-file-name "experiences" custom:org-directory)))
             (org-agenda-sorting-strategy '(timestamp-down))))))
   t))

(with-eval-after-load 'org-agenda
  (custom/knowledge--setup-agenda))

#+end_src

*** 知识库浏览(统一 mode)

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/knowledge-browse :tangle no
(defconst custom:knowledge-browse--buffer-name "*knowledge-browse*")

(defun custom/knowledge-list-cards (domain &optional recent)
  "通过 agenote CLI 返回 DOMAIN 的卡片列表。
RECENT 非 nil 时只请求最近的 RECENT 条；顺序和筛选完全由 CLI 决定。"
  (let* ((args (append (when recent
                         (list "--recent" (number-to-string recent)))
                       (list "--json")))
         (result (apply #'custom/agenote-call domain "list" args))
         (status (plist-get result :status))
         (output (plist-get result :stdout)))
    (unless (eq status 0)
      (error "agenote list 失败: %s" (plist-get result :stderr)))
    (let ((json-object-type 'plist)
          (json-array-type 'list)
          (json-key-type 'keyword))
      (if (string-empty-p output)
          nil
        (json-read-from-string output)))))

(defvar-local custom--knowledge-card-domain nil)
(defvar-local custom--knowledge-card-id nil)

(defun custom/knowledge-card-refresh (&rest _)
  "通过 CLI 刷新当前卡片详情。"
  (let* ((result
          (custom/agenote-call
           custom--knowledge-card-domain "get" custom--knowledge-card-id))
         (inhibit-read-only t))
    (erase-buffer)
    (insert (if (eq (plist-get result :status) 0)
                (plist-get result :stdout)
              (format "agenote get 失败:\n%s" (plist-get result :stderr))))
    (goto-char (point-min))))

(defun custom/knowledge-show-card (domain id)
  "用只读 Org buffer 展示 DOMAIN 中 ID 对应的卡片。"
  (let ((buffer (get-buffer-create "*knowledge-card*")))
    (with-current-buffer buffer
      (org-mode)
      (setq-local custom--knowledge-card-domain domain
                  custom--knowledge-card-id id
                  revert-buffer-function #'custom/knowledge-card-refresh)
      (custom/knowledge-card-refresh)
      (view-mode 1)
      (setq header-line-format
            (format " [%s] %s · g 刷新 · q 关闭" domain id)))
    (pop-to-buffer buffer)))

(defun custom/knowledge-browse--format-line (domain card)
  "把 DOMAIN 的 CARD 格式化为可点击行。"
  (let* ((id (or (plist-get card :id) ""))
         (title (or (plist-get card :title) "(无标题)"))
         (category (or (plist-get card :category) "unknown"))
         (created (or (plist-get card :created) ""))
         (line (format "%-12s  %-10s  %s" category created title))
         (map (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key map (kbd "RET")
                (lambda ()
                  (interactive)
                  (custom/knowledge-show-card domain id)))
    (define-key map [mouse-1]
                (lambda ()
                  (interactive)
                  (custom/knowledge-show-card domain id)))
    (add-text-properties
     0 (length line)
     (list 'custom-knowledge-id id
           'keymap map
           'mouse-face 'highlight
           'help-echo (format "查看 %s" id)
           'follow-link t)
     line)
    line))

(defvar-local custom--knowledge-browse-domain 'human)
(defvar-local custom--knowledge-browse-cards nil)

(defun custom/knowledge-browse-refresh (&rest _)
  "从 CLI 重新加载当前域并重绘列表。"
  (interactive)
  (setq custom--knowledge-browse-cards
        (custom/knowledge-list-cards custom--knowledge-browse-domain))
  (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
    (erase-buffer)
    (insert (propertize
             (format "%s · %d 张\n\n"
                     custom--knowledge-browse-domain
                     (length custom--knowledge-browse-cards))
             'face 'bold))
    (dolist (card custom--knowledge-browse-cards)
      (insert (custom/knowledge-browse--format-line
               custom--knowledge-browse-domain card)
              "\n"))
    (goto-char (point-min))))

(defun custom/knowledge-browse-open-browser ()
  "用 agenote viz 打开当前域的网页可视化。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/agenote-call-async custom--knowledge-browse-domain "viz" "--open"))

(defvar custom/knowledge-browse-mode-map
  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (set-keymap-parent map special-mode-map)
    (define-key map "g" #'custom/knowledge-browse-refresh)
    (define-key map "o" #'custom/knowledge-browse-open-browser)
    (define-key map "q" #'quit-window)
    map))

(define-derived-mode custom/knowledge-browse-mode special-mode "KB-Browse"
  "CLI 驱动的知识库列表视图。"
  (setq-local revert-buffer-function #'custom/knowledge-browse-refresh))

(defun custom/knowledge-browse--open (domain)
  "打开 DOMAIN 的统一知识库列表。"
  (let ((buffer (get-buffer-create custom:knowledge-browse--buffer-name)))
    (with-current-buffer buffer
      (custom/knowledge-browse-mode)
      (setq custom--knowledge-browse-domain domain)
      (custom/knowledge-browse-refresh))
    (pop-to-buffer buffer)))

(defun custom/knowledge-browse-human ()
  "打开人类域知识库列表。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/knowledge-browse--open 'human))

(defun custom/knowledge-browse-agenote ()
  "打开 agenote 域知识库列表。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/knowledge-browse--open 'agenote))

(defun custom/knowledge-viz-open-browser ()
  "在浏览器打开人类域知识库可视化。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/agenote-call-async 'human "viz" "--open"))
#+end_src

*** 健康度面板

agenote CLI 集成:在 Emacs 内浏览 =~/Documents/Org/agenote/= 下的子域视图。

健康度面板（agenote health，人类域）
+ =agenote health= 输出本身已是结构化人类可读文本（节标题 / 指标行带阈值与 ✅），无需重新解析。
+ 直接展示在 ~special-mode~ buffer， ~g~ 调 =agenote health --quality= 取更全数据（含内容长度 / 质量问题节）。

注意：agenote health 只读 *人类域* （ ~experiences/~ ），agent 子域健康度需另查 ~index.json~ （见浏览视图）。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref org/agenote-health :tangle no
(define-derived-mode custom/agenote-health-mode special-mode "agenote-health"
  "展示 `agenote health' 知识库健康度报告（人类域）。
\\{custom/agenote-health-mode-map}")

(defvar custom/agenote-health-mode-map
  (let ((m (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key m "g" #'custom/agenote-health)
    m))

(defun custom/agenote--refresh-health-buffer ()
  "重新调 `agenote --domain human health --quality' 并填充当前 health buffer。"
  (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
    (erase-buffer)
    (insert (or (custom/agenote-call-string 'human "health" "--quality")
                "（agenote 命令不可用）"))
    (goto-char (point-min))))

;;;###autoload
(defun custom/agenote-health ()
  "打开人类知识库健康度面板 *agenote-health*（调 `agenote --domain human health --quality'）。
`g' 刷新。"
  (interactive)
  (if (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)
      (let ((buf (get-buffer-create "*agenote-health*")))
        (with-current-buffer buf
          (unless (eq major-mode 'custom/agenote-health-mode)
            (custom/agenote-health-mode))
          (custom/agenote--refresh-health-buffer))
        (switch-to-buffer buf))
    (message "未找到 agenote 命令")))
#+end_src

* 键位与补全
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: keys-completion
:END:

前缀声明与跨域基础键(=C-x= / =M-s= / =C-c w= / =C-c h=)+ IDE 风格全局直达键 + which-key 本体加载 + helpful 增强帮助 + winner 窗口布局撤销 + 补全框架全家桶(Vertico / Corfu / Consult)。功能相关的 =C-c x= 前缀键已分散到各功能域(见各域末尾)。

** Which-key 本体与辅助包
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: which-key-runtime
:END:

加载 which-key 本体并配置显示参数;helpful 增强帮助系统;winner 窗口布局撤销 / 重做。

+ which-key 中文描述注册由 *界面与外观 → 右键菜单与 Which-key 中文化* 域处理( =custom/which-key-apply-descriptions= + =custom/register-pending-wk-descs= )
+ *中文字符对齐修复*: which-key 3.6.1 已内置 =string-width= 计算,但自动列数在底部 side-window 下仍可能导致中文描述列宽不等。这里用 =advice-add :override= 替换 =which-key--pad-column= ,在底部多列布局下固定为等宽分栏(默认 =custom:which-key-grid-columns 3= 列)
+ =which-key-idle-delay 0.3= :延迟显示,避免干扰快速操作
+ =winner-dont-bind-my-keys t= :避免 winner 抢占 =C-c ←= / =C-c →= (本配置在 =C-c w= 体系下手动绑定 winner-undo / redo)

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 中文菜单固定三列网格布局:底部 side-window 下等宽分栏,
;; 避免 which-key 自动列数导致中文描述列宽不等。
(defconst custom:which-key-grid-columns 3
  "Which-key 中文菜单默认使用的固定网格列数。")

(defun custom/which-key--fixed-grid-layout-p ()
  "判断当前是否启用固定多列 which-key 网格布局。"
  (and (boundp 'which-key-max-display-columns)
       (integerp which-key-max-display-columns)
       (> which-key-max-display-columns 1)
       (boundp 'which-key-popup-type)
       (eq which-key-popup-type 'side-window)
       (boundp 'which-key-side-window-location)
       (eq which-key-side-window-location 'bottom)))

(defun custom/which-key--target-grid-column-width (available-width)
  "根据 AVAILABLE-WIDTH 计算固定网格布局下每列的目标宽度。"
  (if (custom/which-key--fixed-grid-layout-p)
      (let* ((columns which-key-max-display-columns)
             (gaps (max 0 (1- columns))))
        (/ (max 1 (- available-width gaps)) columns))
    available-width))

;; 中文宽度兼容层(私有 API override)见 [[#compatibility][实验性与待确认]] 的 =compat/which-key-width= 片段。
<<compat/which-key-width>>

(use-package which-key
  :demand t
  :config
  (which-key-mode 1)
  (setq which-key-idle-delay 0.3
        which-key-prefix-prefix ""
        which-key-allow-multiple-replacements t
        which-key-max-description-length 28
        ;; 中文条目在终端里更适合稳定的等宽分栏布局:默认三列,超宽屏可升到四列,窄窗口可降回两列。
        which-key-max-display-columns custom:which-key-grid-columns
        which-key-min-column-description-width 16
        which-key-add-column-padding 1
        which-key-min-display-lines 10
        which-key-side-window-max-height 0.38
        which-key-sort-order #'which-key-prefix-then-key-order
        which-key-C-h-map-prompt
        " \\<which-key-C-h-map>\\[which-key-show-next-page-cycle]/\\[which-key-show-previous-page-cycle] 翻页,\\[which-key-show-standard-help] 帮助,\\[which-key-abort] 退出")
  ;; 用基于 `string-width' 的中文友好版覆盖 which-key 原生 `which-key--pad-column',
  ;; 并在固定网格布局下按等宽分栏。加 fboundp 守卫:which-key 已并入 Emacs 30+
  ;; 内置,未来若内部函数改名/移除,静默跳过而非崩溃。
  (custom/which-key-install-width-fix))

(use-package helpful
  :defer t
  :bind (([remap describe-function] . helpful-callable)
         ([remap describe-command]  . helpful-command)
         ([remap describe-variable] . helpful-variable)
         ([remap describe-key]      . helpful-key)))

(use-package winner
  :custom
  (winner-dont-bind-my-keys t)
  :config
  (setq winner-boring-buffers
        (append winner-boring-buffers
                '("*Completions*" "*Help*" "*Apropos*" "*which-key*")))
  (winner-mode 1))
#+end_src

** 前缀声明与跨域基础键
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: keybindings
:END:

Dashboard 前缀摘要的唯一声明源 + 真正跨多个功能域的基础键位。功能相关的 =C-c x= 前缀键已分散到各功能域(见各域末尾),不在此处集中。

+ *14 个前缀声明* :: =custom/declare-binding-prefix= 是 Dashboard 前缀摘要的唯一来源,必须集中。前缀下具体绑定随其功能域。
+ *=C-x*= / *=M-s*= / *=C-c w*= / *=C-c h*= :: 引用的命令跨多个功能域(文件 / 缓冲区 / 搜索 / 窗口 / 终端 / 帮助),无单一归属,集中在本块。
