*** Guix 包管理 (Emacs-Guix)

[[https://git.savannah.gnu.org/cgit/guix/emacs-guix.git/][Emacs-Guix]] ( =guix.el= ) 是 GNU Guix 包管理器的 Emacs 接口。 =M-x guix= 提供 Magit-like popup 入口,可管理 profile、generations、包和服务。本配置启用三项功能:

+ *管理界面* :: =M-x guix= 的 popup 与 list/info 接口
+ *guix-devel-mode* :: scheme-mode 辅助 minor mode,处理 Guix =.scm= 包定义。手动启用,不挂全局 =scheme-mode-hook=,避免影响非 Guix 的 scheme 文件。
+ *guix-prettify-mode* :: 把 =/gnu/store/<hash>-name= 的 hash 部分显示为 =…=

性能策略: 不纳入 daemon 预热白名单 (低频管理工具),用 =:defer t= 按需加载。 =global-guix-prettify-mode= 在 Guix 环境内启用 (非 Guix 环境下无 store 路径可美化)。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package guix
  :defer t
  :when custom:in-guix-environment-p
  :commands (guix
             guix-search-by-regexp
             guix-installed-user-packages
             guix-generations
             guix-prettify-mode
             global-guix-prettify-mode
             guix-devel-mode)
  :config
  (global-guix-prettify-mode 1))

(custom/bind "C-c a x x" #'guix                          "Guix 管理界面"    "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a x s" #'guix-search-by-regexp         "搜索包"           "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a x i" #'guix-installed-user-packages  "已安装用户包"     "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a x g" #'guix-generations              "Profile 代系"     "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a x p" #'guix-prettify-mode            "切换 store 美化"  "生活应用")
(custom/bind "C-c a x d" #'guix-devel-mode               "切换 devel 模式"  "生活应用")
#+end_src

** Guix 集成与 daemon 预热
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: guix-daemon
:END:

Guix 环境检测、shepherd daemon 集成、外部工具加速范式。

+ =custom:in-guix-environment-p= :检测 Guix 环境
+ =exec-path= 在 early-init.el 已扩充(Guix profile bin + ~/.local/bin)
+ 外部命令只缓存命令名，调用时通过当前 =exec-path= 解析，避免长驻 daemon 固定到旧 Guix generation
+ daemon 由 shepherd 的 =emacs-daemon= 服务管理( =emacs --fg-daemon= ),init.el 不重复启用 =server-mode= (仅 standalone 时启用)
+ *预热策略*:daemon 启动时间不敏感，按冷 =require= 基准白名单预载常用交互 feature。不会启动 LSP、聊天或 PDF 子进程；应用型包仍按需加载。
+ 当前白名单消除的本机冷首用成本约为:Magit 0.37s、Org-roam 0.18s、Helpful/TRAMP 0.17s、Apheleia 0.05s、Embark 0.04s。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when custom:in-guix-environment-p
  (message "[custom-config] Guix 环境: %s" custom:guix-profile))

(defconst custom:daemon-warmup-features
  '(org-agenda org-capture org-roam
               apheleia embark magit helpful tramp cape)
  "daemon 启动阶段预载的常用交互 feature 白名单。")

(defun custom/daemon-warmup ()
  "同步预载 `custom:daemon-warmup-features'，让首个 client 直接复用。"
  (let ((gc-cons-threshold most-positive-fixnum)
        (gc-cons-percentage 0.6)
        failed)
    (dolist (feature custom:daemon-warmup-features)
      (condition-case err
          (unless (require feature nil t)
            (push (cons feature "feature not found") failed))
        (error
         (push (cons feature (error-message-string err)) failed))))
    (when (fboundp 'custom/dashboard-prime-caches)
      (custom/dashboard-prime-caches))
    (garbage-collect)
    (when failed
      (message "[custom-config] daemon 预热失败: %S" (nreverse failed)))
    (null failed)))
#+end_src

** Dashboard
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: dashboard
:END:

启动仪表盘:集成待办列表、文件管理器、前缀键概览和鼠标工作流提示。

Dashboard 不依赖第三方 =dashboard= 包:自建 =custom/dashboard-mode= (derived from =special-mode=) + 纯函数渲染引擎。普通内容基于 =string-width= 列模型；固定 banner 字符集按单终端单元计数，规避 TTY CJK 宽度表与终端实际渲染不一致。Dashboard 依赖知识库、帮助、颜色方案和 frame 接口,因此固定在最后一个配置域加载。跨域调用保留 =fboundp= 守卫,但正常路径由文档顺序保证依赖已就绪。

+ Dashboard 调用的跨域函数:
  - =custom/knowledge-list-cards= / =custom/knowledge-show-card= (知识库卡片,走 agenote CLI JSON,见 [[#knowledge-ownership][职责归属]])
  - =custom/help--extract-dashboard-bindings= (快捷键卡片)
  - =custom/register-buffer-refresh!= (主题切换刷新)
  - =custom/add-server-ready-hook= (daemon/client frame 生命周期)

+ 每个 client frame 持有独立 dashboard buffer；frame 参数关联所有权，删除 frame 时同步清理，宽度与渲染代数互不污染。
+ daemon 启动阶段同步预热初始缓存；知识库缓存过期后以 stale-while-revalidate 异步更新，UI 不等待 agenote CLI。

*** 六层架构

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 层   | noweb ref                   | 职责                                       | 副作用       |
|------+-----------------------------+--------------------------------------------+--------------|
| 核心 | =system/dashboard-core=       | face 定义、buffer 常量、TTL 缓存原语       | face         |
| 测量 | =system/dashboard-measure=    | 列模型:measure / pad / truncate            | 纯函数       |
| 注册 | =system/dashboard-registry= | 布局声明 + 卡片 spec 注册表 + tier 判定    | 纯函数       |
| 引擎 | =system/dashboard-engine=     | 三态卡片 + 错误隔离 + 响应式拼接           | 纯函数       |
| 卡片 | =system/dashboard-cards=      | 7 张卡片 fetch/render + IO/计算            | IO           |
| 生命 | =system/dashboard-lifecycle=  | mode、render、frame 状态机、防抖、主题刷新 | 唯一副作用层 |

设计要点:

- 卡片契约: =fetch= 无参 → =(:ok DATA)|(:error MSG)|loading= 三态; =render (DATA WIDTH)= 是纯函数返回行列表,行宽 ≤ WIDTH。空 / 加载中 / 错误 三态可区分,杜绝"假空状态"。
- 错误隔离: 单张卡片任何异常被引擎包成错误卡片,绝不拖垮整页。
- 测量层: 普通内容基于 =string-width= 列模型；固定 banner 字符集按字符数计算实际终端单元(见 [[测量层][测量层章节]])。
- 零临时 buffer: 卡片渲染器是纯函数,拼接是字符串操作,消除了旧实现每刷新 6 次 =generate-new-buffer= 的开销,且每张卡片可独立进 ERT。
- 宽度显式传参: 删除旧的 =--forced-width= / =--override-margin= 动态绑定,宽度沿调用链显式传递。

*** 核心定义

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-core :tangle no

(require 'calendar)

(defconst custom:dashboard-buffer-name "*dashboard*"
  "Dashboard buffer 名称前缀。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--frame-sequence 0
  "用于生成 per-frame dashboard buffer 名称的递增序号。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--generation 0
  "Dashboard 数据失效代数；缓存变化时递增。")

(defvar-local custom/dashboard--owner-frame nil
  "当前 dashboard buffer 所属 frame。")

(defvar-local custom/dashboard--rendered-width nil
  "当前 dashboard buffer 上次渲染的窗口宽度。")

(defvar-local custom/dashboard--rendered-generation -1
  "当前 dashboard buffer 上次渲染的数据代数。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-cache-ttl 60
  "Dashboard 数据源缓存的有效期（秒）。
超过此时间则视为过期，下次取用时重新计算。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-dual-margin 4
  "双列布局左右页面边距列数。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-dual-gap 3
  "双列布局左右卡片之间的间隔列数。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-card-inner-padding 2
  "Dashboard 卡片内容区左右统一保留的内边距列数。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-shortcuts-column-gap 4
  "Dashboard 快捷键双列之间的固定间隔列数。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-refresh-debounce 0.3
  "window-size-change 触发 dashboard 重新渲染的 idle 延迟（秒）。
连续窗口尺寸变化会被合并为一次刷新。")

(defface custom/dashboard-card-bar
  '((t :inherit font-lock-comment-face :slant normal :weight normal))
  "Dashboard 卡片边框（左竖线 `|' + 标题分隔线 `-'）的 face。
边框用宽度恒为 1 列的 ASCII 字符画左框与标题下分隔线。该 face 只设前景、
不设背景，无论中文/图标占几列、窗口多宽，左竖线永远在第 0 列对齐。继承
`font-lock-comment-face' 让边框呈柔和灰调，主题切换自动适配。")

(defface custom/dashboard-card-title
  '((t :inherit font-lock-keyword-face :weight bold))
  "Dashboard 卡片标题的 face。从 `font-lock-keyword-face' 继承强调色 + 加粗。")

(defface custom/dashboard-todo-title
  '((t :inherit default))
  "Dashboard TODO 标题的 face。标题用普通前景色；状态和日期各自独立上色。")

(defface custom/dashboard-dim
  '((t :inherit shadow))
  "Dashboard 弱化信息的 face:快捷键提示、空状态、加载中。")

(defface custom/dashboard-error
  '((t :inherit error))
  "Dashboard 错误卡片的 face:数据源加载失败时显示。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--todo-cache nil
  "TODO 项缓存，结构为 (KEY TIME . DATA)，KEY 为 MAX-ITEMS。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache nil
  "知识库条目缓存，结构为 (KEY TIME . DATA)，KEY 为 MAX-ITEMS。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--projects-cache nil
  "项目列表缓存，结构为 (KEY TIME . DATA)，KEY 恒为 nil（无查询参数）。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--knowledge-error nil
  "最近一次异步知识库更新的错误信息。")

(defvar custom/dashboard--knowledge-process nil
  "当前异步知识库更新进程。")

(defun custom/dashboard--cache-valid-p (cache key)
  "判断 CACHE (KEY TIME . DATA) 是否对 KEY 在 TTL 内有效。"
  (and (consp cache)
       (eq (car cache) key)
       (let ((time (cadr cache)))
         (and time
              (<= (float-time (time-since time))
                  custom:dashboard-cache-ttl)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--cached (cache-var key compute-fn)
  "返回 CACHE-VAR 中 KEY 对应的缓存数据；失效则调用 COMPUTE-FN 重算并缓存。
CACHE-VAR 是缓存变量符号，缓存结构统一为 (KEY TIME . DATA)。
COMPUTE-FN 为无参函数或函数符号，返回要缓存的数据。"
  (let ((cache (symbol-value cache-var)))
    (if (custom/dashboard--cache-valid-p cache key)
        (cddr cache)
      (let ((data (funcall compute-fn)))
        (set cache-var (cons key (cons (current-time) data)))
        data))))

(defun custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache ()
  "清除 dashboard 所有数据源缓存，并标记下次刷新重算。
可在 Org 保存、Magit 操作等改变底层数据后调用。"
  (setq custom/dashboard--todo-cache nil
        custom/dashboard--projects-cache nil
        custom/dashboard--knowledge-error nil)
  (when custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache
    (setcar (cdr custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache) nil))
  (cl-incf custom/dashboard--generation))

#+end_src

*** 测量层

测量层是右列对齐的基石。普通内容统一使用 =string-width= 列模型，放弃与 =window-body-width= 无法直接运算的 GUI 像素宽度。固定 banner 是唯一例外：其 block/box-drawing 字符在 Foot 中各占一个终端单元，但 Emacs TTY 的 CJK 宽度表可能把同一字符计为两列，因此按字符数计算。列模型下 Nerd Font PUA 图标至多 1 列误差(见重设计文档 §1 #9),但卡片布局单位一致、不串位。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-measure :tangle no

(defun custom/dashboard--measure (text)
  "返回 TEXT 的显示宽度（列数）。
普通 dashboard 内容基于 `string-width'，GUI 与 TTY / batch 统一为列模型；
固定 banner 字符画由 `custom/dashboard--banner-art-width' 单独度量。

为何不用像素测量（`string-pixel-width'）：原设计曾尝试 GUI 像素 + TTY 列双模，
但 `string-pixel-width' 返回像素值，而卡片宽度算术（=window-body-width= 列数
减边距）、规则线（`make-string' 按字符数）等全部以列为单位。两种单位混用导致
标题无间距、规则线截断、banner 居中失效（见 commit 修复前的 GUI 截图）。列模型
下 `string-width' 对 Nerd Font PUA 图标算 1 列但字形可能占 2 列像素，存在至多
1 列的右栏平移误差（见重设计文档 §1 #9），但所有算术单位一致、布局不会串位。
该 1 列误差属于字体度量边界，不影响卡片间的列模型约束。"
  (string-width (or text "")))

(defun custom/dashboard--fill (width)
  "生成 WIDTH 列宽的 ASCII 空格填充。"
  (if (<= width 0)
      ""
    (make-string width ?\s)))

(defun custom/dashboard--pad-to (text width)
  "把 TEXT 右填充到恰好 WIDTH 列（不足补空格，超出原样返回不截断）。"
  (let ((slack (- width (custom/dashboard--measure text))))
    (if (> slack 0)
        (concat text (custom/dashboard--fill slack))
      text)))

(defun custom/dashboard--truncate (text width)
  "截断 TEXT 到 WIDTH 列，超宽补 \"...\"。
三个 ASCII 点宽度恒定（3 列），全局统一，避免 \"…\" 的宽度随字体漂移。"
  (if (<= (custom/dashboard--measure text) width)
      text
    (truncate-string-to-width text width 0 nil "...")))

(defun custom/dashboard--spread (left right width)
  "LEFT 靠左、RIGHT 靠右，中间填充到恰好 WIDTH 列。"
  (let ((gap (- width
                (custom/dashboard--measure left)
                (custom/dashboard--measure right))))
    (concat left (custom/dashboard--fill (max 0 gap)) right)))

#+end_src

*** 布局声明与卡片注册表

布局变成数据: 调整卡片顺序 / 增删只动这份声明,引擎不变。每行 1 个 id = 全宽卡片, 2 个 id = 双列并排。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-registry :tangle no
(defcustom custom:dashboard-layout
  '((todo clock)
    (recents projects)
    (knowledge bookmarks)
    (shortcuts))
  "Dashboard 行布局声明。
每行 1 个 id = 全宽卡片；2 个 id = 双列并排（宽度足够时）。改布局只动这份
声明，引擎不变。id 必须在 `custom:dashboard-card-specs' 中已注册。"
  :type '(repeat (repeat symbol)))

(defcustom custom:dashboard-dual-breakpoint 110
  "窗口正文宽度（列）低于此值时，双列布局降级为单列堆叠。
与 modeline tier 的窄窗口降级哲学一致。"
  :type 'integer)

(defvar custom:dashboard-card-specs nil
  "已注册的卡片 spec 列表。
每个 spec 是 plist：:id / :title / :icon / :key / :accent / :size / :fetch / :render。
  :fetch  无参函数 → (:ok DATA) | (:error MSG) | 符号 loading
  :render (DATA WIDTH) → 行字符串列表（纯函数，每行 display-width ≤ WIDTH）
  :size   条目数上限（传给 fetch）
卡片 spec 由 `custom/dashboard-define-card' 注册，引擎通过
`custom/dashboard--card-spec' 按 id 查找。")

(defun custom/dashboard-define-card (id &rest spec)
  "注册或更新 id 为 ID 的卡片 spec。
SPEC 是 plist，关键字见 `custom:dashboard-card-specs'。幂等：重复注册同 id
覆盖旧 spec。spec plist 的 :id 槽会被设为 ID。"
  (plist-put spec :id id)
  (let ((existing (custom/dashboard--card-spec id)))
    (setq custom:dashboard-card-specs
          (cons spec
                (delq existing custom:dashboard-card-specs))))
  spec)

(defun custom/dashboard--card-spec (id)
  "返回 id 为 ID 的卡片 spec，未注册时返回 nil。
`custom:dashboard-card-specs' 是 spec plist 的列表，每个 plist 的 :id 槽
持有其标识，故用 `cl-find' 按 :id 匹配而非 `assq'。"
  (cl-find id custom:dashboard-card-specs :key (lambda (s) (plist-get s :id)) :test #'eq))

(defun custom/dashboard--tier ()
  "返回当前布局 tier：dual（宽窗口双列）或 single（窄窗口单列）。"
  (if (>= (window-body-width) custom:dashboard-dual-breakpoint)
      'dual
    'single))

#+end_src

*** 渲染引擎

引擎统一包边框（左竖线 + 标题 + 规则线）、三态降级与错误隔离,卡片渲染器无需重复处理这些。单张卡片任何异常被隔离为错误卡片(见 A2 修复),绝不拖垮整页。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-engine :tangle no
(defun custom/dashboard--bar ()
  "返回左竖线字符（带边框 face）。宽度恒为 1 列。"
  (propertize "|" 'face 'custom/dashboard-card-bar))

(defun custom/dashboard--rule (body-width)
  "返回标题下分隔线（body-width 列的 `-'，带边框 face）。
列模型下 `-' 的 char-width 恒为 1，make-string N 个 `-' 的视觉宽 == N 列，
恰好等于内容区；像素模式下 `-' 同样可控。规则线右端无需对齐（边框设计本就
无右边缘），所以按列铺满已足够。"
  (propertize (make-string (max 0 body-width) ?-)
              'face 'custom/dashboard-card-bar))

(defun custom/dashboard--card-lines (spec width)
  "把 SPEC 渲染成 WIDTH 宽的完整卡片行列表（含边框/标题/规则线/正文）。
单个卡片的任何异常（fetch 抛错、render 抛错）都被隔离为错误卡片，
不影响同排其他卡片。返回行字符串列表，行间不含空行（空行由布局层加）。"
  (let* ((body-width (max 0 (- width 2)))
         (bar (custom/dashboard--bar))
         (state (condition-case err
                    (funcall (plist-get spec :fetch))
                  (error `(:error ,(error-message-string err)))))
         (body
          (pcase state
            (`(:ok ,data)
             (condition-case err
                 (or (funcall (plist-get spec :render) data body-width)
                     (list ""))
               (error
                (list (propertize (format "渲染失败：%s"
                                          (error-message-string err))
                                  'face 'custom/dashboard-error)))))
            (`(:error ,msg)
             (list (propertize (format "󰙦 暂不可用：%s" msg)
                               'face 'custom/dashboard-error)
                   (propertize "按 g 重试"
                               'face 'custom/dashboard-dim)))
            ('loading
             (list (propertize "... 加载中"
                               'face 'custom/dashboard-dim)))
            (_ (list ""))))
         (icon (propertize (or (plist-get spec :icon) "")
                           'face (or (plist-get spec :accent)
                                     'custom/dashboard-card-title)))
         (title-text (propertize (or (plist-get spec :title) "")
                                 'face 'custom/dashboard-card-title))
         (key-text (propertize (or (plist-get spec :key) "")
                               'face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
         (title-line (custom/dashboard--spread
                      (if (string-empty-p icon)
                          title-text
                        (concat icon " " title-text))
                      key-text
                      body-width))
         (rule (custom/dashboard--rule body-width)))
    (append
     (list bar)
     (list (concat bar " " title-line))
     (list (concat bar rule))
     (mapcar (lambda (line)
               (let ((trimmed (custom/dashboard--truncate line body-width)))
                 (concat bar " " trimmed)))
             body)
     (list bar))))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-row (card-ids total-width)
  "渲染一行（1 或 2 张卡片），返回行字符串列表。
2 张卡片时等高拼接：短卡片补带左竖线的空延续行；右列不补尾部填充，
消除行尾空白。"
  (pcase (length card-ids)
    (1 (custom/dashboard--card-lines
        (custom/dashboard--card-spec (car card-ids)) total-width))
    (2 (let* ((left-spec (custom/dashboard--card-spec (nth 0 card-ids)))
              (right-spec (custom/dashboard--card-spec (nth 1 card-ids)))
              (gap custom:dashboard-dual-gap)
              (left-w (max 1 (/ (- total-width gap) 2)))
              (right-w (max 1 (- total-width gap left-w)))
              (left-lines (custom/dashboard--card-lines left-spec left-w))
              (right-lines (custom/dashboard--card-lines right-spec right-w))
              (height (max (length left-lines) (length right-lines)))
              (empty-left (custom/dashboard--pad-to (custom/dashboard--bar) left-w))
              (empty-right (custom/dashboard--bar)))
         (cl-loop for i below height collect
                  (concat
                   (custom/dashboard--pad-to
                    (or (nth i left-lines) empty-left) left-w)
                   (custom/dashboard--fill gap)
                   (or (nth i right-lines) empty-right)))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--build-lines (width)
  "按当前 tier 展开布局，返回完整行字符串列表。
tier=single 时双列行拆成两个全宽卡片堆叠（响应式降级）；
tier=dual 时按 `custom:dashboard-layout' 原样。卡片间插入空行。"
  (let* ((rows (if (eq (custom/dashboard--tier) 'dual)
                   custom:dashboard-layout
                 (mapcar #'list (apply #'append custom:dashboard-layout))))
         (content-width (max 1 (- width (* 2 custom:dashboard-dual-margin))))
         (all-lines nil))
    (dolist (row rows)
      (let ((row-lines (custom/dashboard--render-row row content-width)))
        (dolist (l row-lines)
          (push l all-lines))
        (push "" all-lines)))
    (nreverse all-lines)))
#+end_src

*** 卡片实现

7 张卡片按 =fetch → (:ok|:error|loading)= + =render (DATA WIDTH) → lines= 协议实现。TTL 缓存三件套(todo/knowledge/projects)原样保留,只改外层协议包装。

+ 按钮构造: 所有可点击项用 =custom/dashboard--button= 生成带局部 keymap 的字符串,RET/mouse-1 调用回调。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-cards :tangle no
(defun custom/dashboard--apply-fixed-pitch ()
  "强制 dashboard buffer 使用固定宽度的默认 face。"
  (setq-local face-remapping-alist
              (cons '(default fixed-pitch)
                    (assq-delete-all 'default face-remapping-alist)))
  (setq-local truncate-lines t))

(defun custom/dashboard--button (label action &optional help-format)
  "返回一个带局部 keymap 的字符串，RET/mouse-1 时调用 ACTION。
ACTION 是无参函数或 interactive 命令符号。HELP-FORMAT 非 nil 时作为
help-echo 格式串（含一个 %s 占位 LABEL），否则 help-echo 显示 LABEL 本身。"
  (let ((btn (copy-sequence label))
        (keymap (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key keymap (kbd "RET")
                (lambda () (interactive) (funcall action)))
    (define-key keymap [mouse-1]
                (lambda () (interactive) (funcall action)))
    (add-text-properties
     0 (length btn)
     (list 'keymap keymap
           'face 'dashboard-items-face
           'mouse-face 'highlight
           'help-echo (format (or help-format "%s") label))
     btn)
    btn))

(defun custom/dashboard--command-button (label command)
  "返回一个调用交互命令 COMMAND 的按钮字符串。
用于 Dashboard 卡片底部「更多」跳转；RET/mouse-1 调用 COMMAND。"
  (let ((btn (copy-sequence label))
        (keymap (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key keymap (kbd "RET")
                (lambda () (interactive) (call-interactively command)))
    (define-key keymap [mouse-1]
                (lambda () (interactive) (call-interactively command)))
    (add-text-properties
     0 (length btn)
     (list 'keymap keymap
           'face 'dashboard-items-face
           'mouse-face 'highlight
           'help-echo (format "运行 %s" command))
     btn)
    btn))

(defun custom/dashboard--compact-path (path)
  "将 PATH 中间目录压缩为首字母，首尾保留完整。
隐藏目录（以 . 开头）取其第二个字符，避免 ~/./e 这种怪异的路径。"
  (let* ((parts (split-string path "/" t))
         (len (length parts)))
    (if (<= len 3)
        path
      (let* ((head (car parts))
             (file (car (last parts)))
             (middle (cl-subseq parts 1 (1- len)))
             (compressed (mapcar (lambda (d)
                                   (cond ((string-empty-p d) d)
                                         ((string-prefix-p "." d)
                                          (if (> (length d) 1)
                                              (concat "." (substring d 1 2))
                                            d))
                                         (t (substring d 0 1))))
                                 middle)))
        (concat head "/"
                (mapconcat #'identity compressed "/")
                "/" file)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--abbreviate-path (path width)
  "将 PATH 缩写以适应 WIDTH 列宽。
先压缩中间目录为首字母，仍超宽则交给测量层 `--truncate' 截断加 \"...\"。
注意：调用方需在 dashboard buffer 上下文内（命中 fixed-pitch remap）。"
  (custom/dashboard--truncate (custom/dashboard--compact-path path) width))

(defun custom/dashboard--todo-state-face (todo-state)
  "返回 TODO-STATE 在 dashboard 待办卡片中使用的标签 face。"
  (pcase todo-state
    ("TODO" 'font-lock-warning-face)
    ("NEXT" 'font-lock-type-face)
    ("INPROGRESS" 'font-lock-constant-face)
    ("WAITING" 'font-lock-comment-face)
    ("DONE" 'success)
    ("CANCELLED" 'shadow)
    (_ 'font-lock-variable-name-face)))

(defun custom/dashboard--todo-date-info (today-abs deadline-str scheduled-str
                                                   deadline-abs scheduled-abs)
  "返回 TODO 项的日期信息字符串，用于 dashboard 待办卡片。"
  (cond
   ((and deadline-abs (< deadline-abs today-abs))
    (propertize (format "  󰥕 过期%d天" (- today-abs deadline-abs))
                'face 'error))
   ((and deadline-abs (= deadline-abs today-abs))
    (propertize "  󰥕 今天截止" 'face 'error))
   (deadline-str
    (let ((diff (- deadline-abs today-abs)))
      (propertize
       (cond ((= diff 1) "  󰥕 明天截止")
             ((<= diff 3) (format "  󰥕 %d天后截止" diff))
             ((<= diff 7) (format "  󰥕 %d天后截止" diff))
             (t (format "  󰥕 %s"
                        (substring deadline-str 1 11))))
       'face (cond ((<= diff 1) 'error)
                   ((<= diff 3) 'warning)
                   ((<= diff 7) 'font-lock-variable-name-face)
                   (t 'font-lock-keyword-face)))))
   ((and scheduled-abs (< scheduled-abs today-abs))
    (propertize (format "  󰔠 过期%d天" (- today-abs scheduled-abs))
                'face 'error))
   ((and scheduled-abs (= scheduled-abs today-abs))
    (propertize (format "  󰔠 %s" (substring scheduled-str 1 11))
                'face 'error))
   (scheduled-str
    (propertize (format "  󰔠 %s" (substring scheduled-str 1 11))
                'face 'font-lock-keyword-face))
   (t "")))

(defun custom/dashboard--compute-todo-items (max-items)
  "实际扫描 `org-agenda-files' 计算未完成 TODO 项（无缓存）。
每个条目记录 (STATE TITLE DATE-INFO FILE POS)，POS 为 heading 在 file 中的
字符位置，用于点击跳转。

`ask-user-about-supersession-threat' 是函数，用 `cl-letf' 绑定其
`symbol-function'（而非 let 绑定变量——那是 dead binding，无法抑制磁盘变更
询问）。fresh daemon 首次渲染的同步 `require' 由 fetch 层的 loading 态兜底。"
  (require 'org-agenda)
  (require 'org)
  (let ((today-abs (calendar-absolute-from-gregorian (calendar-current-date)))
        (count 0)
        raw-items)
    (when (and (fboundp 'org-agenda-files)
               (ignore-errors (org-agenda-files)))
      (dolist (file (org-agenda-files))
        (when (and (< count (* max-items 2)) (file-exists-p file))
          (let ((buf (cl-letf (((symbol-function 'ask-user-about-supersession-threat)
                                (lambda (&rest _) nil)))
                       (find-file-noselect file))))
            (unwind-protect
                (with-current-buffer buf
                  (org-with-wide-buffer
                   (goto-char (point-min))
                   (while (and (< count (* max-items 2))
                               (re-search-forward org-heading-regexp nil t))
                     (let* ((heading-pos (match-beginning 0))
                            (components (org-heading-components))
                            (todo-state (nth 2 components))
                            (title (nth 4 components))
                            (scheduled (org-entry-get (point) "SCHEDULED"))
                            (deadline (org-entry-get (point) "DEADLINE")))
                       (when (and todo-state
                                  (not (member todo-state '("DONE" "CANCELLED"))))
                         (let* ((deadline-abs (and deadline
                                                   (org-time-string-to-absolute deadline)))
                                (scheduled-abs (and scheduled
                                                    (org-time-string-to-absolute scheduled)))
                                (urgency
                                 (cond
                                  ((and deadline-abs (< deadline-abs today-abs)) 0)
                                  ((and deadline-abs (= deadline-abs today-abs)) 1)
                                  ((and scheduled-abs (< scheduled-abs today-abs)) 2)
                                  (deadline-abs 3)
                                  (scheduled-abs 4)
                                  (t 5))))
                           (push (list urgency todo-state title deadline
                                       scheduled deadline-abs scheduled-abs
                                       file heading-pos)
                                 raw-items)
                           (setq count (1+ count)))))))))
            (when (buffer-live-p buf)
              (kill-buffer buf))))))
    (setq raw-items
          (sort raw-items
                (lambda (a b)
                  (or (< (car a) (car b))
                      (and (= (car a) (car b))
                           (and (nth 5 a) (nth 5 b)
                                (< (nth 5 a) (nth 5 b))))))))
    (let ((result nil)
          (n 0))
      (dolist (item raw-items)
        (when (< n max-items)
          (let* ((todo-state (nth 1 item))
                 (title (nth 2 item))
                 (deadline-str (nth 3 item))
                 (scheduled-str (nth 4 item))
                 (deadline-abs (nth 5 item))
                 (scheduled-abs (nth 6 item))
                 (file (nth 7 item))
                 (pos (nth 8 item))
                 (state-face (custom/dashboard--todo-state-face todo-state))
                 (date-info (custom/dashboard--todo-date-info
                             today-abs deadline-str scheduled-str
                             deadline-abs scheduled-abs)))
            (push (list (propertize (concat todo-state "  ") 'face state-face)
                        (propertize title 'face 'custom/dashboard-todo-title)
                        date-info
                        file pos)
                  result)
            (setq n (1+ n)))))
      (nreverse result))))

(defun custom/dashboard--todo-items (max-items)
  "返回所有未完成的 Org TODO 项。
每条 = (STATE-STR TITLE-STR DATE-INFO-STR FILE POS)。按紧急度排序，结果按
MAX-ITEMS 带 TTL 缓存（见 `custom:dashboard-cache-ttl'），可通过
`custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache' 失效。"
  (custom/dashboard--cached
   'custom/dashboard--todo-cache
   max-items
   (lambda () (custom/dashboard--compute-todo-items max-items))))

(defun custom/dashboard--defer-until-org ()
  "保证 org-agenda 在空闲时加载一次，加载后失效缓存并重绘 dashboard。
fresh daemon 首次渲染时 org-agenda 尚未加载，同步扫描会阻塞首屏；
fetch 先返回 loading，由本函数异步加载后触发重绘。幂等。"
  (unless (bound-and-true-p custom/dashboard--org-defer-pending)
    (setq custom/dashboard--org-defer-pending t)
    (run-with-idle-timer
     2 nil
     (lambda ()
       (require 'org-agenda)
       (require 'org)
       (setq custom/dashboard--org-defer-pending nil)
       (custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache)
       (when (fboundp 'custom/dashboard--refresh-if-visible)
         (custom/dashboard--refresh-if-visible))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-todo ()
  "TODO 卡片 fetch：三态。
缓存有效 → (:ok items)；org-agenda 未加载 → loading 并触发延迟加载；
否则扫描。"
  (if (custom/dashboard--cache-valid-p custom/dashboard--todo-cache 5)
      (list :ok (cddr custom/dashboard--todo-cache))
    (if (not (featurep 'org-agenda))
        (progn (custom/dashboard--defer-until-org) 'loading)
      (list :ok (custom/dashboard--todo-items 5)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-todo (items width)
  "TODO 卡片 render。每行做成按钮：跳文件 → goto-char → org-reveal → recenter。
跳转后 `pulsar-pulse' 高亮（pulsar 已全局启用）。"
  (if (null items)
      (list (custom/dashboard--command-button "> 打开 Agenda" #'org-agenda))
    (append
     (mapcar
      (lambda (item)
        (let* ((state-str (nth 0 item))
               (title (nth 1 item))
               (date-info (nth 2 item))
               (file (nth 3 item))
               (pos (nth 4 item))
               (jump (lambda ()
                       (find-file file)
                       (when pos
                         (goto-char pos)
                         (when (fboundp 'org-reveal) (org-reveal))
                         (recenter)
                         (when (fboundp 'pulsar-pulse) (pulsar-pulse)))))
               (prefix (concat "  " state-str))
               (rest (concat title date-info))
               (rest-w (max 0 (- width (custom/dashboard--measure prefix))))
               (rest-trimmed (custom/dashboard--truncate rest rest-w)))
          (custom/dashboard--button
           (concat prefix rest-trimmed) jump
           (format "查看待办 %s" title))))
      items)
     (list (custom/dashboard--command-button "> 查看 Agenda..." #'org-agenda)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-clock ()
  "计时卡片 fetch。org 未加载时返回 :error（显示打开 org 文件提示）。"
  (if (not (featurep 'org))
      (list :error "打开 org 文件后显示计时")
    (let* ((clocking-p (and (fboundp 'org-clocking-p) (org-clocking-p)))
           (today-min (if (fboundp 'org-clock-sum-today)
                          (org-clock-sum-today) 0)))
      (list :ok (list clocking-p today-min)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-clock (data width)
  "计时卡片 render。DATA = (CLOCKING-P TODAY-MIN)。"
  (let* ((clocking-p (nth 0 data))
         (today-min (nth 1 data))
         (today-str (format "%dh %dm" (/ today-min 60) (% today-min 60)))
         (jump-cmd (if clocking-p #'org-clock-goto #'org-clock-in)))
    (append
     (if clocking-p
         (let* ((clock-string (substring-no-properties (org-clock-get-clock-string)))
                (task-name (if (string-match org-ts-regexp-both clock-string)
                               (string-trim (substring clock-string (match-end 0)))
                             clock-string)))
           (list (concat (propertize "▶ 计时中：" 'face 'success)
                         task-name
                         "  "
                         (propertize today-str 'face 'font-lock-type-face))
                 (concat "今日累计："
                         (propertize today-str 'face 'font-lock-keyword-face))))
       (list "当前没有计时的任务"
             (concat "今日累计："
                     (propertize today-str 'face 'font-lock-comment-face))))
     (list (custom/dashboard--command-button
            (if clocking-p "> 跳转到计时任务..." "> 开始计时...")
            jump-cmd)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-recents ()
  "最近文件卡片 fetch。
先排除 remote 路径（避免渲染期触发 TRAMP 连接阻塞 UI），再查 `file-exists-p'
过滤已删除文件。取 4 倍候选再过滤，避免过滤后不足条目数。"
  (if (not (bound-and-true-p recentf-mode))
      (list :error "recentf 未启用")
    (let* ((size 6)
           (cands (seq-take (or (and (boundp 'recentf-list) recentf-list)
                                (default-value 'recentf-list))
                            (* 4 size)))
           (files (seq-filter (lambda (f)
                                (and (not (file-remote-p f))
                                     (file-exists-p f)))
                              cands)))
      (list :ok (seq-take files size)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-recents (files width)
  "最近文件卡片 render。每行做成按钮，点击 find-file。"
  (if (null files)
      (list (propertize "暂无最近文件" 'face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
    (mapcar
     (lambda (f)
       (let* ((display (custom/dashboard--abbreviate-path f width))
              (prefix "  ")
              (w (max 0 (- width (custom/dashboard--measure prefix)))))
         (concat prefix
                 (custom/dashboard--button
                  (custom/dashboard--truncate display w)
                  (lambda () (find-file f))
                  (format "打开 %s" f)))))
     files)))

(defun custom/dashboard--compute-known-projects ()
  "实际计算 dashboard 可展示的项目列表（无缓存）。
用内置 `project-known-project-roots' 取已知项目。"
  (delete-dups
   (seq-filter #'file-directory-p
               (mapcar #'file-name-as-directory
                       (project-known-project-roots)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--known-projects ()
  "返回 dashboard 可展示的项目列表。
回落到 `project.el'。结果带 TTL 缓存（见 `custom:dashboard-cache-ttl'），
可通过 `custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache' 失效。"
  (custom/dashboard--cached
   'custom/dashboard--projects-cache
   nil
   #'custom/dashboard--compute-known-projects))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-projects ()
  "项目卡片 fetch。"
  (list :ok (custom/dashboard--known-projects)))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-projects (projects width)
  "项目卡片 render。"
  (if (null projects)
      (list (propertize "暂无已知项目 — C-x p o 添加"
                        'face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
    (mapcar
     (lambda (p)
       (let* ((display (custom/dashboard--abbreviate-path p width))
              (prefix "  ")
              (w (max 0 (- width (custom/dashboard--measure prefix)))))
         (concat prefix
                 (custom/dashboard--button
                  (custom/dashboard--truncate display w)
                  (lambda () (find-file p))
                  (format "打开项目 %s" p)))))
     projects)))

(defun custom/dashboard--compute-recent-knowledge-entries (max-items)
  "消费 `agenote --domain human list --json' 稳定索引计算最近条目（无缓存）。
CLI 不可用时让异常上抛，由 fetch 层捕获为 :error（而非旧实现的静默吞错返回
nil——那会让 CLI 挂掉显示「暂无条目」假状态）。返回 (ID CATEGORY TITLE) 三元组。"
  (custom/dashboard--knowledge-entries-from-cards
   (custom/knowledge-list-cards 'human max-items)))

(defun custom/dashboard--knowledge-entries-from-cards (cards)
  "把 CARDS plist 列表转换为 (ID CATEGORY TITLE) 三元组。"
  (cl-loop
   for card in cards
   for id = (plist-get card :id)
   for title = (or (plist-get card :title) "(无标题)")
   for category = (or (plist-get card :category) "unknown")
   when id
   collect (list id category title)))

(defun custom/dashboard--recent-knowledge-entries (max-items)
  "返回最近的知识库条目作为 (ID CATEGORY TITLE) 三元组列表。
顺序与筛选由 agenote CLI 决定，Emacs 不递归扫描文件。结果按 MAX-ITEMS 带
TTL 缓存（见 `custom:dashboard-cache-ttl'），可通过
`custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache' 失效。"
  (custom/dashboard--cached
   'custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache
   max-items
   (lambda () (custom/dashboard--compute-recent-knowledge-entries max-items))))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-knowledge ()
  "知识库卡片 fetch：缓存过期时异步刷新并优先返回 stale 数据。"
  (let ((max-items 5))
    (cond
     ((custom/dashboard--cache-valid-p custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache
                                       max-items)
      (list :ok (cddr custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache)))
     (t
      (unless (process-live-p custom/dashboard--knowledge-process)
        (custom/dashboard--knowledge-start-async max-items))
      (cond
       ((and custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache
             (cddr custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache))
        (list :ok (cddr custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache)))
       (custom/dashboard--knowledge-error
        (list :error custom/dashboard--knowledge-error))
       (t 'loading))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--knowledge-start-async (max-items)
  "异步刷新最近 MAX-ITEMS 张知识卡片，已有进程时保持幂等。"
  (unless (process-live-p custom/dashboard--knowledge-process)
    (if-let* ((exe (custom/agenote--resolve-executable)))
        (let ((stdout (generate-new-buffer " *dashboard-knowledge-stdout*"))
              (stderr (generate-new-buffer " *dashboard-knowledge-stderr*")))
          (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-error nil)
          (condition-case err
              (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-process
                    (make-process
                     :name "dashboard-knowledge"
                     :buffer stdout
                     :stderr stderr
                     :command (list exe "--domain" "human" "list"
                                    "--recent" (number-to-string max-items) "--json")
                     :connection-type 'pipe
                     :noquery t
                     :sentinel
                     (lambda (process _event)
                       (when (memq (process-status process) '(exit signal))
                         (if (zerop (process-exit-status process))
                             (condition-case parse-error
                                 (let* ((json-object-type 'plist)
                                        (json-array-type 'list)
                                        (json-key-type 'keyword)
                                        (output (if-let* ((buffer (process-buffer process))
                                                          ((buffer-live-p buffer)))
                                                    (with-current-buffer buffer
                                                      (buffer-string))
                                                  ""))
                                        (cards (unless (string-empty-p output)
                                                 (json-read-from-string output)))
                                        (entries
                                         (custom/dashboard--knowledge-entries-from-cards
                                          cards)))
                                   (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-cache
                                         (cons max-items
                                               (cons (current-time) entries))
                                         custom/dashboard--knowledge-error nil))
                               (error
                                (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-error
                                      (error-message-string parse-error))))
                           (let ((message
                                  (and (buffer-live-p stderr)
                                       (string-trim
                                        (with-current-buffer stderr
                                          (buffer-string))))))
                             (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-error
                                   (if (string-empty-p (or message ""))
                                       "agenote process failed"
                                     message))))
                         (when (eq custom/dashboard--knowledge-process process)
                           (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-process nil))
                         (when-let* ((buffer (process-buffer process))
                                     ((buffer-live-p buffer)))
                           (kill-buffer buffer))
                         (when (buffer-live-p stderr)
                           (kill-buffer stderr))
                         (cl-incf custom/dashboard--generation)
                         (condition-case refresh-error
                             (custom/dashboard--refresh-if-visible)
                           (error
                            (message "[dashboard] knowledge refresh failed: %s"
                                     (error-message-string refresh-error))))))))
            (error
             (when (buffer-live-p stdout)
               (kill-buffer stdout))
             (when (buffer-live-p stderr)
               (kill-buffer stderr))
             (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-process nil
                   custom/dashboard--knowledge-error
                   (error-message-string err))
             (cl-incf custom/dashboard--generation))))
      (setq custom/dashboard--knowledge-error "agenote executable not found"))))

(defun custom/dashboard-prime-caches ()
  "在 daemon 启动阶段同步预热 Dashboard 的常用数据缓存。"
  (dolist (fn (list (lambda () (custom/dashboard--todo-items 5))
                    #'custom/dashboard--known-projects
                    (lambda () (custom/dashboard--recent-knowledge-entries 5))))
    (condition-case err
        (funcall fn)
      (error
       (message "[dashboard] cache warmup failed: %s"
                (error-message-string err))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-knowledge (entries width)
  "知识库卡片 render。点击用卡片 ID 调 `custom/knowledge-show-card'。"
  (if (null entries)
      (list (propertize "知识库暂无条目" 'face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
    (mapcar
     (lambda (e)
       (let* ((id (nth 0 e))
              (category (nth 1 e))
              (title (nth 2 e))
              (display (concat category "/" title))
              (prefix "  ")
              (w (max 0 (- width (custom/dashboard--measure prefix)))))
         (concat prefix
                 (custom/dashboard--button
                  (custom/dashboard--truncate display w)
                  (lambda () (custom/knowledge-show-card 'human id))
                  (format "查看知识卡片 %s" display)))))
     entries)))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-bookmarks ()
  "书签卡片 fetch。"
  (require 'bookmark)
  (list :ok (bookmark-all-names)))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-bookmarks (names width)
  "书签卡片 render。"
  (if (null names)
      (list (propertize "暂无书签" 'face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
    (mapcar
     (lambda (b)
       (let* ((prefix "  ")
              (w (max 0 (- width (custom/dashboard--measure prefix)))))
         (concat prefix
                 (custom/dashboard--button
                  (custom/dashboard--truncate b w)
                  (lambda () (bookmark-jump b))
                  (format "跳转书签 %s" b)))))
     names)))

(defun custom/dashboard--format-shortcut-column (binding width)
  "格式化单列快捷键 BINDING 以适应 WIDTH 列宽。"
  (if (not binding)
      (custom/dashboard--fill width)
    (let* ((key (car binding))
           (desc (cdr binding))
           (key-gap "  ")
           (key-w (custom/dashboard--measure key))
           (gap-w (custom/dashboard--measure key-gap))
           (desc-w (max 0 (- width key-w gap-w)))
           (display-desc (custom/dashboard--truncate desc desc-w))
           (used (+ key-w gap-w (custom/dashboard--measure display-desc))))
      (concat (propertize key 'face 'font-lock-keyword-face)
              key-gap
              display-desc
              (custom/dashboard--fill (max 0 (- width used)))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--extract-top-level-bindings ()
  "提取 dashboard 需要展示的关键顶层快捷键。"
  (when (fboundp 'custom/help--extract-dashboard-bindings)
    (funcall #'custom/help--extract-dashboard-bindings)))

(defun custom/dashboard--render-shortcuts (_data width)
  "快捷键卡片 render（全宽）。_DATA 未使用（绑定来自 help 系统）。
双列布局，每列等宽，列间固定间隔。"
  (let* ((prefix "  ")
         (prefix-w (custom/dashboard--measure prefix))
         (available-width (max 1 (- width prefix-w)))
         (col-count 2)
         (col-gap custom:dashboard-shortcuts-column-gap)
         (col-width (/ (max 1 (- available-width col-gap)) col-count))
         (prefix-bindings (custom/dashboard--extract-top-level-bindings))
         (total (length prefix-bindings))
         (rows-count (ceiling total (float col-count)))
         (row-lines nil))
    (dotimes (i rows-count)
      (let* ((left-idx i)
             (right-idx (+ i rows-count))
             (left (nth left-idx prefix-bindings))
             (right (when (< right-idx total) (nth right-idx prefix-bindings)))
             (left-col (custom/dashboard--format-shortcut-column left col-width))
             (right-col (custom/dashboard--format-shortcut-column right col-width)))
        (push (concat prefix
                      left-col
                      (custom/dashboard--fill col-gap)
                      right-col)
              row-lines)))
    (nreverse row-lines)))

(defun custom/dashboard--fetch-shortcuts ()
  "快捷键卡片 fetch（数据延迟到 render 时取，fetch 只返回占位 :ok）。"
  (list :ok t))

;; 卡片注册表：title / icon / key 与 binding-spec 中的 :dashboard 声明一一对应，
;; 由 configctl audit-keys 校验一致性。accent face 给图标上色，零主题耦合。
(custom/dashboard-define-card 'todo
                              :title "待办" :icon "󰃭" :key "C-c o a" :accent 'warning
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-todo
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-todo)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'clock
                              :title "计时" :icon "󱎫" :key "C-c c i / o" :accent 'success
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-clock
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-clock)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'recents
                              :title "最近文件" :icon "󰋚" :key "C-x C-r"
                              :accent 'font-lock-keyword-face
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-recents
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-recents)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'projects
                              :title "项目" :icon "󰝰" :key "C-x p p" :accent 'font-lock-type-face
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-projects
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-projects)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'knowledge
                              :title "知识库" :icon "󰧑" :key "C-c o k I" :accent 'font-lock-string-face
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-knowledge
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-knowledge)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'bookmarks
                              :title "书签" :icon "󰉋" :key "C-x r b" :accent 'font-lock-constant-face
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-bookmarks
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-bookmarks)

(custom/dashboard-define-card 'shortcuts
                              :title "快捷键" :icon "󰌌" :key "F1 ?" :accent 'font-lock-keyword-face
                              :fetch #'custom/dashboard--fetch-shortcuts
                              :render #'custom/dashboard--render-shortcuts)
#+end_src

*** 生命周期与 Frame 状态机

#+attr_org: :width 70
| 触发                   | 动作                    | 约束                                                                                     |
|------------------------+-------------------------+------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| =custom/dashboard-mode= | 等宽字 + 关闭 hl-line   | 避免 variable-pitch 与当前行背景条切断左竖线                                             |
| 窗口尺寸变化           | 防抖重渲染              | 数据代数或宽度变化时才真正渲染                                                           |
| 主题切换               | dashboard buffer 重渲染 | 走 =register-buffer-refresh!=，face 继承自动适配                                         |
| frame hooks            | 单次调度打开 dashboard  | =after-make-frame= 与 =server-after-make-frame= 可能对同一 frame 连续触发，用 frame 参数去重 |

Dashboard 不对全局 =widget-button--check-and-call-button= 加 advice:该 advice 会影响所有 non-dashboard widget buffer,副作用过大。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref system/dashboard-lifecycle :tangle no

(defun custom/dashboard--ascii-banner-lines ()
  "返回 banner 行列表与状态行（不含居中 padding）。
LITERAL figlet art，box-drawing 字符 string-width≈80-85 列，
在 110 列以上窗口可舒适居中。"
  (let* ((banner '(
                   ""
                   "███████╗███╗   ███╗ █████╗  ██████╗███████╗"
                   "██╔════╝████╗ ████║██╔══██╗██╔════╝██╔════╝"
                   "█████╗  ██╔████╔██║███████║██║     ███████╗"
                   "██╔══╝  ██║╚██╔╝██║██╔══██║██║     ╚════██║"
                   "███████╗██║ ╚═╝ ██║██║  ██║╚██████╗███████║"
                   "╚══════╝╚═╝     ╚═╝╚═╝  ╚═╝ ╚═════╝╚══════╝"
                   ""))
         (status (custom/dashboard--status-line)))
    (if status
        (append banner (list status))
      banner)))

(defun custom/dashboard--status-line ()
  "返回 banner 下方的状态行字符串，或 nil。
汇总 Emacs 版本、启动耗时与当前主题模式；任何字段缺失该槽显示 ?。
全部 nil 守卫，batch / fresh daemon 下安全。

`(emacs-version)' 返回 \"GNU Emacs 31.0.90 ...\"，正则需匹配 GNU 前缀。
启动耗时用秒数自行格式化为紧凑形式（`(emacs-init-time)' 在 batch 下返回
原始秒数字符串如 \"0.002s\"，交互下返回 \"0.5 seconds\"，统一格式化避免漂移）。"
  (let* ((emacs-ver (and (fboundp 'emacs-version)
                         (string-match "GNU Emacs \\([0-9.]+\\)" (emacs-version))
                         (match-string 1 (emacs-version))))
         (init-seconds (and (fboundp 'emacs-init-time)
                            (condition-case nil (emacs-init-time) nil)))
         (init-time (cond
                     ((null init-seconds) "?")
                     ;; 数字开头（batch 返回 "0.002s" / 交互返回 "0.5 seconds"）
                     ((string-match "\\`\\([0-9.]+\\)" init-seconds)
                      (let ((secs (string-to-number (match-string 1 init-seconds))))
                        (if (>= secs 1)
                            (format "%.1fs" secs)
                          (format "%.0fms" (* secs 1000)))))
                     (t init-seconds)))
         (mode (when (boundp 'custom/color-scheme-current-mode)
                 (symbol-name custom/color-scheme-current-mode))))
    (format "Emacs %s · 启动 %s · %s"
            (or emacs-ver "?")
            init-time
            (or mode "?"))))

(defun custom/dashboard--banner-art-width (line)
  "返回固定 banner LINE 实际占用的终端单元数。
banner 只含空格及 block/box-drawing 字符，在当前等宽 GUI 与 Foot TTY 中均为
一字符一单元。不能用 `string-width'：真实 TTY 的 Emacs CJK 宽度表会把这些
字符计为两列，即使终端仍只渲染一个单元，从而让居中 padding 少算近一半。"
  (length line))

(defun custom/dashboard--banner-min-width ()
  "banner ASCII art 居中显示所需的最小窗口列宽。
固定 art 字符在 GUI 与 Foot TTY 中均按一字符一终端单元度量，与
`custom/dashboard--insert-banner' 一致。
另留 10 列左右边距，避免 art 紧贴窗口边缘。"
  (let* ((art-max-line "███████╗███╗   ███╗ █████╗  ██████╗███████╗")
         (art-w (custom/dashboard--banner-art-width art-max-line)))
    (+ art-w 10)))

(defun custom/dashboard--insert-banner (width)
  "在当前 buffer 插入居中的 banner（art + 状态行）。
art 各行左边缘对齐到最长行的居中 padding（figlet art 要求行间左对齐），
状态行单独按自身宽度居中到窗口中央。窄窗口（低于
`custom:dashboard-banner-min-width'）降级为纯文本单行。

+ art 使用固定的一单元字符集，GUI 与 Foot TTY 均用 =length= 算 pad。真实 TTY
  中 Emacs 可能因 CJK 宽度表把这些字符报告为 2 列，而终端实际仍渲染 1 单元；
  用 =string-width= 会让 banner 明显偏左。状态行是混合文本(ASCII + CJK + ·),
  仍使用 =string-width=。

空行（art 首尾的 \"\"）插入纯换行，不插入前导 padding 空格，避免产生
满是空格的视觉空行。"
  (let ((banner-face 'font-lock-keyword-face)
        (status-face 'custom/dashboard-dim))
    (if (< width (custom/dashboard--banner-min-width))
        (let ((pad (max 0 (/ (- width (length "LITERAL")) 2))))
          (insert "\n"
                  (custom/dashboard--fill pad)
                  (propertize "LITERAL" 'face banner-face)
                  "\n\n"))
      (let* ((lines (custom/dashboard--ascii-banner-lines))
             (status (custom/dashboard--status-line))
             (art-lines (cl-remove-if
                         (lambda (l) (or (string-empty-p l) (equal l status)))
                         lines))
             ;; 固定 art 字符在 GUI/Foot TTY 中都是一字符一单元。
             (art-max-w (apply #'max
                               (mapcar #'custom/dashboard--banner-art-width
                                       art-lines)))
             (art-pad (max 0 (/ (- width art-max-w) 2))))
        (insert "\n")
        (dolist (line lines)
          (cond
           ((string-empty-p line) (insert "\n"))
           ((equal line status)
            ;; 状态行用 string-width 居中（混合文本，两环境一致）
            (let ((status-pad (max 0 (/ (- width (custom/dashboard--measure line)) 2))))
              (insert (custom/dashboard--fill status-pad)
                      (propertize line 'face status-face)
                      "\n")))
           (t
            ;; art 行统一 pad
            (insert (custom/dashboard--fill art-pad)
                    (propertize line 'face banner-face)
                    "\n"))))
        (insert "\n")))))

(defun custom/dashboard--buffer-name-for-frame (frame)
  "返回 FRAME 专属的 dashboard buffer 名称。"
  (let ((id (frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-id)))
    (unless (integerp id)
      (setq id (cl-incf custom/dashboard--frame-sequence))
      (set-frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-id id))
    (format "%s<%d>" custom:dashboard-buffer-name id)))

(defun custom/dashboard--frame-buffer (&optional frame)
  "返回 FRAME 专属的 dashboard buffer，不存在时创建。"
  (let* ((target-frame (or frame (selected-frame)))
         (existing (frame-parameter target-frame 'custom-dashboard-buffer)))
    (if (and (buffer-live-p existing)
             (with-current-buffer existing
               (eq custom/dashboard--owner-frame target-frame)))
        existing
      (set-frame-parameter target-frame 'custom-dashboard-id nil)
      (let ((buffer (get-buffer-create
                     (custom/dashboard--buffer-name-for-frame target-frame))))
        (with-current-buffer buffer
          (unless (derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode)
            (custom/dashboard-mode))
          (setq-local custom/dashboard--owner-frame target-frame))
        (set-frame-parameter target-frame 'custom-dashboard-buffer buffer)
        buffer))))

(defun custom/dashboard--release-frame-buffer (frame)
  "清理 FRAME 专属的 dashboard buffer。"
  (let ((buffer (frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-buffer)))
    (when (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
               (with-current-buffer buffer
                 (eq custom/dashboard--owner-frame frame)))
      (kill-buffer buffer))))

(defun custom/dashboard-render (&optional frame)
  "自有渲染入口：erase → banner → 布局引擎 → insert。
不经过第三方 dashboard 包任何函数；单卡片错误由布局引擎隔离。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((target-frame
          (or (and frame (frame-live-p frame) frame)
              (and (derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode)
                   (frame-live-p custom/dashboard--owner-frame)
                   custom/dashboard--owner-frame)
              (selected-frame)))
         (buf (custom/dashboard--frame-buffer target-frame)))
    (prog1 buf
      (with-current-buffer buf
        (unless (derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode)
          (custom/dashboard-mode)
          (setq-local custom/dashboard--owner-frame target-frame))
        (let* ((window (or (get-buffer-window buf target-frame)
                           (frame-selected-window target-frame)))
               (width (if window (window-body-width window) (frame-width target-frame)))
               (margin-str (custom/dashboard--fill custom:dashboard-dual-margin)))
          (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
            (erase-buffer)
            (custom/dashboard--insert-banner width)
            (dolist (line (custom/dashboard--build-lines width))
              (insert margin-str line "\n"))
            (goto-char (point-min))
            (setq-local custom/dashboard--rendered-width width
                        custom/dashboard--rendered-generation
                        custom/dashboard--generation)))))))

(define-derived-mode custom/dashboard-mode special-mode "Dashboard"
  "自建 dashboard mode（derived from `special-mode'）。
不依赖第三方 dashboard 包：渲染完全由 `custom/dashboard-render' 驱动。"
  (setq-local truncate-lines t)
  (custom/dashboard--apply-fixed-pitch)
  (setq-local cursor-type nil
              line-spacing 0.15
              show-trailing-whitespace nil)
  (when (fboundp 'hl-line-mode) (hl-line-mode -1))
  (when (boundp 'display-line-numbers-mode) (display-line-numbers-mode -1))
  (buffer-disable-undo)
  (let ((map (make-sparse-keymap)))
    (define-key map "g" #'custom/dashboard-refresh)
    (define-key map "n" #'next-line)
    (define-key map "p" #'previous-line)
    (define-key map "q" #'quit-window)
    (use-local-map map)))

(defun custom/dashboard-refresh ()
  "失效缓存并刷新当前 frame 的 Dashboard。"
  (interactive)
  (custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache)
  (custom/dashboard-render custom/dashboard--owner-frame))

(defconst custom:dashboard-placeholder-buffer-names
  '(" *server*" "*scratch*" "*Messages*")
  "允许 dashboard 自动接管的 Emacs 初始占位 buffer 名称。")

(defun custom/dashboard--placeholder-buffer-p (buffer)
  "判断 BUFFER 是否仍是 client frame 的占位缓冲区。"
  (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
    (with-current-buffer buffer
      (and (not (minibufferp buffer))
           (not buffer-file-name)
           (or (member (buffer-name buffer)
                       custom:dashboard-placeholder-buffer-names)
               (and (eq major-mode 'fundamental-mode)
                    (string-prefix-p " " (buffer-name buffer))))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--server-edit-buffer-p (buffer)
  "判断 BUFFER 是否正在服务 emacsclient / with-editor 编辑请求。"
  (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
    (with-current-buffer buffer
      (or (bound-and-true-p server-buffer-clients)
          (bound-and-true-p with-editor-mode)
          (derived-mode-p 'git-commit-mode)))))

(defun custom/dashboard--single-window-frame-p (frame)
  "判断 FRAME 是否仍只有一个普通窗口。
Dashboard 只接管这种新 client frame 的空白启动状态；若 Magit、提交编辑器
或其他命令已经分裂窗口，则保留现有窗口配置。"
  (and (frame-live-p frame)
       (= (length (window-list frame 'no-minibuf)) 1)))

(defun custom/dashboard--refresh-if-visible ()
  "立即刷新所有可见 dashboard buffer。"
  (custom/dashboard--refresh-visible-now))

(defvar custom/dashboard--refresh-timer nil
  "dashboard 可见性刷新的 pending idle timer。")

(defun custom/dashboard--refresh-visible-now ()
  "立即检查所有可见 dashboard 窗口，数据代数或宽度变化者重新渲染。"
  (when (timerp custom/dashboard--refresh-timer)
    (cancel-timer custom/dashboard--refresh-timer))
  (setq custom/dashboard--refresh-timer nil)
  (dolist (frame (frame-list))
    (when (frame-live-p frame)
      (dolist (window (window-list frame 'no-minibuf))
        (let ((buffer (window-buffer window)))
          (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
            (with-current-buffer buffer
              (let ((width (window-body-width window)))
                (when (and (derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode)
                           (or (/= custom/dashboard--rendered-generation
                                   custom/dashboard--generation)
                               (not (equal custom/dashboard--rendered-width width))))
                  (with-selected-window window
                    (custom/dashboard-render frame)))))))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--maybe-refresh-visible (frame)
  "可见 dashboard 需要更新时延迟重渲染（合并连续尺寸变化）。"
  (when (seq-some
         (lambda (window)
           (with-current-buffer (window-buffer window)
             (derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode)))
         (window-list frame 'no-minibuf))
    (when (timerp custom/dashboard--refresh-timer)
      (cancel-timer custom/dashboard--refresh-timer))
    (setq custom/dashboard--refresh-timer
          (run-with-idle-timer custom:dashboard-refresh-debounce
                               nil
                               #'custom/dashboard--refresh-visible-now))))

(defun custom/dashboard--display-in-frame (frame)
  "在 FRAME 中显示并刷新 dashboard。"
  (when (and (frame-live-p frame)
             (custom/dashboard--single-window-frame-p frame))
    (with-selected-frame frame
      (let ((buf (custom/dashboard--frame-buffer frame)))
        (when-let* ((window (frame-selected-window frame)))
          (set-window-buffer window buf)
          (with-selected-window window
            (custom/dashboard-render frame)))))))

(defconst custom:dashboard-open-delay 0
  "新建 client frame 后等待事件循环下一拍再判定 dashboard。")

(defconst custom:dashboard-open-retries 1
  "若首次判定过早，dashboard 允许追加重试的次数。")

(defun custom/dashboard--frame-startup-state (frame)
  "返回 FRAME 当前的启动缓冲区状态。"
  (cond
   ((not (custom/dashboard--single-window-frame-p frame)) 'busy)
   (t
    (if-let* ((window (and (frame-live-p frame)
                           (frame-selected-window frame)))
              (buffer (window-buffer window)))
        (with-current-buffer buffer
          (cond
           ((derived-mode-p 'custom/dashboard-mode) 'dashboard)
           ((custom/dashboard--server-edit-buffer-p buffer) 'server-edit)
           ((buffer-file-name) 'file)
           ((custom/dashboard--placeholder-buffer-p buffer) 'placeholder)
           (t 'busy)))
      'pending))))

(defun custom/dashboard--run-open-check (frame retries-left)
  "根据 FRAME 当前状态决定是否显示或重试 dashboard。"
  (when (frame-live-p frame)
    (set-frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-open-scheduled nil)
    (pcase (custom/dashboard--frame-startup-state frame)
      ('placeholder
       (custom/dashboard--display-in-frame frame))
      ('dashboard
       (when-let* ((window (frame-selected-window frame)))
         (with-selected-window window
           (custom/dashboard-render))))
      ('file nil)
      ('server-edit nil)
      ('busy nil)
      (_
       (when (> retries-left 0)
         (custom/dashboard--schedule-open-for-frame
          frame (1- retries-left)))))))

(defun custom/dashboard--schedule-open-for-frame (frame retries-left)
  "为 FRAME 安排一次 dashboard 打开检查。RETRIES-LEFT 为剩余重试次数。"
  (when (and (frame-live-p frame)
             (not (frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-open-scheduled)))
    (set-frame-parameter frame 'custom-dashboard-open-scheduled t)
    (run-at-time
     custom:dashboard-open-delay nil
     (lambda (idle-frame idle-retries-left)
       (when (frame-live-p idle-frame)
         (custom/dashboard--run-open-check idle-frame idle-retries-left)))
     frame retries-left)))

(defun custom/dashboard-open-for-client-frame (&optional frame)
  "在 daemon 创建的 client FRAME 中打开 dashboard。
仅当该 frame 仍停留在占位缓冲区时接管，避免覆盖 `emacsclient FILE`。"
  (custom/dashboard--schedule-open-for-frame
   (or frame (selected-frame))
   custom:dashboard-open-retries))

;; Org 文件保存后失效缓存：dashboard 的 todo/knowledge 数据源可能已变。
(add-hook 'after-save-hook
          (lambda ()
            (when (and buffer-file-name
                       (string-prefix-p (expand-file-name custom:org-directory)
                                        (expand-file-name buffer-file-name)))
              (custom/dashboard-invalidate-cache)
              (custom/dashboard--refresh-if-visible))))

(add-hook 'window-size-change-functions #'custom/dashboard--maybe-refresh-visible)

(when (fboundp 'custom/register-buffer-refresh!)
  (custom/register-buffer-refresh!
   'custom/dashboard-mode #'custom/dashboard-render))

(custom/add-server-ready-hook #'custom/dashboard-open-for-client-frame)
(add-hook 'delete-frame-functions #'custom/dashboard--release-frame-buffer)

#+end_src

*** 可选增强

标题计数仍可在以后按需加入；它需要扩展 fetch 协议返回未截断总数，与本轮 client 延迟优化无关。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
<<system/dashboard-core>>
<<system/dashboard-measure>>
<<system/dashboard-registry>>
<<system/dashboard-engine>>
<<system/dashboard-cards>>
<<system/dashboard-lifecycle>>
#+end_src

** 实验性与待确认
:PROPERTIES:
:CUSTOM_ID: compatibility
:END:

*** 实验性

本节集中放置生效中的版本兜底 shim(Ghostel/with-editor、which-key 中文宽度、Arei/Yasnippet .elc 兼容、vertico-buffer 形态降级)。它们都是「待上游修复」的 workaround,而非待完成的本地功能——代码已生效,移除条件在上游。

以下 noweb 片段(均为 =:tangle no= )定义 Emacs 31 兼容性 shim,由使用方在文档更靠前的位置前向引用(=<<emacs31/...>>= / =<<compat/...>>= )展开到 main.el。tangle 后代码插入到使用点,加载顺序不变;定义集中在此处是为了可发现性与未来上游修复后统一清理。

#+attr_org: :width 70
| Shim                       | 根因                                 | 受影响功能                     | 移除条件                                            |
|----------------------------+--------------------------------------+--------------------------------+-----------------------------------------------------|
| =compat/ghostel-with-editor= | with-editor 3.5.2 无 pre-spawn 接口  | ghostel 内 git commit          | Ghostel/with-editor 提供公开 pre-spawn 环境构造接口 |
| =compat/which-key-width=     | which-key 自动列数中文宽度不等       | which-key 中文描述             | which-key 原生布局足够(=fboundp= 守卫容忍 API 变化)   |
| =emacs31/source-load-compat= | Guix .elc 与 Emacs 31 宏展开不兼容   | Arei / Yasnippet               | 所有相关包改由 Emacs 31 重编译                        |
| =emacs31/arei-compat=        | arei 0.9.7 .elc 与 Emacs 31 宏不兼容 | Scheme 染色([[#language-modes][各语言 major mode]]) | 升级 arei 或 Guix 用 Emacs 31 重编译                |
| =emacs31/yasnippet-compat=   | yasnippet .elc 缺少 global mode 状态 | 新 client major-mode 初始化    | 升级 yasnippet 或 Guix 用 Emacs 31 重编译             |
| vertico-buffer 形态降级    | bug#80812,buffer-local-set-state 宏  | vertico-buffer 形态            | vertico 上游修复 / Guix 重编译(见下方)              |

**** Ghostel pre-spawn 的 with-editor 适配

with-editor 3.5.2 的公开导出函数只支持已经存在的 shell/term/vterm 进程；Ghostel 的 pre-spawn hook 运行在进程创建前。当前必须复用宏内部 setup，删除条件是 Ghostel 或 with-editor 提供公开的 pre-spawn 环境构造接口。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref compat/ghostel-with-editor :tangle no
(defun custom/ghostel-export-git-editor ()
  "在 Ghostel 创建进程前把 GIT_EDITOR 注入动态 `process-environment'。"
  (when (fboundp 'with-editor--setup)
    (let ((with-editor--envvar "GIT_EDITOR"))
      (with-editor--setup))))
#+end_src

**** Which-key 中文宽度与网格布局补丁

which-key 3.6.1 已内置 =string-width= 计算，但自动列数在底部 side-window 下仍可能导致中文描述列宽不等。补丁用 =advice-add :override= 替换 =which-key--pad-column=，在固定网格布局下按等宽分栏。加 =fboundp= 守卫：which-key 已并入 Emacs 30+ 内置，未来若内部函数改名/移除，静默跳过。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref compat/which-key-width :tangle no
(defun custom/which-key--pad-column-fixed-width (col-keys available-width)
  "为 COL-KEYS 生成更适合中文的 which-key 列布局。
AVAILABLE-WIDTH 为当前 which-key 可用宽度。固定网格布局下按等宽分栏,
非网格布局下按 which-key 原生逻辑但用 `string-width' 计算。"
  (let* ((grid-layout-p (custom/which-key--fixed-grid-layout-p))
         (col-key-width (+ which-key-add-column-padding
                           (which-key--max-len col-keys 0)))
         (col-sep-width (which-key--max-len col-keys 1))
         (target-col-width (custom/which-key--target-grid-column-width available-width))
         (desc-space (max 1 (- target-col-width col-key-width col-sep-width)))
         (col-desc-width (if grid-layout-p
                             desc-space
                           (max which-key-min-column-description-width desc-space)))
         (col-width (+ col-key-width col-sep-width col-desc-width))
         (col-format (concat "%" (int-to-string col-key-width) "s%s%s")))
    (cons col-width
          (mapcar
           (pcase-lambda (`(,key ,sep ,desc ,_doc))
             (let* ((display-desc (truncate-string-to-width
                                   desc col-desc-width 0 nil which-key-ellipsis))
                    (padding (max 0 (- col-desc-width (string-width display-desc)))))
               (concat
                (format col-format key sep display-desc)
                (make-string padding ?\s))))
           col-keys))))

(defun custom/which-key-install-width-fix ()
  "安装 Which-key 中文宽度与网格布局补丁。
加 fboundp 守卫:which-key 已并入 Emacs 30+ 内置,未来若内部函数改名/移除,
静默跳过而非崩溃。"
  (when (fboundp 'which-key--pad-column)
    (advice-add 'which-key--pad-column :override
                #'custom/which-key--pad-column-fixed-width)))
#+end_src

**** arei .elc 与 buffer-local-set-state 不兼容(bug#80812)

arei 0.9.7 的 .elc(Guix 用旧版 Emacs byte-compile)与 Emacs 31 的 =buffer-local-set-state= 宏不兼容:arei-mode 第 356 行调用该宏,但旧 .elc 把它当函数调用,参数 =(sesman-system 'Arei)= 被整体传给 =buffer-local-set-state--get= → =wrong-type-argument (symbolp 'Arei)= 。此错误在 =scheme-mode-hook= 里抛出,会中断整个 scheme-mode 初始化,导致 font-lock 失效 → Scheme 代码不染色。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref emacs31/source-load-compat :tangle no
(defun custom/load-library-from-source (library)
  "从 LIBRARY 对应的 .el 源加载，绕过不兼容的 Guix .elc。"
  (condition-case err
      (when-let* ((found (locate-library library))
                  (source (concat (file-name-sans-extension found) ".el"))
                  ((file-exists-p source)))
        (load source nil t t)
        t)
    (error
     (message "[literal] %s 从源加载失败: %S" library err)
     nil)))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref emacs31/arei-compat :tangle no
(defun custom--load-arei-from-source ()
  "从 .el 源强制加载 arei,绕过与 Emacs 31 不兼容的 .elc。
GNU store 只读无法删 .elc,且 .el/.elc 时间戳相同(均为 epoch 1),
`load-prefer-newer' 无效,故需显式从 .el 加载。返回 t 若成功。"
  (custom/load-library-from-source "arei"))

(defun custom--maybe-enable-arei-mode ()
  "在真实 Scheme 编辑 buffer 中启用 `arei-mode'(跳过 org src 临时 buffer)。

对 `arei-mode' 调用包裹 condition-case:minor-mode 的故障不应中断
`scheme-mode-hook' 的后续步骤(尤其 font-lock 初始化)。"
  (unless (string-prefix-p " *org-src-fontification:" (buffer-name))
    (condition-case err
        (arei-mode 1)
      (error
       (message "[literal] arei-mode 启用失败,Scheme 染色不受影响: %S" err)))))
#+end_src

**** yasnippet global mode 状态变量缺失(bug#80812,同根因)

Yasnippet 0.14.3 的 Guix =.elc= 在 Emacs 31 中加载后，=yas-global-mode= 虽为 non-nil，但 =yas-minor-mode--set-explicitly= / =yas-minor-mode--suppress-set-explicitly= 没有定义。新 client 切换 major mode 时，globalized minor mode hook 因 =void-variable= 中断。与 Arei 相同，运行时从 =.el= 源重新展开宏即可恢复完整定义。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :noweb-ref emacs31/yasnippet-compat :tangle no
(defun custom--load-yasnippet-from-source ()
  "从 .el 源加载 Yasnippet，补齐 Emacs 31 global mode 状态变量。"
  (custom/load-library-from-source "yasnippet"))
#+end_src

**** vertico-buffer 形态降级(bug#80812,同根因)

vertico-buffer 形态在 Emacs 31 master + Guix Emacs 30 编译的 vertico elc 下报 =vertico-buffer--setup: Wrong type argument: symbolp= 。根因与 arei 相同:Emacs 31 重构了 =setq-local= (基于新 =set-local= 函数,bug#80812),而 vertico-buffer 内部用的 =buffer-local-set-state= 宏在 30 编译的 elc 里展开不兼容。上游追踪:[[https://github.com/minad/vertico/issues/670][minad/vertico#670]]。修复前所有原本想用 =buffer= 的形态临时降级为 =indexed= ;vertico 上游修复或 Guix 用 Emacs 31 重编译后,在 [[#completion][Completion 框架]] 数据块的 =vertico-multiform-categories= 里把对应条目从 =indexed= 恢复为 =buffer= (如 =(consult-imenu indexed)= → =(consult-imenu buffer)= )。

**** byte-compile-root-dir(elfeed-3.4.2 tangle 崩溃)

=tangle= 阶段 =elfeed-link.el= 注册的 =elfeed= org link 被 =org-babel-tangle--unbracketed-link= 经 =org-store-link= 试探,触发 autoload → =require 'elfeed-show= → =require 'elfeed= 。elfeed.el 内 =(cl-eval-when (load eval) (unless byte-compile-root-dir ...))= 这段 byte-code 假设变量已 bound,但 elfeed-3.4.2 不可绕开,Emacs 31 下报 void-variable。

+ *shim 位置* :: =init.el= (非 emacs.org tangle 产物;bootstrap 阶段 =org-babel-tangle-file= 之前 =(defvar byte-compile-root-dir nil)= )
+ *根因* :: Emacs 31 仍 defvar =byte-compile-root-dir= (见 =bytecomp.el:1232= ),但 bytecomp.el 是 lazy-load,默认不在初始环境
+ *根治* :: 升级到 elfeed-4.0.1

** 主配置收尾

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(when (custom/daemon-runtime-p)
  (custom/daemon-warmup))

(message "[custom-config] 启动完成")

(provide 'main)
;;; main.el ends here
#+end_src
